# 2026-10-22 — Audit IV: full production audit (evidence, priorities, fixes)

The whole repo read again on the night of 2026-10-22 / 2026-10-23, at `223de3f58` (tree clean; index.html sha256 `8fafd7c2`,
the build that is live). Eight parallel sweeps over a clean clone, each briefed with the gotchas, the open backlog (Audit II
a-items, Audit III c-items) and the rule "report only what you traced". Every number claim was re-run with `node` against the
real `data/*.json` of that commit; the responsive and failure-state claims come from ~200 headless-Chromium runs of
`tools/harness` (six widths, every route, every window, RPC/DexScreener/PulseScan down or slow); the live site was checked from
the owner's Mac (built-in browser): ptgc-ufo.com answered 200 with the stale `tw.css` described in e19, `/apple-touch-icon.png`
and `/ptgc` 404, the Worker answered `Cache-Control: no-store`, the pipeline's last run was a Worker dispatch at 00:15 UTC
(so the hourly beat is alive tonight), `pipeline-status.json` said `carried: 0`, the dashboard mounted with 7 tiles and no
console errors, and the staking contract answered `totalStaked() = 187,479,993,044` / `totalLocked() = 124,364,219,325`.
**No code was changed.** The merged, prioritised list is first (ids `e1`–`e47`, the ids the roadmap artifact should carry under
"AUDIT IV"); the eight raw sweeps follow, one section each, with the full evidence, the "checked and found OK" lists and the
ideas. Line numbers are the clone's = the Mac's tree at `223de3f58` unless another file is named.

| Sweep | Scope |
|---|---|
| S | Security — secrets, XSS sinks, URL handling, the Worker, workflows, headers, dependencies |
| N | Calculation accuracy in index.html — every model, tested with real data |
| M | Calculation accuracy on portfolio / calculators / ledger / charts + the Worker's maths |
| D | Data pipeline, workflows, observability, backup, scaling |
| R | Client RPC / API usage, call budgets, timeouts, failure behaviour (harness-measured) |
| P | Performance, dependencies, SEO / metadata, browser compatibility (Safari / iOS) |
| U | Accessibility, UI / UX consistency, error / empty states, testing coverage |
| V | Responsive scan — 1920 / 1380 / 1024 / 768 / 390 / 320, every page and window |

## What Audit IV says in one paragraph

The site is in good health where the last six weeks went: the dashboard's price / burn / Value Generated / holders / LP /
volume models all reproduce from the real files (sweep N's "checked OK" list is long), the pipeline fails safe step by step, the
honest-failure rules hold on the dashboards, every window is a proper dialog, there is no horizontal scroll at 390 anywhere,
no secret in the tree, no XSS sink that reaches external data, and every CDN script is SRI-pinned. The trouble is at four
edges. (1) **Two of the three newest money features have an arithmetic slip each**: the Top 100 "cushion" subtracts the wrong
neighbour and is always too generous (e1), the holder rank ranks a wallet against its own stale entry after a sell (e9), and
the rank window compares "today" against a differently-built population so most holders see a built-in ▼ step (e10). (2)
**The portfolio and the sibling pages never received the hardening index.html got**: the UFO reflections scan has the
`toBlock:'latest'` bug index fixed a month ago (e2), a failed UFO balance is a silent 0 (e8), Get Code silently produces nothing
for an emoji label (e7), three pages can hang on a stalled fetch with no timeout and no Retry (e14), and the RPC pool copies
pre-date a41 (e16). (3) **The affiliates money path has three display/Worker inconsistencies** (e4, e5) and the Worker's
`/read` / `/write` take an unvalidated path that walks out of the ToolBox repo (e6). (4) **Nothing tells the owner when
things stop**: no watchdog for a pipeline that is never started, carried-forward steps show ✅, the compile check passes 0
bytes of two pages, the committed Tailwind CSS is stale on the live site and no CI runs anything (e19–e23). Everything in the
P0 and P1 tiers is small-to-medium and low-risk; the whole P0 tier is about two days of work.

## SEVERE — read these first

**Status 2026-10-23: e1 + e2 LIVE (`d6a7b13c6`, live-verified); e3, e4, e7, e8, e19 LIVE (`b071ba060`); e9, e10, e11 LIVE (`db1bd3aca`; e10's real cause = PulseScan page repeats, see HANDOVER); e12, e17, e18, e20–e25 BUILT (`tools/audit4-batch3.py` + `tools/audit4-ci.py`, not pushed); P0 left: e5, e6 (Worker v8, parked by Shaka); e13 waits on his "people only" call.**

- **e1** The Top 100 window's "Your cushion" overstates how much a top-100 holder can sell by `list[99]−list[100]` (for #100
  today: says 20.52M, truth 7.15M). One-token fix. Live since 2026-10-22 16:20 UTC.
- **e2** Portfolio UFO "Lifetime earned": the incremental scan reads to `latest` but caches `to:head`; a transfer in the 1–2
  block gap is counted twice on the next visit and stays inflated until site data is cleared.
- **e6** Worker `/read?path=` and `/write?path=` accept `../../<other-repo>/contents/<file>` — with the ToolBox secret and a
  cross-repo token that writes the dashboard's `index.html` on `main`, which `deploy.yml` publishes. Precondition is real but
  not public; fix is a one-line whitelist.
- **e3** The DAO Treasury panel prints "$0" / an understated Total DAO Value when any one of ~15 reads fails — no label.
- **e4 / e5** A referrer below the monthly minimum is shown per-buy commissions and "Unpaid" in the same window that says
  nothing is pending; the Worker compares `thresholdType` case-sensitively and turns a 0 % month into 2 %.

## The merged list (e1–e47)

Format: **id · priority · status · effort / risk · recommendation — title.** Then area · problem · why · fix. Status words are
the brief's: CONFIRMED ISSUE (traced and/or reproduced), LIKELY ISSUE (traced, the triggering condition not reproduced),
POTENTIAL RISK, OPTIONAL IMPROVEMENT. Effort S = an hour or two, M = a day or two, L = a week+. "(N-1)" = the raw finding(s)
behind the item, below. P0 = wrong money / wrong number shown to users, data loss, security · P1 = reliability, mobile,
performance, usability or architecture that users or the owner will hit · P2 = meaningful, not urgent · P3 = polish / future.

### P0 — CRITICAL (fix this week, in this order)

- **e1 · P0 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Top 100 "Your cushion" subtracts the wrong neighbour; overstated for every
  wallet inside the top 100.** `tools/top100/t1.jsx:161` (index.html:12565) `const cush=Math.max(0,k-(list[100]||0));` —
  `list` already has the checked wallet removed (`t1Without`), so `list[99]` IS the overall #101 and `list[100]` is #102. The
  tile says "above #101 — sell more than this and you drop out". Worked (holder-ranks.json PTGC): the #100 wallet 412,485,544 →
  prints 20,523,982, truth 7,145,790; #50 → 316.2M vs 302.8M. Both views (holders and stakers). *Why*: it is the one number
  that tells a holder how much they can sell and stay in; it is always too generous. *Fix* (financial-formula change, old
  `k−list[100]` → new `k−list[99]`): one token in t1.jsx, re-run `python3 tools/top100.py`, harness `t1-check.js` with a fixture
  where `k === list[99]` → cushion 0. (N-1)
- **e2 · P0 · CONFIRMED (code) / LIKELY (trigger) · S / Low · FIX NOW — UFO "Lifetime earned" double-counts a transfer that
  lands between `head` and `latest`.** portfolio.html:1413 `toParam=(b>=headBlock)?'latest':…` sends the last slice to
  `latest`; 1508 caches `{to:head}` with `head` read before the scan (1478); the next visit scans `c.to+1 → head'` and re-reads
  the same blocks. index.html's copy got `exactTo` for this (gotcha 4); `grep -c exactTo portfolio.html` → 0. A send in the
  gap inflates reflections by the whole transfer, permanently (the `reflWei<0n` guard only catches a double-counted receive).
  *Fix*: port `exactTo` (index.html:2115–2124) and read `balanceOf` at block `head` inside `computeUfoReflectionsWei` so
  balance and sums are from one block. No formula change. (M-1)
- **e3 · P0 · LIKELY · S / Low · FIX NOW — DAO Treasury: any failed read after step 1 becomes 0 and the totals print as fact.**
  index.html:4676–4815 `fetchDAOData`: `fetchPLS()`→0 zeroes every PLS leg (4797); `BigInt(mgmtBalanceResult.result||'0x0')`
  (4729) turns an RPC miss into "0 PLS"; `priceMap[…]||0` (4762) and `if(t&&t.usdValue>0)` (4774) drop a token whose price
  failed; `ptgcPrice=ptgcPriceRes||0` (4784). Render prints `fmtUSD(daoData.totalValue||0)` with no partial flag, and
  `quickRefresh` never re-runs it. Worked with today's files: PTGC leg $35,906, HEX $16,462, mgmt PLS $17,208 — each can vanish
  from "Total DAO Value" silently. *Fix*: `if(!(plsPrice>0))return null`; treat `result==null` like the guarded contract read
  (4682); carry `partial:true` and print "—" / "≥ $X · n reads failed" (the a18 overlay). (N-3, R-6)
- **e4 · P0 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Referrer window: per-buy commission, "Comm:" header and Status pill ignore the
  month entry's threshold verdict.** index.html:16608 pushes `{...b,month,rate}` without `entry.meetsThreshold`; 16851
  `belowCount` is always 0; 16887 and 16918 print `ptgcAmount×rate/100` for a month that owes 0; 16920 never says "Below".
  The pending tile 40 lines above (16619–16624) gets it right. Worked: threshold $250, buys worth $120 → tile 0, table "Comm.
  2.00M · Unpaid". *Fix*: carry `meetsThreshold` on the row; zero the two sums when `false`. Display only. (N-4)
- **e5 · P0 · POTENTIAL RISK · S / Low · FIX NOW — Worker money logic: `thresholdType` compared case-sensitively, `||2`
  turns a 0 % month into 2 %, the entry's frozen rate can differ from the rate STEP 4 used, and the API month cut is the API
  server's clock.** `handover/ptgcapi-worker-v7.js:304/315` (`'USD'` falls into the PTGC branch → "250 PTGC" → everyone
  meets it), 256/302 (`settings.commissionRate || 2`), 321 (recomputed each sync with the CURRENT month rate while
  `entry.commissionRate` is written once), 235 (`tx.timestamp.slice(0,7)` on a zone-less string). The page lower-cases
  (`normSettings` 16254) because 'USD' has been seen. *Fix*: `String(x||'usd').toLowerCase()`; `Number.isFinite(rate)?rate:2`
  in four places; write `entry.commissionRate = rate` in STEP 4; confirm the API timestamp zone. Redeploy as v8 (`handover/`
  is the only source copy). (N-5, M-12, N-13)
- **e6 · P0 · POTENTIAL RISK (mechanics CONFIRMED) · S / Low · FIX NOW — Worker `/read` / `/write` path traversal + an
  unauthenticated, uncached public read that can burn the owner's PAT budget.** (a) `v7:67/425/507` interpolate `path`
  unencoded; `new URL('…/ToolBox/contents/../../PTGC-UFO-Dashboard/contents/index.html')` normalises to the dashboard repo,
  `branch:'main'` (499), `deploy.yml` publishes `main`. Needs the ToolBox secret (sent from a browser per
  `sessions/2026-09-29.md:24–26`) + a token that spans repos (the v7 `DISPATCH_TOKEN || GITHUB_TOKEN` fallback presupposes
  one). (b) `/public/commissions` = two authenticated GitHub calls per GET, `no-store`, no Workers cache, no rate rule; the
  page adds `?t=Date.now()` (16220). 2,500 GETs/h exhaust the user-wide 5,000/h PAT limit → affiliates page 503, cron sync
  fails, ToolBox fails, the hourly `workflow_dispatch` gets 403 and the pipeline drops to GitHub's ~4 runs/day. *Fix*: (a)
  `^data/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+\.json$` whitelist + `encodeURIComponent`, make `DISPATCH_TOKEN` mandatory, scope `GITHUB_TOKEN` to
  ToolBox Contents; (b) `caches.default` with a 60 s TTL for the public body + one Cloudflare rate rule + a read-only token for
  the public path; later KV. (S-1, S-2)
- **e7 · P0 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Portfolio GET CODE produces an EMPTY code when any wallet label has an emoji /
  en-dash / CJK; LOAD CODE then says "Invalid portfolio code" and REPLACES the list.** portfolio.html:1538–1543
  `'GRAY-'+btoa(JSON.stringify(data))` in a `catch{return null}`; `handleGetCode` opens the window with `code||''`; Copy copies
  "". Harness: name "Cold 🚀" → `{"code":""}`. The owner's own buttons are "📋 GET CODE". Third occurrence of the `btoa`
  Latin-1 trap (Worker v4/v6). *Fix*: UTF-8-safe base64 both ways (`TextEncoder` → `btoa`; decode tries UTF-8 then plain
  `atob` so old codes still load); show an error instead of an empty box; M-18's "replace N wallets?" ask on Load. (U-1, M-7,
  M-18)
- **e8 · P0 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — A failed UFO `balanceOf` is a silent 0 with a green dot: UFO total short,
  breakdown "0 UFO", rank badge "not holding".** portfolio.html:1611–1614 `okWord` guards the six PTGC words only;
  1661–1666 `ufoOk→ufoBalance=null` without throwing; 3790 and PortfolioV2.jsx:177/490 `d.ufo.balance||0`. The PTGC batch and
  the UFO call are separate requests, so a rotation / rate-limit between them produces exactly this. *Fix*: move the UFO call
  into the `Promise.all` at 1597 and extend `.every(okWord)` → red dot + Retry like a PTGC failure. (M-6)

### P1 — HIGH (next two weeks)

- **e9 · P1 · CONFIRMED · M / Low · FIX NOW — Holder rank ranks a wallet that sold since the 2-hourly snapshot against its
  own stale entry: one place too low, "▲ N more to pass #K" where #K is itself.** portfolio.html:1966–1968 `pfRank` has no
  address; `holder-ranks.json` `b` is bare balances (addresses only in `top`, 110). Worked on the real list: index 49
  (708,163,209) with its snapshot balance → `{r:50,pass:1,043,099}` ✓; after selling ONE token → `{r:51,pass:2}`. The Top 100
  checker fixed this for `top` only (A2, `t1Without`); the League box, breakdown badges, rank window hero and WHAT IT TAKES
  ladder inherit it. 3,844 ties in the list make the ghost common. *Fix*: write `readBlock` in `build-holder-ranks.mjs`, read
  the wallet's `balanceOf` at that block (one archive `eth_call`, `pfhRpc` already does these) and drop that one key before
  ranking; or extend `top` to the first ~2,000 rows (≈100 KB) and dedupe by address for every Dolphin-or-better wallet.
  (M-2, U-3)
- **e10 · P1 · CONFIRMED · M / Medium · FIX NOW — Rank window: "today" is ranked on PulseScan's list (shared ties,
  `is_contract`), every other day on the chain-replayed index (dense ties, `eth_getCode`) — different populations (17,247
  vs 17,071), so "▼ N places" has a built-in step for everyone below ~#1,000.** Re-ranking all 17,071 index wallets' yesterday
  balances with `pfRank` on today's file: ranks 1–100 median +0, 1,001–5,000 median +19, 5,001–10,000 median +64, 10,000+
  median +122. *Fix*: one population — `build-rank-history.mjs` takes its exclusions from the same source as
  `build-holder-ranks.mjs` and both tie the same way; until then compute the last point the index way. (M-3)
- **e11 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — "BEST EVER" / "LOWEST" tiles are the best / worst of the SELECTED period.**
  PfRank.jsx:90–92/136–137 (`pts=full.slice(i0)`). 14,141 of 15,317 wallets held ≥ 400 days have a life-best better than their
  1Y-best (the default period). *Fix*: compute the tiles from `full`; keep the period's best for the chart gridline. (M-4)
- **e12 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Rewards + Fresh Capital calculators: UFO "your share" is 9.1 % lower than
  the Portfolio's for the same bag.** calculators.html:4031–4035 `eligible = circ − LP` (800.36 B live) vs portfolio
  `pfUfoEligible` = supply − burn − contract − OA wallet (71.76 B) − 13 pools (728.56 B). 1 B UFO: 0.1249 % vs 0.1373 %.
  *Fix* (formula change, shown): subtract the OA wallet + token contract in calculators (two `balanceOf`, addresses in
  `PF_UFO_EXCL`), or publish one `elig` from the pipeline for both pages. (M-5)
- **e13 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Token Allocation tier counts: the burn address is PTGC's only "Poseidon",
  the DAO treasury and four unknown PulseX pools are "Sharks", ~1,088 contracts are "Squid & below".**
  `scripts/build-token-allocation.mjs:149` ("do NOT exclude the burn address"), index.html:4892–4911. File: Poseidon 1 (no
  person holds ≥ 5.75 %), Whale 13 vs 12 people ≥ 1 %, Shark 104 vs 108 people ≥ 0.1 %. The Top 100 window on the same panel
  says "people only". *Fix*: reuse `build-holder-ranks.mjs`'s `EXCLUDE` + `is_contract` in the builder and in
  `fetchHolderTiers`; or caption "addresses incl. burn". A filter, not a formula. (N-2)
- **e14 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Three pages hang on a stalled fetch with no timeout, no error, no Retry.**
  (a) portfolio.html:1178–1186 / 1244–1251 bare `fetch` on DexScreener gates `initialLoad` → with a 40 s stall the page is
  blank at 20 s and ZERO wallet reads were issued (harness `SLOW=40000`); (b) index.html:13454–13460 PTGC awaits six raw
  data reads (no signal) BEFORE `fetchDex` → `DATA_SLOW=30000` left `#/ptgc` a skeleton at 14 s while `#/ufo` painted; the
  ladder is gated on `loading` so a hang is forever; (c) calculators.html:5009–5018 gate awaits bare `fetch`es → "Loading
  Calculators…" at 22 s. *Fix*: one `fetchData(url, 8000)` with AbortController for the ~30 raw reads; don't gate wallet
  reads on prices (`setInitialLoad(false)` before the await); move PTGC's `await cachesP` after first paint as UFO does; use
  the page's own `dsJson` at calculators 308/638. (R-1, R-2, R-3)
- **e15 · P1 · POTENTIAL RISK · S / Low · FIX NOW — A value-generated.json older than 3 h makes every UFO visit fire ~950
  RPC calls (864 `eth_getLogs`), 312 of them Mint scans on four pools the contract never mints into.** index.html:4061,
  13087–13095, 2620–2680; `HARDCODED_UFO_PAIRS` grew 3→7 on 2026-10-06; the pipeline builder scans 4. Ten viewers during a
  pipeline stall ≈ 9,000 calls in half a minute → `-32005` → every endpoint in cooldown for everyone. *Fix*: pass only the
  three constructor pools (the 1947 filter) — 864 → 552 with identical numbers; later raise the browser-rescan trigger
  toward the 48 h interim limit. (R-4)
- **e16 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — portfolio and calculators carry the pre-a41 RPC pool: no cooldown, no
  `-32005` rotation, no transport flag; portfolio's `getLogsRange` bisects on a dead transport (7× the calls).**
  portfolio.html:1111–1125, 1412–1439; calculators.html:228–242. Harness `LOGS_503=1`: 168 getLogs posts for 8 ranges vs 24
  with index's logic; on a hanging node ≈ 21 min per wallet of "scanning the chain…". *Fix*: copy index.html:1839–1890 into
  both pages and the two a41 lines into `getLogsRange`. (R-5)
- **e17 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — The disclaimer's "I Understand & Accept" is below the fold on every phone
  (358 px hidden at 390, 667 at 320; 106 px at 1380×900), one scroll box, no cue, no Escape.** index.html:17521–17570
  (`maxHeight:'90vh'`). First screen every new phone visitor sees. *Fix*: flex column, text in `flex-1 overflow-y-auto`, the
  button in a fixed footer, `90dvh`; copy unchanged so no `DISCLAIMER_VERSION` bump. (V-1, U-4)
- **e18 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — The four CDN `<script>` tags block the parser, so the c14 boot shell cannot
  paint until ~752 KB gz of scripts has arrived.** index.html:63–69 (+ portfolio 13–15, calculators 13–16, ledger 12–14).
  Measured (CDP throttle): 1.6 Mbps FCP 9,032 ms → 788 ms with `defer`; 6 Mbps 2,672 → 324 ms; mount time unchanged; the four
  React pages mount cleanly deferred (Babel's `transformScriptTags` runs on DOMContentLoaded). Leave charts.html alone.
  *Fix*: `defer` on the tags; teach `tools/harness/run.js:14–20` to keep it. (P-1)
- **e19 · P1 · CONFIRMED (live) · S / Low · FIX NOW — The committed `tw.css` is stale: 63 dead selectors from the old
  Affiliates page, and `.text-purple-500/40` (index.html:17285, the receipts' copy icon) has no rule on the live site.**
  Committed 83,975 B vs a fresh `npm run css` 79,711 B; live `tw.css?v=2` fetched from the Mac = the stale 83,975 B file. The
  harness serves its own `tw.out.css` (run.js:15), so no screenshot could show it. *Fix*: `npm run css`, commit, `?v=3` on five
  pages; add `TW=committed` to the harness and `git diff --exit-code tw.css` to the pre-push checklist. (P-2)
- **e20 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — `npm run compile` compiles 0 bytes of portfolio.html and calculators.html;
  nothing runs in CI.** tools/harness/compile.js:3 takes the FIRST babel tag, which on those pages is `src="h2-header.jsx"`
  (empty body) → "compiled ok 0 bytes". *Fix*: `matchAll` over every body-carrying babel tag + `h2-header.jsx`, loop the five
  pages, exit 1 on the first error; then the ~3-minute CI job in U-19 (Playwright container, compile ×5, mount ×5, fail on
  console errors / boot shell still present) on pushes that touch the pages. (U-7, U-19)
- **e21 · P1 · LIKELY · S / Low · FIX NOW — A compile error leaves "LOADING…" on index.html forever and a black page on
  the four sibling pages.** index.html:1553–1560; the siblings' `#root` is empty; no `error` listener anywhere. *Fix*: a
  6-line head script on all five pages that swaps the shell text on a Babel error or after 12 s with `#root` unchanged; the
  siblings get the same 10-line shell as index. (U-8)
- **e22 · P1 · POTENTIAL RISK · S / Low · FIX NOW — Nothing alerts when the pipeline stops being STARTED, and the
  `DISPATCH_TOKEN` expiry is unrecorded.** `v7:108–131` logs 401/403/404 to a Cloudflare console nobody reads; GitHub's :37
  cron fires ~4×/day; it already happened for 7 days (gotcha, 2026-10-06). Created ~2026-10-07 with "a long expiry" — a 30-day
  default dies ~2026-11-05. *Fix*: note the expiry in HANDOVER today (regenerate ≥ 1 year); a secret-less
  `pipeline-watchdog.yml` on `23 */3 * * *` that exits 1 when `pipeline-status.json` is > 3 h old or any step's
  `lastSuccessAt` is past its cadence — GitHub's own failure mail is the alert. (D-3)
- **e23 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Pipeline honesty gaps: five builders carry forward WITHOUT `::warning::`
  (status says `carried: 0`, summary ✅); an unparseable history file is treated as "first run" and would commit a one-row
  `lv-snapshots.json` / `holder-history.json` (the only copies); `holder-history.json` is trimmed to 365 rows.**
  `pipeline-run.mjs:64`; `build-charts-data.js:249–263`, `build-charts-intraday.js:159`, `build-pair-volume.mjs:107`,
  `fetch-coingecko-data.js:556`, `build-value-generated.mjs:875`; `lv-snapshot.js:135–141`, `fetch-burn-history.js:241–244,
  901–912, 961–966`; `fetch-ufo-ptgc-burns.js:298–314`. *Fix*: one `::warning::` line per site; "exists but unreadable →
  `process.exit(1)`" in four places; delete the 365-row trim. (D-2, D-1, D-8)
- **e24 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Calculators on a phone (the default tab): "Fee 5 %/6 %" printed over the
  token names (29 px overprint), "Split" clipped, Moon Math's "📂 Load from Portfolio →" cut mid-word.** calculators.html:
  4548–4566, 4692–4702, 3582–3591. *Fix*: `flex-col sm:flex-row` on the two rows, `flex-wrap` on the bag row. (V-3, V-4)
- **e25 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Leagues window at 390: "STARTING SUPPLY" / "CIRCULATING SUPPLY" collide
  (`left:50%` caps the toggle at half the panel); Socials "Combined" header overflows its column at 901–1150 px (title cut

- **e26 · P1 · CONFIRMED · S / Low · FIX NOW — Affiliates v2: referrer rows, name spans, "▶ n" expanders and sort headers

## SECURITY FINDINGS

e6 (Worker path traversal; unauthenticated uncached fan-out to the owner's PAT), e5 (Worker money-logic edges), e32 (Load
Code DoS-by-paste), e41 (dead password hash in the page; `monthlyLogs` projection; iframe `sandbox` c10; `CORS *`,
non-constant-time compare, token fallback c41; no CSP / HSTS / XFO on the Pages custom domain). **Found OK**: no secret in
7,839 tracked files (the CoinGecko key only ever reaches a request header; the published status file carries no key); workflow
inputs reach the shell only through `env:`, no `pull_request_target`, minimal `permissions`, zero npm dependencies in CI;
`deploy.yml`'s allow-list keeps `handover/`, `tools/`, `scripts/`, `design/`, `_to_delete/` and the caches off the site (the
repo itself is public — the notes are readable on GitHub, a deliberate choice); 0 `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` / `eval`; every
`innerHTML` sink traced to constants, numbers or the pipeline's own files; all external data rendered as JSX; every data-built
`href` has a fixed `https://` prefix; `target=_blank` ↔ `noopener` 32/32; URL params and hash routes exact-whitelisted; the
`GRAY-` decoder is shape-checked on every load; all 13 CDN script tags + the lazy html2canvas loader carry sha384 SRI that
matches the npm tarballs; no `postMessage` listener; no applicable dependency advisory (Babel CVE-2025-27789 needs a transform
the `react` preset never runs). Not checked: git history (depth-1 clone) — run `gitleaks` once on the Mac over all 23,935
commits.

## PERFORMANCE FINDINGS

e18 (parser-blocking CDN scripts — the single biggest first-paint win, measured 9.0 s → 0.8 s at 1.6 Mbps), e15 (the stale-file
UFO rescan: ~950 RPC calls per visitor), e34 (Live Feed polling in hidden tabs, portfolio's unbatched per-wallet bursts,
duplicate UFO fee reads), e38 (Logos thumbnails 5.6 MB, Chart.js on the critical path, no preconnect / shell on the siblings,
nested components, box-shadow animation), e33 (pipeline: one RPC, 59 % removable `eth_call`s in top-stakers, 6,900 git blobs a
day from rank-index, 398 s value-generated rescan). Baseline (sweep P): index.html 1.49 MB / 403 KB gz, Babel compile ~2.7 s
long task on this CPU; a PTGC visit reads 1.5 MB raw / 292 KB gz of JSON + ~1.5 MB of art; CDN scripts 752 KB gz. **Found
OK**: identical pinned versions and matching SRI on all pages; `data-presets="react"` everywhere (c1); c12/c13/c14/c15 landed
(no play CDN, `no-cache` reads, 512 px logos, WebP plates, matched preloads); every interval `document.hidden`-guarded except
the deck; listeners and observers balanced; every Chart.js instance destroyed; `canvases: 0` at mount; the Top 100 list is
memoised with module-scope rows; idle runtime quiet.

  Allocation tier counts from it (PTGC ≥ 1 %: 12, ≥ 0.1 %: 108, ≥ 0.01 %: 804 today) and publish `pfUfoEligible`'s result as
  `data/ufo-eligible.json` — e12 and e13 close by construction and the panel agrees with its own Top 100 window.

  removes 15 live reads per PTGC visit and most of e3's failure surface.
- **A `tools/harness/test/` folder, no browser**: `extract.js` (written in sweep U) pulls the pure helpers (`fmtAbbr`,
  `fmtPrice`, `pfRank`, `pfLeague`, `t1Rank`/`t1Without`, `encodePortfolio`, `computeValueGen`, `creatureCount`, …) out of the
  babel blocks; the ten table tests listed in sweep U would have caught e1, e7, e9, e36 and e46 the day they were written.
  Runs in the CI job before the mount.
- **A pipeline watchdog that is also data**: let `pipeline-watchdog.yml` write `data/pipeline-health.json` — the g4 freshness
  strip and the c51 Telegram digest then have one 200-byte file to read.
- **Execution price for Sell / Buy rows from a request already made**: `pftDetail` fetches the tx's token transfers; the
  WPLS / DAI counter-leg ÷ the token amount × the WPLS price is "the price each trade got" (e40's wording promise) with no new
  calls.
- **Seed the Live Feed's Top Pools from `data/swap-volume.json`** (per-pool hourly rows for 90 days) instead of the 80-chunk
  scan on first open; scan only since `generatedAt`.
- **`value-generated` checkpoint in the swap-volume cache pattern**: keep decoded rows for `[scanFrom, head − 1 day]` in an
  undeployed cache → 912 → ~20 `eth_getLogs`, 398 s → ~30 s, same sums (c19's unfinished half).
- **"Snapshot vs live" line in the rank footer** ("list read 12 min ago · your balance live") — makes e9 explainable until it
  is fixed, and the per-token `readAt` is already in the file.
- **A Portfolio jump row** under 640 px (Holdings · PTGC · UFO · History · Transactions · Wallets) on the sticky band — the
  section anchors exist; the page is 8,700 px on a phone.
- **A "Windows" row under the KPI tiles** (Volume · Holders · RH · Top 100 · Leagues · DAO Buys) and renaming the Socials tab
  "Share" — three windows today are reachable only through "RH" / "ⓘ" and the share cards only through "Socials".
- **A nightly reconciliation line** in the pipeline log: Σ(Transfer→0x369) vs `balanceOf(0x369)` per token — e39 becomes a
  known constant instead of a question.

## THINGS I WOULD NOT CHANGE

- **The architecture as it stands**: one `index.html` compiled in the browser with `data-presets="react"`, the committed
  `tw.css`, the `tools/*.py` fragment splicers with their markers, the headless harness and its probes. The build (Vite /
  TypeScript, b1–b2) is parked on purpose and nothing in this audit needs it; every fix above is a targeted edit inside the
  existing shape.
- **index.html's data layer**: the RPC pool with a41 (cooldown, `-32005` rotation, `transport` flag, shared head block, in-flight
  log dedupe), `dsGet` (dedupe, 30 s cache, throttle, `Retry-After`, 15-min stale fallback), `getLogsChunked` with `exactTo`,
  the retry ladder, the `loadCancelled` / `tokenRef` guards, the `LS` table with `lsGet` shape checks. Copy it to the siblings
  (e16); do not redesign it.
- **The honest-failure rules** (gotchas 3, 20, 22) and the `computeValueGen` single model (gotcha 16) — the audit found the
  places that missed them (e3, e8, e27, e36), not a flaw in the rule.
- **The pipeline's shape**: one hourly workflow, three lanes, per-step gates, `pipeline-status.json`, the carry-forward
  design, tmp+rename where it exists, the `_site` allow-list, the `[skip ci]` data commits, the :37 stand-down. e23 and e33 are
  additions to it.
- **The Modal shell and PfModal** (dialog roles, Escape stack, focus trap, scroll lock, portal) — every window that uses them
  passed; the two that don't (e26) should adopt them.
- **The number formatters on index.html and `pfAbbr`** (unit after rounding, `en-US`, the sub-micro notation with the
  `sr-only` full decimal) — port them, don't rewrite them.
- **Portfolio v2's transaction engine** (`pftKey` dedupe, per-tx leg netting, Move handling from either side, the fee gross-up
  rules, the coverage reporting) and the rank-index method (reflection factor from six untouched holders, 30/30 random
  wallet-days exact) — both reproduced correctly here.
- **The deliberate decisions in the gotchas**: UFO's ATH from the old contract, no "tax" wording, the creature emojis and
  their layout, `Dashboard` not keyed by token, the owner's art and the 2026-10-22 looks (Affiliates, Top 100).

## How to work this list

Each e-item names its raw finding(s) in parentheses; the raw section has the exact lines, the probe output or the `node`
one-liner that proved it, and the smallest fix. Do the P0 tier as one batch script in `tools/` (the Audit III pattern:
idempotent, `done_marker`, "0 edit(s)" on re-run), harness-walk, push, then the live check. Items that change a displayed
number (e1, e2, e9, e10, e11, e12, e13) are marked as formula changes above with the old → new worked example — show the owner
the example before building. The Worker items (e5, e6) are a v8 of `handover/ptgcapi-worker-v7.js`, deployed by hand.

## Raw sweep S

### Sweep S — Security (Audit IV, 2026-10-22)

Scope: whole clone at `/home/claude/audit/repo` (7,839 tracked files; depth-1 clone, so git history was NOT scanned — see
Ideas), the Worker source `handover/ptgcapi-worker-v4…v7.js`, both workflows, the five pages + `h2-header.jsx` + the
`tools/*/` fragments. Live checks: `ptgc-ufo.com`, `ptgcapi.ptgctools.workers.dev` and `stats.goptgc.com` are blocked by this
sandbox's egress proxy (CONNECT 403), so no live headers were captured; `raw.githubusercontent.com` is reachable and was used
to confirm repo visibility. Harness runs need a Chromium that is not installed here — every XSS claim below is a static trace.

Still open from Audit III, confirmed still true, one line each (no new write-up):
- c10 — `index.html:6914` `<iframe key={url} src={url} … allow="clipboard-write" referrerPolicy="strict-origin"` has no `sandbox`.
- c41 — `?debug=1` public switch (`index.html:39`, `:1806`); Worker `ALLOWED_ORIGIN = '*'` (`v7:33`), `authHeader !== env.TOOLBOX_SECRET`
  non-constant-time (`v7:62`), `/write` takes any `path` (`v7:74–81`), `DISPATCH_TOKEN || GITHUB_TOKEN` fallback (`v7:108`).
- c14 — no `onerror` fallback on the unpkg / jsdelivr tags (`index.html:63–69`); unpkg down = blank page on four pages, charts.html
  survives (its React-free code only needs jsdelivr + cdnjs).
- Audit II item "I" (public handover notes name the deploy account's SMS-only 2FA) — `handover/sessions/2026-09-14.md:148–152` is
  still in the public repo (`raw.githubusercontent.com/…/handover/HANDOVER.md` → 200). Owner dropped account hardening on
  2026-09-23 (HANDOVER.md:1755) — deliberate; noted, not re-argued.

---

#### S-1 · `/public/commissions` is an unauthenticated, uncached fan-out to the owner's GitHub token — anyone can burn the token's hourly budget (and the Worker's daily quota)
- status: LIKELY ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: `handover/ptgcapi-worker-v7.js:57–58, 424–445, 535–543, 581–589`; `index.html:13192, 16220`
- problem: Every GET of the public endpoint does TWO authenticated GitHub API calls and nothing is cached or rate-limited:
  ```js
  // v7:57  if (request.method === 'GET' && path === '/public/commissions') return await getPublicCommissions(env);
  // v7:432 const metaRes = await fetch(url, { headers: headers('application/vnd.github.v3+json') });
  // v7:441 const rawRes  = await fetch(url, { headers: headers('application/vnd.github.raw') });
  // v7:427 'Authorization': `token ${env.GITHUB_TOKEN}`,
  // v7:587 'Cache-Control': 'no-store',   // v5 (2026-09-23): no proxy may hold a copy of the numbers
  ```
  There is no `caches.default`, no `cf: { cacheTtl }`, no KV, no per-IP limit. The page itself defeats every cache on purpose
  (`index.html:16220`: `` fetch(`…/public/commissions?t=${Date.now()}`,{cache:'no-store'}) ``), so even organic traffic is 2 GitHub
  calls per Affiliates-tab open.
- evidence: Traced the full path (lines above). GitHub's primary limit for a user's personal access tokens is 5,000 requests/hour
  and is shared by every PAT of that user, so `GITHUB_TOKEN` (reads, the 08:00 sync write, ToolBox's `/read`+`/write`) and
  `DISPATCH_TOKEN` (the :07 `workflow_dispatch`, `v7:107–133`) draw on one bucket. Worked example: 2,500 plain GETs of
  `/public/commissions` (≈ 1 minute with 40 parallel curls, no auth needed) → the token answers 403 for the rest of the hour →
  `readFileRaw` throws `GitHub read failed: 403` → (a) the affiliates page shows "Could not reach the commission data (HTTP 503)"
  (`v7:542`, `index.html:16221`), (b) the cron sync returns `{success:false}` (`v7:376–382`), (c) ToolBox's own reads/writes fail,
  (d) `dispatchDataPipeline` gets 403 and gives up without retry (`v7:127`) — the hourly pipeline then depends on GitHub's own
  :37 schedule, which the workflow comment says fires only ~4×/day. Separately, a Workers free plan allows 100,000 requests/day:
  at 10 req/s the endpoint is exhausted in ~2.8 h and the Worker answers 1027 errors (cron included) until midnight UTC.
- why it matters: One person with a shell can take the affiliates page, the commission sync and the hourly data pipeline down
  for an hour at a time, repeatedly, at zero cost and with no login. It also means real growth in Affiliates traffic (2,500
  tab-opens/hour) would do the same by accident.
- fix: (1) In `getPublicCommissions`, serve from the Workers cache: `const c = caches.default; const key = new Request(url.origin +
  '/public/commissions'); let hit = await c.match(key); if (hit) return hit;` … build the response with `Cache-Control:
  public, max-age=60` for the CACHE copy, `ctx.waitUntil(c.put(key, res.clone()))`, and keep `no-store` on what the browser sees
  if the 2026-09-22 proxy worry still stands (two headers: cache the Worker-side copy by key, strip before returning). That turns
  2 GitHub calls per visitor into 2 per minute. (2) One Cloudflare rate-limiting rule (free tier includes one): `/public/*` 30
  requests / 10 s per IP → 429. (3) Give the public read its own fine-grained token (Contents: read, ToolBox only) so a burned
  read budget can never block the write/sync/dispatch tokens. (4) Longer term: have the sync also `PUT` the public projection into
  KV and serve the public endpoint from KV — zero GitHub calls per visitor.
- effort: Small (1 and 2 are ~15 lines + one dashboard rule)
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### S-2 · `/read` and `/write` take `path` straight into the GitHub URL — `..` walks out of the ToolBox repo into any repo the token can reach, including the dashboard that auto-deploys to ptgc-ufo.com
- status: POTENTIAL RISK (the URL mechanics are CONFIRMED; the blast radius depends on `GITHUB_TOKEN`'s scope, which the v7
  fallback design implies is wider than ToolBox)
- priority: P1
- area: `handover/ptgcapi-worker-v7.js:66–81, 425, 478, 507`; `.github/workflows/deploy.yml:15–16`
- problem: The `path` query parameter is interpolated unencoded and unvalidated:
  ```js
  // v7:67  const filePath = url.searchParams.get('path');          // decoded: %2e%2e%2f → ../
  // v7:425 const url = `https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_OWNER}/${GITHUB_REPO}/contents/${path}`;
  // v7:507 `https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_OWNER}/${GITHUB_REPO}/contents/${path}`   (PUT)
  ```
  The WHATWG URL parser that `fetch` uses collapses dot segments before the request leaves the Worker.
- evidence: `node -e "console.log(new URL('https://api.github.com/repos/ShakaVibe/ToolBox/contents/../../PTGC-UFO-Dashboard/contents/.github/workflows/x.yml').href)"`
  → `https://api.github.com/repos/ShakaVibe/PTGC-UFO-Dashboard/contents/.github/workflows/x.yml`. So
  `PUT /write?path=../../PTGC-UFO-Dashboard/contents/index.html` with a valid `X-ToolBox-Auth` writes the dashboard's
  `index.html` on `main` (`v7:499` `branch: 'main'`), and `deploy.yml:15–16` (`push: branches: [main]`) publishes it to
  ptgc-ufo.com within minutes. `/read?path=../../<any-private-repo>/contents/<file>` reads it. The precondition is the secret +
  a token that spans repos; the v7 header (`v7:29`, "falls back to GITHUB_TOKEN if unset") only works if `GITHUB_TOKEN` already
  has Actions write on the dashboard repo, and Audit III called it "the wider ToolBox token". The secret itself is sent from a
  browser (`handover/sessions/2026-09-29.md:24–26`: "Shaka's console, ToolBox → Sync … PUT …/write?path=…"), i.e. it lives in
  the ToolBox front-end, where a malicious extension, a shared machine or a pasted console snippet can lift it — and it is
  compared with `!==` with no attempt limit (`v7:62`).
- why it matters: `handover/security-buy-sell-2026-09-14.md:14–19` is the owner's own threat model: "whoever can change
  index.html can … swap the URL for a look-alike domain with a drainer widget, or repoint the fee-share address". Today the
  ToolBox secret is a second, weaker key to exactly that, and nothing in the Worker stops it.
- fix: Smallest change, in both `/read` and `/write`: `if (!/^data\/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+\.json$/.test(filePath)) return jsonResponse({error:'bad path'},400);`
  (ToolBox only ever touches `data/affiliate-commissions.json`, `v7:34`) and build the URL with
  `encodeURIComponent(filePath).replace(/%2F/g,'/')`. Then: make `DISPATCH_TOKEN` mandatory (drop the `|| env.GITHUB_TOKEN`)
  and re-issue `GITHUB_TOKEN` as a fine-grained token scoped to ToolBox, Contents read/write only. Optional: compare the secret
  with `crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual` on equal-length byte buffers.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low (ToolBox's one path still matches)
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### S-3 · The SHA-256 of a human-chosen password ships in index.html — unsalted, one round, and the gate it protects is dead
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P2
- area: `index.html:10179–10180` (and `:1801`, `handover/sessions/2026-09-30.md:50–60`)
- problem:
  ```js
  const HEADER_PREVIEW_GATE_HASH='3937fe17477ae6bc6f7f72b358671e83a7b07bc2b00c7ec660a93083de047c37';   // sha256(hex) of the URL word (?<word>)
  const HEADER_PREVIEW_PASS_HASH='3937fe17477ae6bc6f7f72b358671e83a7b07bc2b00c7ec660a93083de047c37';   // sha256(hex) of the password
  ```
  Same hash for both (the notes: "he chose one secret for both the word and the password"), plain `sha256(str)` (`:10181–10197`),
  no salt, no stretching. `HEADER_DESIGN='v2'` (`:1801`) has made the gate moot since 2026-10-03; Audit III c42 already lists it as
  dead code.
- evidence: Lines quoted. A single-round SHA-256 of a word or short phrase is an offline dictionary job (billions of guesses per
  second on one GPU); the page is public and the repo is public, so the hash is in every scraper's copy forever. I did not
  attempt to crack it.
- why it matters: Zero consequence for the preview itself. The only consequence is reuse: if that word is also (or becomes) a
  Cloudflare, GitHub, registrar, ToolBox or wallet passphrase, it is recoverable from `view-source:ptgc-ufo.com`. The owner
  cannot "un-publish" it; he can only make sure it is used nowhere else.
- fix: Delete both constants, `sha256Hex`, `hdrPreviewRequested` and `HeaderPreviewGate` (the c42 cleanup); treat the word as
  burned — never reuse it. If a gate is ever wanted again, put it in the Worker (the notes already say so).
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER (bundle with c42)

#### S-4 · Load Code accepts any number of wallets and any name length — a crafted `GRAY-` code is a one-paste self-DoS that persists
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P2
- area: `portfolio.html:1577–1595` (`sanitizeWallets`, `decodePortfolio`), `:3659–3666` (fan-out), `:3637` (persist), `:3814–3824`
  (`handleLoadCode`); `calculators.html:2580, 2597, 2626, 2682, 2931`; `index.html:12439`
- problem: The decoder validates the shape but caps nothing:
  ```js
  // :1581 if(!isValidAddr(address)||seen.has(address.toLowerCase())){dropped++;return;}
  // :1583 wallets.push({address,name:typeof w.name==='string'?w.name:(typeof w.n==='string'?w.n:''),group:…||'Default'});
  // :3661 wallets.forEach(w=>{ if(!portfolioData[w.address.toLowerCase()]){ fetchWalletData(w.address); } });
  // :3637 try{localStorage.setItem(PORTFOLIO_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(wallets));}catch{}
  ```
  `handleLoadCode` replaces the list wholesale (`:3820 setWallets(decoded.wallets)`); `addWallet` has no cap either; the LABEL
  input (`tools/pf-v2/PortfolioV2.jsx:268`) has no `maxLength`.
- evidence: `fetchPortfolioData` (`:1597–1700`) issues 10 `callContract`/`rpcFetch` calls per wallet before the reflections scan
  is queued. Worked example (node): 2,000 distinct valid addresses with empty names encode to a ≈130 KB `GRAY-…` string, which
  pastes fine; `sanitizeWallets` keeps all 2,000; mount fires 20,000 `eth_call`s at once against the three public RPCs
  (`:1089–1093`), then 2,000 queued `eth_getLogs` scans; the list is saved, so every later visit — and `calculators.html`
  (`:2580…`, staking reads per wallet) and the index Top-100 window (`:12439`) — repeats it until "Clear saved wallets & reload"
  (`:4060`). The 40-iteration quota loop in `pfLsSet` (`:1864`) then thrashes on the per-wallet caches.
- why it matters: Social engineering is cheap in a Telegram community ("paste this code to see the whale wallets"). The victim's
  browser hangs; the shared RPC endpoints rate-limit everyone (`-32005` rotation, `:1119–1121`) — the public RPC is the same
  pool index.html uses for every visitor's prices and burns.
- fix: In `sanitizeWallets`: `const MAX=50;` stop after 50 kept entries (report `dropped`), `name:(…).slice(0,40)`,
  `group:(…).slice(0,24)`; `handleLoadCode` says "code holds N wallets — the first 50 were loaded"; `maxLength={40}` on the
  LABEL input. No financial formula touched.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### S-5 · The public endpoint ships `monthlyLogs` whole while `receipts` are projected — buyers' wallets (and any future ToolBox-only field) leak by default
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P3
- area: `handover/ptgcapi-worker-v7.js:549–566, 271–281`
- problem:
  ```js
  // v7:550 referrers: data.referrers || {},
  // v7:551 monthlyLogs: data.monthlyLogs || {},          // whole object
  // v7:552 receipts: (data.receipts || []).map(r => ({ id: r.id, … feeCovered: r.feeCovered })),   // 12 named fields
  ```
  Each entry's `buys[]` carries `buyerWallet: tx.user_address` (`v7:273`) — the referred CUSTOMERS' wallets, not the affiliates'.
- evidence: `grep -c buyerWallet index.html` → 0: the page never shows it, so it is shipped for nothing. The receipts projection
  exists precisely because receipts carry fields that must stay private (`voidReason`, `v7:16–17`); entries get no such filter,
  so anything ToolBox writes into an entry tomorrow (a note, a dispute flag) is public the same hour.
- why it matters: Low today (the referral API upstream is unauthenticated, so the pairing is obtainable elsewhere), but it is
  an allow-nothing-by-default rule being applied to half the payload only.
- fix: Project entries to the fields `index.html` reads (`username, wallet, buysCount, usdAmount, ptgcAmount, commissionRate,
  commissionPtgc, meetsThreshold, status, paidTxHash, paidDate, lastSyncDate`, and `buys[] → {date, usdAmount, ptgcAmount,
  txHash, paid, paidTxHash, paidDate}`) plus `settings` and `lastSyncDate` per month.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low (the page's shape guard only requires `referrers` and `monthlyLogs` to be objects, `index.html:16223`)
- recommendation: LATER

### Checked and found OK

**Secrets (whole tree, node_modules excluded, 7,839 tracked files).** Zero hits for `ghp_`, `github_pat_`, `sk-`, `xox*`,
`AKIA`, `BEGIN … PRIVATE KEY`, JWTs, `Bearer …`, Telegram bot tokens, `key=`/`token=` URL credentials, `password|secret|api_key
= "…"` literals, email addresses or phone numbers. The only secret-shaped strings are the two SHA-256 constants (S-3) and
PulseChain addresses. The CoinGecko key exists only as `${{ secrets.COINGECKO_API_KEY }}` → env → request header
`x-cg-pro-api-key` (`scripts/build-charts-data.js:75`, `build-charts-intraday.js:74`, `fetch-coingecko-data.js:73`,
`build-pair-volume.mjs:46`), never in a URL; the runner logs only `key=set|MISSING` (`scripts/pipeline-run.mjs:101`) and the
published `data/pipeline-status.json` carries no key (`lastError` is the child's last 300 chars of stderr, which never prints it).
Worker v4–v7 all read `env.TOOLBOX_SECRET` / `env.GITHUB_TOKEN` bindings; no literal in any version. No `.env` tracked.
`.DS_Store` is tracked but holds only the word "handover". NOT checked: git history (depth-1 clone) — see Ideas.

**Workflows.** `only=`/`force=` reach the shell only as `env: ONLY/FORCE` (`data-pipeline.yml:57–59`) and are read via
`process.env` (`scripts/pipeline-gate.mjs:19–20`, `pipeline-run.mjs:101,133`) — never interpolated into a `run:` string;
`workflow_dispatch` needs write access to fire anyway. No `pull_request_target`, no `issue_comment` triggers. `permissions` are
minimal and explicit (`contents: write` for the data job; `contents: read` + `pages: write` + `id-token: write` for deploy).
Only first-party `actions/*` (checkout@v7, setup-node@v7, configure-pages@v4, upload-pages-artifact@v3, deploy-pages@v4),
major-tag pinned — SHA pinning stays the owner's own "minor" item. `concurrency` + `timeout-minutes` set on the data job. Data
commits use `GITHUB_TOKEN` + `[skip ci]`, cannot trigger deploy. The pipeline scripts import only `node:` builtins (`fs`, `path`,
`https`, `child_process`) — zero npm dependencies run in CI; `spawn` is called with fixed argv, no shell.

**What is published vs. what is public.** `deploy.yml:56–58` ships `*.html *.css *.jsx *.png robots.txt sitemap.xml logos/`
and `data/*.json` minus `ptgc-burns-*.json`, `ufo-ptgc-burns-cache.json`, `swap-volume-cache.json`, `top-stakers-cache.json`
(`-maxdepth 1`, so `rank-history/`, `rank-index/`, `ufo-v1-earned/` are not on the site either — the pages read them from raw
GitHub). `handover/`, `tools/`, `scripts/`, `design/`, `_to_delete/`, `fetch-metrics.js`, root `holder-history.json` are NOT on
ptgc-ufo.com. They ARE public on github.com — the repo is public (`raw.githubusercontent.com/ShakaVibe/PTGC-UFO-Dashboard/main/
handover/HANDOVER.md` → 200, 223,397 B; `…/handover/ptgcapi-worker-v7.js` → 200). The ToolBox repo is private (`…/ShakaVibe/
ToolBox/main/README.md` → 404). The four excluded caches and root `holder-history.json` hold chain-derived data only
(`lastBlock, addrs`; `schema, cursor, pools, senders, swaps…`). `Claude outputs/` is git-ignored and absent from the tree.
`robots.txt` allows everything; `sitemap.xml` lists the pages.

**XSS sinks.** 0 `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, 0 `eval` / `new Function` / string `setTimeout` / `srcdoc` / `document.write` /
`insertAdjacentHTML` across the five pages, `h2-header.jsx`, `tools/pf-v2/*.jsx`, `tools/top100/t1.jsx`, `tools/kpi-v2.jsx`.
`innerHTML` sinks, each traced: `index.html:5691` (SVG `<path d>` from numbers), `:7185–7195` and `:7329–7331` (DAO-buys
tooltips: numbers + `b.wallet.slice(0,6)…slice(-4)` from the pipeline's own `data/dao-buys.json`), `:16683` (static);
`charts.html:491, 1083, 1162, 1168, 1195, 1290 (setStatus — all seven callers pass constants/numbers/TOKENS keys), 1375 (tk.logo/
tk.sym from the static TOKENS table :842–862), 2166, 2290, 2345–2357, 2432, 2453, 2476, 2518` (all from `TOKENS`/`RANGES`/
`PRESETS` constants + numbers). Nothing from DexScreener, GeckoTerminal, PulseScan, CoinGecko, the Worker or localStorage reaches
an HTML string. Affiliate usernames/wallets (22 `.username` sites), pair symbols, PulseScan token names, wallet labels are all
JSX children/attributes (React-escaped). Logo URLs from DexScreener land only in `<img src>` (not a script sink).

**Links.** All 33 template-literal `href`s in index.html start with a fixed `https://…/` or `./…` prefix (DexScreener, DEXTools,
scan.pulsechain.com, logos/); the one bare-variable href is the `SWITCH_ORIGIN` constant (`:6920`); the referral link is
`encodeURIComponent`'d (`:16680`). `javascript:` is unreachable. `target="_blank"` ↔ `noopener`: index 27/27, portfolio 2/2,
calculators 1/1, charts 1/1, ledger 1/1. The single `window.open` passes `'noopener'` (`index.html:7168`). 0 `http://` src/href
(no mixed content).

**URL / hash / storage inputs.** `?token`, `?from` → exact `'PTGC'|'UFO'` compare on every page (`index.html:17660–17664`,
`portfolio.html:1168`, `calculators.html:4974–4975`, `charts.html:528–531`); `?open` → `'swap'|'feed'` only (`index.html:17661`);
`?home` regex-whitelisted (`:1793`); hash route whitelisted (`:17588–17604`); `?debug=1` is a boolean regex. There is no
`?code=`/`?wallet=` — Load Code is paste-only; the `GRAY-` decoder is prefix → `atob` → `JSON.parse` → `sanitizeWallets` inside
try/catch (address regex `^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$`, string-typed name/group, dedupe), invalid → "Invalid portfolio code". The saved
list is re-sanitized on every load (`:3596`). Portfolio's `goBack` builds `index.html?token=<from>` (`:4026–4029`) — the
destination is fixed, index re-validates. Console output is a no-op without `?debug=1` (`index.html:39`); the wallet addresses
that `portfolio.html:3680` / `calculators.html:2612` log on error are the user's own, in the user's own console.

**SRI / CDN.** Every external script tag carries sha384 + `crossorigin="anonymous"`: index (react, react-dom, babel, chart.js),
portfolio/calculators/ledger (react, react-dom, babel[, chart.js]), charts (chart.js, date-fns adapter, html2canvas) — 13 tags,
plus the lazy html2canvas loader sets `el.integrity`/`el.crossOrigin` (`index.html:6515–6523`). Tailwind is the committed
`tw.css?v=2` on all five pages (c13 done) — the "one unverifiable script" from the 2026-09-14 note is gone. Only the Google
Fonts stylesheet has no SRI (impossible by design — the CSS varies by UA; it executes no script). Hosts actually contacted by a
visitor: 23 (list from grep; the privacy-paragraph point is c41's).

**Worker (beyond S-1/S-2).** Auth is fail-closed when the binding is missing (`null !== undefined` → 401). `CORS *` + custom
header + no cookies = no CSRF surface. `/public/commissions` leaks only `e.message` of a GitHub status on 503. Cache-Control
`no-store` is applied to every JSON answer. The sync dedupes on `transaction_hash`, filters by `DATA_START_DATE`, and writes compact
JSON with the read sha (optimistic concurrency). Dispatch posts `inputs: {}` with the API version header and stops on 401/403/404.

**Switch widget.** `partnerAddress` only when it matches `^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$` (`index.html:1686`); `allow="clipboard-write"`,
`referrerPolicy="strict-origin"`; no `message` listener and no `postMessage` on any page; no `window.ethereum`.

**Dependencies.** react / react-dom 18.3.1 — no advisory (CVE-2025-55182 "React2Shell" concerns `react-server-dom-*` 19.x,
not used). @babel/standalone 7.26.4 — CVE-2025-27789 (ReDoS in generated code for named-capturing-group regex, fixed 7.26.10)
only applies when the `transform-named-capturing-groups-regex` plugin runs; all five pages use `data-presets="react"` only
(`index.html:1562`, `portfolio.html:1073–1074`, `calculators.html:85–86`, `ledger.html:26`), so the transform never runs — bump
when the SRI hashes are next refreshed, not urgent. chart.js 4.4.1, chartjs-adapter-date-fns 3.0.0 (date-fns 2.x), html2canvas
1.4.1 — no advisories (html2canvas has had no release since 2022; abandonment risk, not a vulnerability). `tools/harness`
(`playwright-core 1.47.0`, `tailwindcss 3.4.16`) is local-only, `node_modules` untracked. No root `package.json`/lockfile.

**Nothing IDOR-like.** Every per-wallet read is public chain data via public RPC/PulseScan; the Worker exposes one public
projection and the rest behind the secret; no per-user state exists server-side.

### Ideas

- Run `gitleaks detect --source . --log-opts="--all"` once on the Mac (the full 23,935-commit history): this clone is depth 1,
  the notes describe secrets being handled by hand (`sessions/2026-09-30.md:58`), and a historical paste is the one class of
  leak this sweep could not rule out.
- Serve `/public/commissions` from KV written by the sync (one `PUT` per sync, zero GitHub calls per visitor) — removes S-1's
  token coupling entirely and lets the page drop its `?t=` buster for an ETag.
- `crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual(enc(authHeader), enc(env.TOOLBOX_SECRET))` after an equal-length check — three lines, closes
  the c41 compare note for good.
- The affiliates fixture in `tools/harness/run.js:257–259` quotes real usernames from the live program (one is an e-mail local
  part with a domain hint). Harmless to the site, but it is the only place the public repo pins a specific person to the
  program — swap for the synthetic a54 names when the harness is next touched.

## Raw sweep N

### Sweep N — Data & calculation accuracy in index.html (2026-10-22)

Scope: every calculation in index.html that produces a number a visitor sees, traced model by model and tested with `node -e` against the real `data/*.json` of the clone (pipeline run 2026-10-22 21:08–21:16 UTC). Line numbers are the clone's. The Top 100 source is `tools/top100/t1.jsx` (spliced into index.html); its line is given alongside.

#### N-1 · Top 100 "Your cushion" uses the wrong neighbour — overstated for every wallet inside the top 100
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: index.html:12565 (`tools/top100/t1.jsx:161`), printed at 12573 (stakers) and 12586 (holders)
- problem: `const cush=Math.max(0,k-(list[100]||0));` — `list` is the ranked amounts WITHOUT the checked wallet (`t1Without`, 12561), so `list[99]` is the 100th OTHER wallet and `list[100]` the 101st other. A wallet inside the top 100 drops out the moment its balance falls below `list[99]` (that wallet becomes #100, the checked wallet #101). The tile says "Your cushion … above #101 — sell more than this and you drop out" but subtracts the 101st other = the overall #102.
- evidence: `data/holder-ranks.json` PTGC: `b[99]=412,485,544` (#100), `b[100]=405,339,754` (#101), `b[101]=391,961,562` (#102). Checking the #100 wallet itself (k = 412,485,544): `t1Without` removes its own entry → `list[99]=405,339,754`, `list[100]=391,961,562`. Code prints cushion = 412,485,544 − 391,961,562 = **20,523,982**; the true cushion is 412,485,544 − 405,339,754 = **7,145,790**. Selling anything between 7.2M and 20.5M PTGC puts this wallet at #101 while the tile said it was safe. For the #50 wallet (708,163,209): code 316,201,647 vs true 302,823,455. Overstated by `list[99]−list[100]` for every wallet in the top 100, both views (holders and stakers share `cush`).
- why it matters: it is the one tile that tells a top-100 holder how much they can sell "and stay in"; it is always too generous.
- fix: `const cush=Math.max(0,k-(list[99]||0));` (financial-formula change: old `k−list[100]`, new `k−list[99]`; worked example above: 20.52M → 7.15M for #100). "To pass #N" (`up=list[rank-2]`) and "To break in" (`T2=list[99]`) are right and unchanged. Re-run the `tools/top100` splice.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: FIX NOW

#### N-2 · Holder-tier counts count the burn address as the only PTGC "Poseidon" (and LP pools / DAO wallets as Sharks)
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: `scripts/build-token-allocation.mjs:149–153` (file read at index.html:13571–13582), live scan index.html:4892–4911, printed at 14736–14740 (Token Allocation panel), 10324 (`CreatureStripV2`), 8520 (Allocation share card), `HoldersSocialModal`/`HoldersSingleModal` via `holdersTierCounts`
- problem: the tier scan excludes only the known LP pools and the staking contract — `// Tiers. Exclude LP pools + staking; do NOT exclude the burn address (matches the app)` — so every address PulseScan lists is a "holder". `0x…0369` holds 46,327,984,469.8 PTGC = 13.90 % of starting supply → `counts.Poseidon++` (≥ 10 %, line 4906). The panel prints "🔱 1".
- evidence: `token-allocation.json` PTGC tiers `{Poseidon:1, Whale:13, Shark:104, Dolphin:714, SquidAndBelow:17359}`; `holder-ranks.json` (people only, burn/DAO/contracts removed) has NO PTGC wallet above 19,180,154,537 = 5.75 %, so the one Poseidon can only be the burn address. Cross-count on the people-only list: ≥ 1 % → 12 wallets (panel 1+13 = 14), ≥ 0.1 % → 108 (panel 118), ≥ 0.01 % → 804 (panel 832). The extra "Sharks" are the DAO treasury (`0xeeac…`, 1,999,973,694 = 0.60 %) and PulseX pools the exclude list does not know (`Liquid/pTGC` 1.68 B, `Burn/pTGC` 1.38 B, `808/pTGC` 1.10 B, `TEDDY BEAR/pTGC` 0.48 B — from `burn-summary.json` pairs). UFO: panel Whale 18 vs 16 people ≥ 1 %. "Squid & below" = PulseScan total − counted − 8 → includes ~1,088 contract addresses (`holder-ranks` `left.contracts`). The Top 100 window on the same panel says "people only — left out: … the burn addresses, the DAO wallets".
- why it matters: "1 Poseidon" reads as "someone owns 10 % of PTGC"; it is the burn. Two surfaces on one panel disagree about who a holder is.
- fix: in `build-token-allocation.mjs` add the burn address, the DAO wallets/contract and any `is_contract` holder to `exclude` (the `EXCLUDE` set `build-holder-ranks.mjs:44–49` already has), subtract them from `SquidAndBelow`; mirror in `fetchHolderTiers` (skip `holder.address.is_contract` and `ADDR.BURN`). A filter, not a formula change. If Shaka prefers "addresses incl. burn", the caption must say so.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: FIX NOW

#### N-3 · DAO Treasury panel prints "$0" / an understated "Total DAO Value" when a price or balance read fails
- status: LIKELY ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: index.html:4676–4815 (`fetchDAOData`), called at 13622 with `p` from `fetchPLS()` (13462); printed 14786–14872
- problem: three places turn a failed read into 0 and multiply (gotcha 3/20): (a) `fetchPLS()` returns 0 when DexScreener and both stable pools fail (4228, 3711); `fetchDAOData(…,p)` then computes `availableUSD=availablePLS*plsPrice` (4797) = 0, `pendingUSD` = 0, `managementTokens[0].usdValue=mgmtPLS*0`, and `totalValue` (4799) omits every PLS — printed as `fmtUSD(daoData.availableUSD||0)` = "$0", unlabelled. (b) `const mgmtPLSWei=BigInt(mgmtBalanceResult.result||'0x0')` (4729) — an RPC miss (`{result:null}`) is "0 PLS". (c) `priceMap[…]||0` (4762): a token whose price failed contributes $0 silently (not even listed). `fetchDAOData` runs once per load; `quickRefresh` never re-runs it, so "$0" stays until a reload.
- evidence: `treasury-summary.json` wallet1: 3,148,866,225 PLS, 13,257,855 HEX, 28,033 PLSX, 1,999,973,694 PTGC; `dao-buys.json` latest grid PLS = $0.00000543296 → the management wallet's PLS alone is 3,148,866,225 × 0.00000543 ≈ **$17,100** that becomes $0 under (a) or (b). The DAO contract's `eth_getBalance` is guarded (4682); the management wallet's is not.
- why it matters: the treasury panel and its share card are quoted by the community; a quiet $0 on a DexScreener wobble is a wrong money number.
- fix: `if(!(plsPrice>0))return null` at the top of `fetchDAOData` (panel shows "—", the a16 path); treat `mgmtBalanceResult.result==null` like 4682; carry `price:null` for a failed token price, print "—" for that row and mark `totalValue` partial. No formula change.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: FIX NOW

#### N-4 · Referrer modal: per-transaction commission and status ignore the entry's threshold verdict (a54 residue)
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: index.html:16608 (`allTx` rows), 16850–16851, 16887 (current-month "Comm:" header), 16918 (Comm. column), 16920 (Status pill)
- problem: gotcha 27: "`meetsThreshold` is set on the ENTRY … never on a buy. Any test of `b.meetsThreshold` is testing a field that does not exist". The referrer modal's Transaction History still does: `allTx.push({...b,month,rate:…})` (16608) copies the buy without the entry's verdict; `belowCount=monthTxs.filter(t=>t.meetsThreshold===false)` (16851) is always 0; the Status pill (16920) never says "Below" (every unpaid buy of a below-minimum month reads "Unpaid"); the Comm. column (16918) prints `ptgcAmount×rate/100` for a buy whose entry owes 0; the current-month header "Comm:" (16887) sums the full rate regardless.
- evidence: 40 lines up (16619–16624) the same modal's pending tile correctly reads `isBelowThreshold=me.meetsThreshold===false`. Worked example (worker STEP 4, `ptgcapi-worker-v7.js:302–322`): threshold $250, an entry with buys of 40,000,000 + 60,000,000 PTGC worth $120 → `meetsThreshold=false`, `commissionPtgc=0`. Pending tile: 0. Transaction table: Comm. 800,000 + 1,200,000 PTGC, Status "Unpaid", header "Comm: 2.00M", no "Below" chip on the month row.
- why it matters: a referrer below the monthly minimum is told, in one window, that 2.00M PTGC is "Unpaid" and that nothing is pending — the a54 complaint.
- fix: at 16608 add `meetsThreshold:entry.meetsThreshold` to the pushed row; at 16887 and 16918 multiply by `(tx.meetsThreshold===false?0:1)` or print "—"; 16850–16851 then work unchanged. Display only; the worker's verdict is untouched.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: FIX NOW

#### N-5 · Worker: `thresholdType` compared case-sensitively — a "USD" setting silently becomes a PTGC threshold
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P1
- area: `handover/ptgcapi-worker-v7.js:304` `const thresholdType = settings.thresholdType || 'usd';` and 315 `} else if (thresholdType === 'usd') {` (the only source copy of the live Worker)
- problem: the page lower-cases `thresholdType` (`normSettings`, 16254) because "the API has sent 'USD'" (gotcha 27). The Worker — which writes the verdict the page respects — does not: with `'USD'` STEP 4 takes the `else` branch and compares `entry.ptgcAmount >= threshold`; with the documented 250 that is "250 PTGC" (≈ $0.0045), met by everyone, so every entry is `meetsThreshold:true` and billed.
- evidence: lines quoted; `normSettings` exists because that casing has been observed. Live settings not readable from here — a risk, not a confirmed miscount.
- fix: `String(settings.thresholdType||'usd').toLowerCase()` in STEP 4 (and in the settings-save path); redeploy; one manual check of a below-$250 month.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: FIX NOW

#### N-6 · TXNS 24H tile: the count is DexScreener's, the % change is CoinGecko's (and their buy/sell splits disagree)
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:10165–10166 (count `data.buys+data.sells`), 10142 (`txc` from `txnChange24h` = `getTransactionChange`, 4551)
- problem: the number is `fetchDex`'s sum over every DexScreener pair (30 for PTGC); the arrow is `transaction-history.json` (CoinGecko, 20 pools) last snapshot vs the one nearest 24 h ago. c24's twin for transactions.
- evidence: DexScreener sum PTGC = 238 buys + 379 sells = **617**; CoinGecko `transactions.total` = **579** (326/253) vs 594 a day earlier → tile prints "617 · 238 / 379 · ↓ 2.5 %". The buy/sell skew is opposite between sources for both tokens (UFO: DS 98/62, CG 73/87) — one labels direction differently; the tile shows DS's split with CG's trend.
- fix: derive the change from the same source as the count (DexScreener vs the previous `burn-summary` snapshot's `txns`, the fallback at 10142 already), or label the arrow "vs yesterday (CoinGecko)". Fold into c24.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

#### N-7 · `fetchDex` price fallback picks "deepest pair with a price", not `dsPricePair` (gotcha 35)
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:3905 `const pp = mp || pairs.find(p=>parseFloat(p.priceUsd)>0) || pairs[0];`, also 3809 / 3856
- problem: when the single `/pairs/` call for the main pair fails but `dsPairsFor` answers, the price, 24 h change, market cap and `rememberImpliedSupply` come from the first pair in liquidity order with a `priceUsd` — base side not checked. Gotcha 35 documents that a quote-side listing's `priceUsd` is the OTHER token's price and that fake deep pools exist; `dsPricePair` was written for exactly this and every other price path uses it.
- evidence: today's PTGC list by `liquidity.usd`: pTGC/WPLS $197K (base) first, so no live effect; the 5th–7th are `Liquid/pTGC` $61K, `Burn/pTGC` $50K, `808/pTGC` $40K — PTGC on the QUOTE side with `priceUsd` 0.00000004831 / 0.00000005149 / 0.00000004028 (~400× below PTGC). One fake pool with claimed liquidity above $197K and the outage path prints a 400× wrong price and remembers a 400× wrong implied supply in localStorage.
- fix: `const pp = mp || dsPricePair(pairs, addr) || pairs[0];` and the same at 3809/3856. No formula change.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

#### N-8 · Staleness gates — the three reads that never say they are old
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:4002–4008 (`fetchHolders` PTGC), 13709 (`holdersByPeriod.current`), 10699–10702 (`nhNet`), 4218–4222 (`getBurnPeriods`)
- problem / evidence — the constants and what the UI does past them:

  | constant | value | older → |
  |---|---|---|
  | `FreshnessBadge` | 450 s | amber dot |
  | `BURN_HISTORY_STALE_MS` / `MAX_AGE` | 2.5 h / 7 d | amber "as of" / windows "—" |
  | `VALUE_GEN_STALE_MS` / `INTERIM_MS` | 3 h / 48 h | live scan behind labelled figures / file ignored |
  | `HOURLY_AMBER_MS` (DAO Buys, NH, VS) | 2.5 h | amber line |
  | `NH_MAX_AGE`, `VS_MAX_AGE`, LP vol file | 36 h | null → "—" / dashed |
  | `ALLOC_STALE_MS` / `DEAD_MS` | 8 h / 24 h | "as of" / file ignored, scan |
  | `cgFresh` (7D/30D/90D volume) | 12 h | null → "—" |
  | `T1_AMBER` / `T1_STALE` | 12 h / 48 h | note / ranks hidden |
  | `fileLiveFresh` (PTGCbyUFO v2 file) | 24 h | fallback to window |
  | **PTGC holder count** | none | — |
  | **holder-history 7D/30D/90D + `getHolderChange`** | none | — |
  | **transaction/liquidity-history 24 h change** | none (`findSnapshotAt` checks only the PAST point vs now−24 h) | — |

  (1) The PTGC HOLDERS tile is `holder-history.json`'s last snapshot (`fetchHolders` returns it before any live call, 4002–4004; the tile prefers `holdersByPeriod.current`, 13709) with no age check — a stalled `burn-history` step freezes "18,200" unlabelled, while the change line goes "—" after 36 h. (2) `nhNet`'s `covered` tests only the START of the window (`coveredFrom>since+2h`); the END is `Date.now()`. A file 24–36 h old (accepted by `NH_MAX_AGE`) has no event in the last 24 h → net 0 → the tile prints "— 0" (10162), a claim. Worked: today's head 21:08 UTC; at 2026-10-23 22:00 (25 h later, < 36 h) the 24 h window contains none of the 473 PTGC events → "— 0". (3) `getBurnPeriods` returns `p.d7?.amount||0` — a period missing from the file is "0", not "—" (all five exist today).
- fix: (1) gate the snapshot on age (reuse `BURN_HISTORY_STALE_MS`, same writer) and label; (2) `covered = coveredFrom<=since+2h && headTs >= now−2h` (or end the window at `head.ts` and say so); (3) `??null`.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

#### N-9 · burn-summary's PTGC `totalBurned` (log sum) is 762 M above the burn-address balance the panel shows
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P2
- area: `scripts/fetch-burn-history.js:359` (Transfer logs to `0x…0369`), consumed at index.html:3761 (`mcapFromPrice` cold cache) and as the source of the 24H/7D/30D/90D windows (4216–4222); headline is `fetchBurn` = `balanceOf(0x369)` (4043)

Gap 762,185,222 = 1.62 %. Headline, Leagues "Burned" and the donut use the balance; the four window tiles and the KPI "30D BURN" use the logs. If systematic (e.g. gross amount emitted on a fee-bearing transfer to 0x369, net received), every window is ~1.6 % high; if one-off history, nothing is wrong today.
- fix: one script run (log sums per period vs balance deltas at the period boundaries), then document or switch the windows. No formula change until known.
- effort: Small (investigation) · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

#### N-12 · Live Feed: a SELL on a known pool is valued net of fee, on an unknown pool grossed up
- status: OPTIONAL IMPROVEMENT · priority: P3
- area: index.html:5398–5402 (`decode`: `amt=tokIn+tokOut`) vs 5456 (`resolveViaReceipt`: `gross=into/(1-st.fee)`)
- evidence: a 1,000,000 PTGC sell: the pool receives 950,000 → decode row USD = 950,000×p, gen = 47,500×p; receipt path: gross 1,000,000, gen 50,000×p. The pair's `amountIn` is after the transfer fee.
- fix: in `decode`, when `side==='SELL'` and `st.fee<1`, `amt=tokIn/(1-st.fee)`. · effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

#### N-13 · `getEntryCommUsd` rate precedence differs from the Worker's PTGC figure; `commissionUsd` is never written
- status: POTENTIAL RISK · priority: P3
- area: index.html:16271–16279; `ptgcapi-worker-v7.js:302, 321` (month rate) and 257 (`commissionUsd: 0`, never updated)
- problem: PTGC commission = the Worker's `commissionPtgc` (month rate at last sync); USD = page-computed `usdAmount × (e.commissionRate||fallback)` (the rate frozen into the entry at creation). Edit a month's rate after entries exist and the two disagree.
- fix: prefer the month's rate when it differs, or have the Worker write `commissionUsd`. · effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER
