#### N-15 · `getWhaleProgress` floors without the a42 epsilon
- status: OPTIONAL IMPROVEMENT · priority: P3 · area: index.html:3497 vs 8022/8627 (`Math.floor(pct+1e-9)`)
- problem: panel bar and KPI card use the plain floor; the Socials cards use the epsilon. At an exact whole percent the bar reads "99.9 %" for a tick. Cosmetic; `creatureCount` is used everywhere else (verified below).

- still open from Audit III: c25 residual — `toLocaleString()` without a locale at 5179 (Contacts 24h), 5868 (title), 14568 (`live.txCount`).
- still open from Audit III: c24 — N-6 is its TXNS twin.
- still open from Audit III: c42 — LIQ/MCAP 1 dp on the KPI card (11960), 2 dp on the tile (13907).

### Checked and found OK

- **Decimals / BigInt**: PTGC, UFO, WPLS all 18; `fetchBurn` `Number(BigInt(x))/10**18` (1e-16 relative error on 4.6e28 wei); `weiToTokens = Number(bg/1e12)/1e6` exact to 1e-6 tokens; `fetchLPFromChain` divides wei by `BigInt(10**18)` (exactly representable). No `parseInt` on wei.
- **`fetchDex`**: price = main pair first; `liq`/`vol` are sums over every pair (30 PTGC pairs: $708,466 / $19,987 — equal to `burn-summary` figures); `tokensInLP` takes `liquidity.base` or `.quote` by side — re-summed 19,522,887,157 = file, 5.86 % = tile; `mcap` = DexScreener `marketCap` 5,166,478 ≈ price × (333.33 B − 46.31 B) so the remembered implied supply is circulating; outage → change/vol/buys/sells null → "—"; `mergeDex` identity guard sound.
- **Burn**: `pct = burned/totalSupply()*100` = 46,327,984,469.8/333,333,333,333 = 13.90 % (allocation file 13.89 floor; DexScreener's implied burn agrees); `creatureCount` on all 100 exact tenths between 0.1 and 10 reconstructs the input (0 wrong); `getBurnC(13.90,'Squid')` → 🔱1 🐋3 🦈8 🐬9 🦑8. `TierInfoModal` circulating = starting − `burn.total`, null → "—" (c6 done); `LeaguesCombinedCard` needs both burns.
- **PTGC burned by UFO**: v1 base 4,588,360,141 + live `ptgcBurnedAll` 314,143,722 = 4,902,503,863 (generator's combined 4,923,344,099 includes pTGC's own 0.5 % leg — documented); windows = v2 chain + v1 rows (`c==='v1'` only, swapback double-count guard); v1 row sums 24h/7d/30d/90d = 16,407 / 1,402,597 / 2,746,727 / 8,105,223 — identical to the file's v1 `periods`; `windowReachesLaunch` false today, `ptgcBurnedAllOk` true → checkpointed lifetime path.
- **Value Generated**: one model; UFO never volume × fee unless `estimate:true` (compare card from the PTGC page, "7D EST", only when the snapshot is missing or > 48 h); a missing price → bucket null AND total null with `missing[]`; PTGC = volume × 0.05 with buckets 2/1/1/0.5/0.5. 24h delivered sample: ufoBurned 19.95 M, ptgcBurned 2.56 M, LP WPLS 9.99 M UFO + 4.46 M PLS, priced by `vgPriceMap` (null when 0).
- **"vs 7d avg"**: `(v24 − v7/7)/(v7/7)` identical at 10141, 11911, 13885, 11031; PTGC today (19,987 − 43,859)/43,859 = −54.4 % on all four.
- **Holders**: `nhView` per wallet, seconds throughout; today PTGC 24h/7d/30d net = +6 / +31 / +113, UFO 0 / +10 / +18, all `covered`; `holders24h` computed once and threaded to tile, KPI card, Holders ⓘ card, Socials card (c5 done); `holdersRate` pins avg=0 to ±100; `getHolderChange` snapshot-to-snapshot, skips same-day.
- **Token Allocation donut**: Held = supply − burned − inLP − staked − locked → slices sum to 100 % exactly; null slice → empty ring (a15); UFO three slices 100 − b − l.
- **Header tiles**: PLS RATIO = price/PLS (0.000018/0.00000543 = 3.31 ≈ DexScreener `priceNative` 3.29); X'S TO ATH = max(0.00132623, 0.00133)/price = 74.1x; X'S TO A PENNY 557x; LIQ/MCAP = Σliq/mcap = 13.71 %; PTGC IN LP 19.52 B = 5.86 %; all null-through.
- **KPI card**: 30D burn `bp.d30!=null` guard, no price → "—"; `burnUSD` only under `burnKnown`; mcap badge = price change.
- **LP Pairs**: band totals = Σ listed rows = tile ("All"); Value Gen = vol × `cfg.feeRate`, "—" when fee unknown; `useLpLiqChange` compares like with like (pairCount 30); curves from `pair-volume-7d.json` (28 × 6 h bins, ms).
- **Volume window / Humans vs Bots**: `vsView` sums human+bot; HvB 24h PTGC $20,385 vs DexScreener $19,987 vs CoinGecko 7d/7 $43,859 (the known c24 spread); `vwDays` and HvB bins UTC; `cgVolWindows` gates age (12 h), token age and monotonicity (307,013 ≤ 1,593,082 ≤ 3,771,836 ✓).
- **DAO Buys**: `price = usd/ptgc` reproduces the file to 6 sig figs on all 321 buys; lifetime avg Σusd/Σptgc = 0.0000957890 = `totals.avgPrice`; refunds subtracted in the generator (`build-dao-buys.mjs:140–144`); fresh buys priced at today's PLS and labelled; `ts` seconds → ×1000 consistently.
- **Top 100** (apart from N-1): `t1Key` = the builders' `key()` exactly; `t1Rank` = 1 + strictly above (ties share; 3,844 ties in PTGC `b`); `t1Without` removes the checked wallet's stale entry; "to break in" = `list[99] − k + 1`; "to pass #N" = `list[rank−2] − k + 1`; `mapUserInfo` words 0/5 match `build-top-stakers.mjs:91–95`; Top-100 share = 48.7 % of starting supply; stakers' `free = balance − staked` matches the 2026-10-21 on-chain check; `T1_EXCL` = the builders' set.
- **Affiliates** (apart from N-4/N-5/N-13): `lifetimeOf` sums the monthly logs only; `getEntryComm` respects an explicit 0 and `meetsThreshold===false`; ALL-TIME and month bands share helpers; month keys and printed dates are UTC; `new Date(m+'-01T12:00:00Z')` is Safari-safe; `AfPodium` ranks by `ptgcAmount`.
- **Formatters**: `fmtAbbr` 999.994→"999", 999.995→"1.00K", 999,995→"1.00M", 999,999,999→"1.00B"; `fmtPrice` 0.0001→"$0.000100", 0.00009999→"$0.0₄9999", 0.0000999996→"$0.0₃1000", 0.000002468482→"$0.0₅2468"; `fullPriceText` "$0.00000246848"; `fmtSnapshotAge` tolerates negative age; all pin `en-US`; NaN/null → "—" (Infinity would print "InfinityT" but no caller can pass it).
- **Time**: no `new Date("YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM")`; every string parse is ISO; block timestamps are seconds and every consumer converts once (`nhView`, DAO buys, `getBlockTs`, deck `ts=now−(head−block)*10000`).
- **Races / caches**: `load()` guards every setter with `loadCancelled`, resets `burn` to `{supply:0}` (consumers key on `supply>0`), aborts the tier scan; `quickRefresh` captures `t=token` and checks `tokenRef.current`; other-token caches reset on switch; `LS.TIERS`/`PTGC_PRE`/`DECK_POOLS`/`H2_PX`/`IMPLIED_SUPPLY` keyed by address or token and shape-checked, `LP_VOL` by pair — no cross-token or cross-day key found.

### Ideas

- A nightly reconciliation line in the pipeline log: Σ(Transfer→0x369) vs `balanceOf(0x369)` per token — N-9 becomes a known constant.
- `holder-ranks.json` already carries people-only balances; derive the tier counts from it (PTGC ≥ 1 %: 12, ≥ 0.1 %: 108, ≥ 0.01 %: 804 today) and the two surfaces on the panel agree by construction, with no second PulseScan walk.
- Show "#100 holds … / #101 holds …" beside the cushion so its anchor is visible (N-1 would have been obvious).
- `pipeline-status.json` has `lastSuccessAt` per step; one `dataAge(step)` helper could replace the eleven per-file thresholds in N-8's table with one rule per cadence.

## Raw sweep M

### Sweep M — Data & calculation accuracy on the sibling pages (portfolio / calculators / ledger / charts / Worker maths)

Method: full read of `tools/pf-v2/*.jsx` (the source of the portfolio body) and the classic loaders in `portfolio.html`
(fetchPortfolioData, computeUfoReflectionsWei, agg, share code), `PfRank.jsx`, the calculators' Rewards / Fresh Capital /
X-Multiplier / Moon Math blocks, the whole of `ledger.html`'s classifier, charts.html's series/merge/percent code and the
Worker's `runCommissionSync`. Every number claim below was reproduced with `node -e` against the real `data/*.json` in the
clone (holder-ranks.json, rank-index/*, rank-history/PTGC.json, charts-data.json, charts-intraday.json, treasury-recent.json,
dao-buys.json, top-stakers.json, burn-summary.json) and, where the page reads the chain, against live `rpc.pulsechain.com`
(reachable from here; DexScreener and PulseScan v2 holders were not). Line numbers are from the clone; for the portfolio I give
both the built page (`portfolio.html`) and the fragment (`tools/pf-v2/*.jsx`) — the fragments are the source of truth.
Already-known items (Audit III c43 S-4/S-7/S-8, roadmap C2 local-day grouping, E1 dead code) are not re-reported.

---

#### M-1 · UFO "Lifetime earned" scan reads to `latest` but caches `to: head` — transfers in the gap are counted twice on the next visit
- status: LIKELY ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: portfolio.html:1412–1413 (`getLogsRange`), 1450–1463 (`_getLogsChunked`), 1478–1522 (`computeUfoReflectionsWei`: `head`, `scan`, `finish`, the `grays_ufo_refl_v1:` cache)
- problem: `const toParam=(b>=headBlock)?'latest':('0x'+b.toString(16));` (1413) — the last slice of every scan (and the right half of every bisect of it) is sent as `toBlock:'latest'`, but the result is saved as `{v:1,to:head,…}` (1508) where `head` was read by `eth_blockNumber` *before* the scan (1478). The next visit scans `c.to+1 → head'` (1513) and re-reads every block after the old `head`. index.html's copy of the same helper got the `exactTo` flag for exactly this reason (HANDOVER gotcha 4, index.html:2115–2124); portfolio.html's copy never did (`grep -c exactTo portfolio.html` → 0).
- evidence: Worked example. First visit: `head = H`. The full scan from block 26,989,200 is ~9 ranges × 2 directions with bisects, 10–20 s on a public RPC, so by the time the last range runs the chain is at H+1 or H+2 (10.3 s blocks). The wallet sells 500 M UFO in block H+1 → `sent` includes it (emitted leg + fee leg → `outW` and `feeW`). `finish` computes `refl = balance(t0) − (in − out)`; the balance was read at t0 *before* the sell, so this visit already prints `true_refl + 500 M`, and the cache stores `to:H` with the sell inside `outW`. Second visit: balance is fresh (post-sell), the incremental scan starts at H+1 and finds the same sell again → `outW` holds it twice → `refl = true_refl + 500 M`, now permanently. The guard at 1507 (`if(reflWei<0n)return null` → drop cache, rescan) only fires when a *receive* is double counted and large enough to push the result negative; a double-counted *send* can never trip it, so an inflated "Lifetime earned" (and the "From reflections" slice of the ring, and the UFO "Earned by source" box) survives every refresh until the visitor clears site data. A second, transient skew comes from the queue design (gotcha 23): the balance is read in `fetchPortfolioData` but the scan runs later, one wallet at a time, so on a 20-wallet deck the last wallet's balance can be minutes older than its scan.
- why it matters: the UFO reflections figure is the page's headline reward number; for an active trader (the bot wallet in the roadmap trades every few minutes) the probability that one of its transfers lands in the 1–2 block gap on some visit is high, and the error is the full size of the transfer, not a rounding.
- fix: (a) port index.html's `exactTo` and pass it from `scan()` so the slice ends at `head` exactly; (b) read `balanceOf` at block `head` (an archive `eth_call` with `'0x'+head.toString(16)`, the page already does these for the chart) inside `computeUfoReflectionsWei` instead of using the earlier `balanceWei`, so balance and sums are from the same block. No formula changes — the same `balance − (in − out)`, just all three at one block.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-2 · Holder rank: a wallet that sold since the 2-hourly snapshot is ranked one place too low and told to "pass" its own ghost
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: portfolio.html:1964–1970 (`pfRankKey`, `pfRank`, `pfPassTxt`); fragment tools/pf-v2/PfLeagues.jsx; data/holder-ranks.json (`b` carries no addresses, only `top` has the first 110)
- problem: `pfRank` ranks the LIVE balance against a list that still contains the wallet's OWN snapshot balance: `while(lo<hi){…if(b[m]>x)lo=m+1;…} … return{r:lo+1,pass:…b[lo-1]-x+1}`. Any wallet whose balance fell since the snapshot (a sell, a send to its own second wallet, an LP add) has its old entry strictly above its live key, so it is counted as "one wallet above you" and becomes the "next wallet to pass".
- evidence: `node -e` against the real list (PTGC, 17,247 entries, read 2026-10-22 21:11 UTC): the wallet at index 49 (708,163,209 PTGC) with its exact snapshot balance → `{r:50, pass:1,043,099}` (correct: b[48] − b[49] + 1). The same wallet after selling ONE token → `{r:51, pass:2}` — rank #51 and "▲ 2 more to pass #50", where #50 is itself. Buying moves the wallet past its ghost, so the error is one-sided: it only ever shows after a sell/send, which is exactly when a holder looks. The window's hero (`TODAY #51`), the breakdown badge, the League-box line, "TO PASS #50 · 2 PTGC more" and the WHAT IT TAKES ladder (`ms=[live.r-1,…]`) all inherit it.
- why it matters: a wrong rank and a nonsensical "2 more to pass" on a page launched to the community three days ago ("thanks @lemonpopp"); it will be reported as a bug by the first holder who sells and refreshes.
- fix (no new formula, one subtraction): have `build-holder-ranks.mjs` write the block it read at (`readBlock`), and in `pfRank` read the wallet's `balanceOf` at that block (one archive `eth_call`, the chart's `pfhRpc` already does these), take `key()` of it, and if that key is strictly above the live key, subtract one from the count of entries above (`r−1`) and skip that entry when picking the "pass" target. Cheaper alternative with the same effect for the top 110: dedupe by address against `tokens[tok].top`. Either way, worked example above becomes `{r:50, pass:1,043,100}` after the 1-token sell.
- effort: Medium (Small for the top-110-only variant)
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-3 · Rank window: "today" is ranked against a different population than every other day, so "▼ N places" has a built-in step for everyone below #1,000
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: portfolio.html:2961–2962 (`full[today]=live?live.r:0`), 2966–2969 (`chg=live.r-first`, BEST/LOWEST); scripts/build-rank-history.mjs:78–79 (`isContract` = `eth_getCode` at `latest`), 113 (`index()` ties: dense, by address) vs scripts/build-holder-ranks.mjs:9–10 (PulseScan `is_contract`), portfolio.html:1966–1968 (ties shared)
- problem: the history points come from `rank-index` (chain replay, contracts = `eth_getCode`, ties broken by address → dense ranks) while today's point is `pfRank` on `holder-ranks.json` (PulseScan holders, `is_contract` flag, ties share a rank). The two lists do not contain the same wallets: PTGC index yesterday = 17,071 holders (`meta.holders[-1]`, `rank-history/PTGC.json cur` = 17,071), holder-ranks today = 17,247 — 176 more.
- evidence: `node -e`: for every one of the 17,071 wallets in `rank-history/PTGC.json cur` (yesterday's end-of-day balances), rank them with the page's own `pfRank` against today's `holder-ranks.json` and compare with their index rank for yesterday. Same balances, two methods: ranks 1–100 median +0 (max +2), 101–1,000 median +2, 1,001–5,000 median **+19** (p90 +31), 5,001–10,000 median **+64** (p90 +92, min +29), 10,000+ median **+122** (p90 +137, and −637 at the dust tie block where the index's dense ranks sit far below the shared rank). So a wallet at #8,000 that did nothing opens the window to "#8,064 ▼ 64 places vs 7 days ago", the line ends with a downward step on every chart, and `topN`/LOWEST can flip on the live point. (The top 30 agree exactly — verified — which is why the launch check at #50 looked right.)
- why it matters: the window's purpose is the delta; for roughly 16,000 of 17,000 PTGC holders the delta is dominated by the method change, not by their balance.
- fix: make the two lists one population. Simplest: have `build-rank-history.mjs` take its contract/exclusion set from the same source as `build-holder-ranks.mjs` (PulseScan `is_contract` cached in `st.contracts`, plus the same EXCLUDE), and rank ties the same way (either shared in both, or dense in both — `pfRank` is a one-line change to dense: `lo+1` → count of entries `>= x` is not needed, just stop sharing). Until then, compute yesterday's point the live way (rank yesterday's `cur` balance in today's list) so at least the last step is apples to apples.
- effort: Medium
- risk: Medium (the index rebuild is the daily step; verify on the 30/30 random wallet-days the session note used)
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-4 · "BEST EVER" and "LOWEST" tiles are the best/worst of the SELECTED PERIOD, not ever
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: portfolio.html:2966–2967, 3012–3013 (`held=pts.filter(r=>r>0)`, `best=Math.min(...held)`, label `BEST EVER`); fragment tools/pf-v2/PfRank.jsx:90–92, 136–137
- problem: `pts=full.slice(i0)` is the period slice (default `per='1Y'`, remembered in `grays_pf_rank_per`), and `best`/`worst`/`bestI` are computed on `pts`. On 7D the tile says "BEST EVER #120 · Oct 18" for a wallet that was #12 last year.
- evidence: `node -e` over the whole PTGC index: of 15,317 wallets held ≥ 400 days, **14,141** have a life-best better than their 1Y-best (the default view). Example `0x0001a369…`: life-best #307, 1Y-best #9,786, today #10,121 → the tile prints "BEST EVER #9,786".
- why it matters: "best ever" is the one brag number; it is wrong for 92 % of long-term holders on the default period.
- fix: compute `best`/`worst` for the tiles from `full` (life), keep the period's best for the chart's top gridline (that one is documented as the period's). Or rename the tiles "BEST (1Y)" — but the owner asked for "best".
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-5 · Rewards + Fresh Capital calculators: UFO "your share of rewards" is ~9 % lower than the Portfolio's for the same bag (they exclude different supply)
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: calculators.html:4031–4035 (`eligibleSupply = circSupply − totalUFOInLP`), 4486 (`eligSup=isPTGC?circSupply:eligibleSupply`), 399–402 (`tokensInLP` from DexScreener `liquidity.base/quote`); vs portfolio.html:1829–1830, 1841–1854 (`PF_UFO_EXCL`, `pfUfoEligible`: supply − burn − token contract − OA wallet 0x92DD…574D − 13 pools)
- problem: the Portfolio (round 31, measured on chain 2026-10-15) excludes the owner-excluded EOA 0x92DD…574D and the token contract from the reward-eligible supply; the calculators exclude only burn + LP. Both pages describe the same quantity ("share of every holder reward" / `userShareOfSupply` × 1 % of volume).
- evidence: live chain read 2026-10-22 (`rpc.pulsechain.com`, the page's own list): supply 999.999 B, burn 96.040 B, token contract 0.037 B, OA wallet **71.764 B**, 13 pools 103.605 B → Portfolio eligible = **728.56 B**; calculators eligible = 999.999 − 96.040 − 103.605 = **800.36 B** (DexScreener's LP figure ≈ the pools). A 1 B UFO bag: Portfolio "≈ 0.1373 % of every holder reward", Rewards calculator 0.1249 % → every projected UFO reflection figure (daily / monthly / yearly tiles, Fresh Capital's UFO side, the PTGC-vs-UFO comparison) is 9.1 % low. (The PTGC side uses `circSupply` on both pages — consistent.)
- why it matters: a holder comparing the two pages sees two different "your share"; the calculator is the one used to decide between PTGC and UFO.
- fix: this touches a financial formula — old: `share = bag / (supply − burn − LP)`; new: `share = bag / (supply − burn − LP − excludedWallets − tokenContract)`. Worked: 1 B / 800.36 B = 0.1249 % → 1 B / 728.56 B = 0.1373 %. Smallest change: `fetchBurn`-style read of the OA wallet + contract balances in calculators (two `balanceOf` calls, the addresses already exist in `PF_UFO_EXCL`), subtracted at 4034. Better: publish `elig` once (pipeline or the Portfolio's 10-min cache key) and read it on both pages.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-6 · A failed UFO `balanceOf` is a silent 0: green dot, UFO total short, breakdown "0 UFO", rank badge "not holding"
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: portfolio.html:1611–1614 (`okWord` guard covers the six PTGC words only), 1661–1666 (`ufoOk` → `ufoBalance=null`, no throw), 3790 (`result.ufo.balance+=(d.ufo.balance||0)`), tools/pf-v2/PortfolioV2.jsx:177/490 (`d.ufo.balance||0`)
- problem: a11 made every PTGC word a hard failure ("the whole wallet is marked failed … shows a red dot with a retry"), but the UFO read that follows is allowed to fail softly: `ufoBalance=null`, the wallet is stored as data (`st==='ok'`, green dot), `agg` adds 0, the breakdown prints `0` / `$0.00` for UFO and `why(0)` → "not holding" on the UFO RANK badge. The only trace is the Lifetime-earned box going "unavailable · Retry" (because `computeUfoReflectionsWei(…, null)` returns null).
- evidence: `node -e` through the exact branch: `ufoBal` of `null`, `'0x'` or `undefined` (what `rpcFetch` returns after the three endpoints fail, or a JSON-RPC error reply) → `balance null`, `agg adds 0`, `breakdown uT 0`, state ok. The PTGC batch and the UFO call are separate requests (1597 vs 1661), so an endpoint rotation / rate-limit between them produces exactly this split.
- why it matters: "never a fake 0" is the site's stated rule (gotchas 3, 20, 22); here the Total portfolio value and the UFO tile are understated by a whole wallet while every indicator says the wallet is fine.
- fix: add `ufoBal` to the `okWord` check (throw → the wallet gets the red dot and the retry like a PTGC failure), i.e. move the UFO call into the `Promise.all` at 1597 and extend the `.every(okWord)` list. Then `ufo.balance` is always a number and the `||0` fallbacks at 177/490/3790 become dead rather than lying.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-7 · GET CODE shows an empty code (and "Copied!") when any wallet name or group contains an emoji or non-Latin-1 character
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: portfolio.html:1538–1543 (`encodePortfolio`: `'GRAY-'+btoa(JSON.stringify(data))`, `catch{return null}`), 3807–3812 (`handleGetCode` → `setGeneratedCode(code||'')`), 3905 (`<code>{generatedCode}</code>`), 3827–3829 (`copyCode` copies `''` and shows "✓ Copied!")
- problem: `btoa` throws `InvalidCharacterError` on any code point above U+00FF; the catch returns null and the modal renders an empty box with a working-looking Copy button.
- evidence: `node -e`: name "Cold wallet" → `GRAY-W3siYSI6…`; name "Main 🐋" → `Invalid character` (null); group "Amélie" → encodes (Latin-1 survives `btoa`/`atob` symmetrically). Wallet names are free text (`PfEditWallet`, the LABEL field) and the owner's own copy is full of emojis, so a 🐋 or 🦈 in a name is likely.
- why it matters: the backup/restore path (HOW IT WORKS: "Use Get Code to back it up") silently produces nothing — the user pastes an empty string on the other device and gets "Invalid portfolio code".
- fix: encode UTF-8 first: `btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(json)))` and in `decodePortfolio` try `decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(x)))` then fall back to plain `atob` (old codes keep working). Also show an error in the modal when `code` is null instead of an empty box.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-8 · Recent Transactions' PRICE column and "paid / received" dollars are the hour's (or day's) file price, not "the price each trade got"
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: portfolio.html:2758–2762 (`p=pfhPriceAt(px,tok,t)`, `usd=|net|×p`), 2713 (`px=r.usd/|amt|` → identical to `p`), 2079 (`pfhPriceAt`: hourly within 3 h, else daily within 2 d); HOW IT WORKS text PortfolioV2.jsx:538 "lists every buy, sell … with the price each trade got"; column header `PRICE`, value footers "paid" / "received"
- problem: no execution price is ever read; the row's $ is `amount × nearest candle close`, and the PRICE column is that same candle divided back out.
- evidence: `charts-intraday.json` hourly PTGC has 284 points over 14 days — 46 gaps longer than 1.5 h, the longest 8 h — so for a trade inside a gap the "price it got" is the daily close from `charts-data.json` (the 2-day fallback). A 100 M PTGC sell that filled at $0.0000150 during a dump, in an hour whose close printed $0.0000180, shows "received $1,800 · $0.0₄1800 per PTGC" instead of $1,500. The day window's "Tokens out −$…", the feed's "net" per day, the Details window's $ and the value-chart tooltip inherit the same number.
- why it matters: the sentence in HOW IT WORKS is a promise the data cannot keep; for tax-time use (roadmap F2) the distinction matters.
- fix: wording first ("valued at that hour's price") — Small. Real execution price = the counter-leg in the same tx (`pftDetail` already fetches the tx's token transfers for Sell/Buy rows): WPLS/DAI amount ÷ token amount × that token's price. Medium, and only for rows on screen.
- effort: Small (wording) / Medium (real price)
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW (wording) · LATER (price)

#### M-9 · The 24H pills silently switch between two different quantities depending on whether the archive read succeeded
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P2
- area: portfolio.html:3250 (`tChg`), 3276–3277 (`tokChg`, `pChg`, `uChg`), 2160–2192 (`usePf24h` → `null` on any failure)
- problem: with `h24` the pill is the change of the HOLDINGS' value (balance 24 h ago × price then → now, trades included). When `usePf24h` throws (one batch failure, one wallet unread, a price gap > 3 h at that minute) the same pill shows DexScreener's 24 h PRICE change applied to today's balance (`prev=v/(1+c/100)`), with no marker.
- evidence: worked example — a wallet held 1 B PTGC yesterday and bought 1 B more today at ≈ $18,000. Archive path: (36,000 − 18,000)/18,000 → "▲ 100.00% 24H". Fallback path: DexScreener h24 = −1.24 % (burn-summary.json today) → "▼ 1.24% 24H". Same page, same wallet, different RPC luck.
- why it matters: a holder who just bought reads a red pill; a holder who sold reads green.
- fix: when `h24` is null print "—" (the pill component already has a `na` state) or tag the fallback ("price only"). One-line change at 3250/3276.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-10 · A `carried` holder list (one token's PulseScan read failed) is never detected as stale — the page only checks the file's `generatedAt`
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P2
- area: portfolio.html:1952–1960 (`PF_RANKS_STALE=48 h`, `stale=…Date.parse(d.generatedAt)`), 3565 ("list read {pfAgo(generatedAt)}"); scripts/build-holder-ranks.mjs:110–115 (`out.tokens[sym] = { ...old, carried: true }` with the OLD `readAt`, then `generatedAt = now`)
- problem: the builder keeps the previous list for a token whose read failed (`::warning::holder-ranks UFO failed … keeping the previous list`) and stamps a fresh `generatedAt`. The page reads neither `readAt` nor `carried` (`grep -c "readAt\|carried" portfolio.html` → 0), so a UFO list from last week is ranked as live, the footer says "list read 20 min ago", and the badge footer promises "refreshed every 2 hours".
- evidence: today's file has per-token `readAt` (PTGC 21:11:58, UFO 21:13:01) — the field exists and is ignored.
- why it matters: PulseScan's holders endpoint is the one that already failed often enough for the builder to need the carry path; the first multi-day outage makes every UFO rank on the site silently stale while the PTGC half looks fresh.
- fix: in `usePfRanks` compute staleness per token from `tokens[tok].readAt` (fallback `generatedAt`), mark `carried` tokens, and let `pfRank` return null for a stale token only; print "read {readAt}" per token in the footer.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-11 · Charts 3M / 6M / 1Y: UFO's "% change from start" starts 2026-07-10 while every other token starts at the range start — the share card ranks them as if comparable
- status: LIKELY ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: charts.html:842–851 (`minTs: Date.UTC(2026,6,10)` for UFO), 1321–1333 (`buildDatasets`: "each token uses its own full data range independently", `p0=raw[0].price`), 1398–1414 (`getShareItems`: `pct=(pN−p0)/p0`, sorted), 2474–2498 (share bars scaled to `maxAbs`)
- problem: the normalisation baseline is each token's first available point, not the range start; UFO's history (new contract, floored at Jul 10) is 101–104 days on any range longer than that, and nothing on screen, in the legend or on the share card says so (`_limited` is only set on the fetch-fallback path, 1652).
- evidence: `node -e` on `charts-data.json` (1Y): PTGC −91.7 % over 367 days from 2025-10-21; UFO −33.8 % over 101 days from 2026-07-10; PTGC over the same 101 days is −58.2 %. The 1Y share card prints "UFO −33.8 % / PTGC −91.7 %" as a ranking.
- why it matters: it reads as "UFO held up 3× better over the year" — a comparison the data cannot support; the chart title says "Percentage Change from Start".
- fix: when a token's first point is later than the range start, either rebase every token to the latest common start (one line: `p0` at `max(firstTs)`), or flag the series (dashed line + "since Jul 10" in the legend and on the share card) using the existing `_limited`/`limited` plumbing.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-12 · Worker: a month whose `commissionRate` is set to 0 is billed at 2 %, and the entry's frozen `commissionRate` can disagree with the rate STEP 4 actually used
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P2
- area: handover/ptgcapi-worker-v7.js:256 (`commissionRate: settings.commissionRate || 2`), 302 (`const rate = settings.commissionRate || 2`), 321 (`commissionPtgc = ptgcAmount × rate/100`); index.html:16271–16279 (`getEntryCommUsd` uses `e.commissionRate||fallbackRate`), 16318/16349 (`rate=month.settings?.commissionRate||2`)
- problem: `||2` treats an explicit `0` (a paused promo month) as "unset" — the Worker computes and the page prints 2 % commissions for a 0 % month. Separately, `entry.commissionRate` is written once when the entry is created (256) but `commissionPtgc` is recomputed every sync with the month's *current* `settings.commissionRate` (302/321); the page's USD commission uses the frozen entry rate, its PTGC commission the Worker's. Change a month's rate after its first entry exists and the two columns use different rates.
- evidence: code trace; `0||2 === 2` in JS. gotcha 27 already fixed the identical `||` trap for `commissionPtgc` ("never `||` it") — the rate still has it in four places. Also noted: `txMonth = tx.timestamp.slice(0,7)` (235) takes the month from the referral API's string, which the 2026-09-29 health check shows as `2026-09-29 04:23:55` (no zone) — the month cut is the API server's clock, while the page headings say every date is UTC.
- why it matters: money decisions (the PAID OUT / Commissions tiles) depend on these; a 0 % month is the natural way to pause the program.
- fix: `const rate = Number.isFinite(settings.commissionRate) ? settings.commissionRate : 2` in all four places; in STEP 4 also write `entry.commissionRate = rate` so the entry carries the rate it was computed with. Confirm the API timestamp zone with goptgc (one question) and, if it is not UTC, append the zone before `.slice(0,7)`.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low (Worker redeploy; v8 is already planned)
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### M-13 · Ledger counts token→PTGC swaps as "PTGC Buys"; the DAO Buys chart does not — July 2026 disagrees (6 / 183.0 M vs 5 / 168.1 M)
- status: POTENTIAL RISK

July: Ledger 6 buys / 183,010,483 PTGC, chart 5 / 168,142,533; the extra row is `0x43a877df…` (2026-07-05, wallet 2, 5.56 B "Burn" token → 14.87 M PTGC via a multicall router).

- fix: either note on the Ledger tile "(PLS-paid buys: N)" or have the chart count token-paid swaps priced at the PTGC candle. Owner's call; one line either way.

- status: POTENTIAL RISK

With the pipeline stopped for three days the 7D chart shows a 3-day hole, `tracked` ("of N held") undercounts, and the end step from the last real point to today's live point crosses the hole.

- fix: push `null`/`-1` for unknown days and draw them as a dotted bridge, and print "history to {date}" in amber when `h.days-1 < today-1` (the footer already has the date).
