#### P-1 · The four CDN `<script>` tags block the parser, so the c14 static shell cannot paint until ~760 KB gz of scripts has arrived — `defer` moves first paint from 9.0 s to 0.8 s on a 1.6 Mbps link
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: index.html:63–69 (`<script src="https://unpkg.com/react@18.3.1/…"`, react-dom, `@babel/standalone`, `chart.js`), portfolio.html:13–15, calculators.html:13–16, ledger.html:12–14
- problem: all four tags are plain `<script src>` in `<head>` (no `defer`/`async`). The static shell added for c14 sits in `<body>` at index.html:1553, so the HTML parser reaches it only after react (4 KB gz) + react-dom (43 KB) + babel (636 KB) + chart.js (69 KB) = 752 KB gz have downloaded and executed. The shell was meant to be the thing a slow or blocked visitor sees; today it appears only once the heavy scripts are in.
- evidence: `tools/harness/run.js` copy with a CDP network throttle and gzip on the local server (`/home/claude/audit/work/P/run-p4.js`, probe `paint-probe.js`), `#/ptgc` at 390×844:
  - 1.6 Mbps / 150 ms RTT: as shipped `first-contentful-paint` **9,032 ms**, DOMContentLoaded 14,188 ms; with `defer` on the four tags **788 ms**, DCL 13,233 ms (the page mounted, same text, no page errors).
  - 6 Mbps / 80 ms: as shipped FCP **2,672 ms**, DCL 6,629 ms; with `defer` FCP **324 ms**, DCL 6,748 ms.
  - `DEFER=1` runs of portfolio.html, calculators.html and ledger.html: `errors: none`, pages mounted (Babel standalone's `transformScriptTags` is registered on `DOMContentLoaded`; deferred scripts execute before that event, in document order, so react → react-dom → babel → chart ordering is preserved and `h2-header.jsx` is still fetched by Babel itself).
- why it matters: on a phone on a weak connection the visitor stares at a black `body{background:#0a0a0a}` for the whole script download before even the "THE GRAYS DASHBOARD … LOADING…" shell shows; on the sibling pages (empty `#root`) it is black until React mounts. The c14 work is invisible exactly when it is needed.
- fix: add `defer` to the four CDN tags on index.html and to the three/four tags on portfolio, calculators and ledger (`<script defer src="…" integrity="…" crossorigin="anonymous">`). Leave charts.html alone (its plain scripts call `Chart` during parsing). The inline head script (index.html:36–46) is unaffected. Verify with the normal harness walk (the harness regexes at run.js:14–20 strip attributes on rewrite — add `defer` to their replacements or the harness will not exercise it).
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### P-2 · The committed `tw.css` is stale: it predates the 2026-10-22 Affiliates redo, so `text-purple-500/40` (index.html:17285) has no rule live and 63 dead selectors ship with it
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: tw.css (committed, 83,975 B) vs `npm run css` output (79,711 B); index.html:17285; tools/harness/run.js:15 (the harness serves `tw.out.css`, never the committed file)
- problem: gotcha 8 says "a class with no rule in tw.css does nothing live". A fresh build from the five pages + h2-header.jsx is byte-identical to the harness's `tw.out.css` (105,159 B both — `cmp` clean) but NOT to the committed minified `tw.css`: 1,476 selectors committed vs 1,416 fresh; 63 only in the committed file (`.text-pink-400`, `.bg-purple-500/15`, `.border-pink-500/20`, `.from-gray-900`, `.divide-purple-500/10`, … — the old Affiliates page's palette), 3 only in the fresh build: `.text-purple-500\/40` (real, used at index.html:17285 `<CopyBtn text={tx.txHash} className="text-purple-500/40 hover:text-purple-300"/>`), `.container` and `.\!shrink` (scanner false positives from prose/JS). So the receipts' copy icon on the new Affiliates page has no colour rule live (it inherits `.tx`'s colour; `hover:text-purple-300` does exist), and `npm run css` was last run before the page edit — the second half of the script (`--minify -o ../../tw.css`) did not land in the commit.
- evidence: `npx tailwindcss -c tailwind.config.js -i tw.in.css -o /home/claude/audit/work/P/tw.fresh.css --minify` → 79,711 B; selector diff script in this sweep's shell log; `grep -c 'text-purple-500\\/40' tw.css` → 0, fresh → 1. The harness cannot show this because run.js:15 swaps `tw.css?v=2` for its own fresh `tw.out.css`.
- why it matters: one visible miss today (small), but the mechanism is the one the HANDOVER warns about: any class added since the last `npm run css` is silently unstyled on the live site while every harness screenshot looks right. 4 KB of dead CSS is the harmless half.
- fix: run `npm run css`, commit `tw.css`, bump `tw.css?v=2` → `?v=3` on the five pages (Pages caches 10 min). Consider a `RUNTIME: harness uses committed tw.css` switch (`TW=committed node run.js …`) so a pre-deploy run can be made with the file that will actually ship, and a one-line check in the session checklist: `npm run css && git diff --stat tw.css` must be empty before pushing a page edit.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### P-3 · Inputs with font-size < 16 px make iOS Safari zoom the page on focus — Portfolio's "paste a wallet" box, its search, the Top 100 search and the new-group field
- status: LIKELY ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: portfolio.html:82–83 (`.pf2-add input{…font-size:15.5px}` / `.pf2-add input.ad{…font-size:14px}`), :91 (`.pf2-newg input{…font-size:15px}`), :104 (`.pf2-srch input{…font-size:14px}`), :210 (`.pf2-mmf input{…font-size:13.5px}`); index.html:1085 (`@container (max-width:560px){… .t1inp input{font-size:15px}}`, Top 100 search — the same block is tools/top100/t1.css); index.html:10233 (operator password `text-sm`)
- problem: iOS Safari auto-zooms the viewport when a text input with computed font-size below 16 px receives focus, and does not zoom back out on blur. The viewport meta is `width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0` (correct — do not add `maximum-scale=1`, that breaks pinch-zoom for everyone). Portfolio's main address field is 14 px monospace; the Top 100 window's search is 15 px at phone widths.
- evidence: the rules quoted above; `.pf2-ew .in{…font-size:16px}` (portfolio.html:157) shows the edit-wallet field was already sized to avoid this. Behaviour not reproducible in headless Chromium — it is WebKit's documented auto-zoom (threshold 16 px); verify on an iPhone by tapping the ADD WALLET address box.
- why it matters: the first thing a phone user does on the Portfolio is paste an address; the page jumps to ~115 % zoom and stays there, clipping the right edge of the wallet cards until they pinch out. Same on the Top 100 window's CHECK box.
- fix: `font-size:16px` for those inputs at `max-width:640px` (five declarations in portfolio.html + one line in tools/top100/t1.css, then `python3 tools/top100.py` / `tools/pf-v2.py` to re-splice). Keep the desktop sizes.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### P-4 · The three Logos windows still use the full-resolution download files as thumbnails: 5.6 MB into ~200 px cells, no `loading="lazy"` — the unfinished half of Audit III c15/P-9
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:15446–15452 (PTGC list), 15455–15456 (`<img src={`logos/ptgc/${logo.file}`} … className="max-w-full max-h-full object-contain"/>`), UFO and Combined lists below (15511 draws the root `07_Combined_PTGC_UFO_Transparent.png` at `h-12` scaled 1.5)
- problem: c15 is marked done (backdrops → WebP, `logo-512.webp` for `TOKENS.*.logo` — verified: every `TOKENS.*.logo` / sibling config now points at `logos/*/logo-512.webp`, 34,928 and 39,598 B, and nothing draws the 2000×2000 originals except these windows). But the Logos windows' `<img>`s still point at the originals: logos/ptgc PNGs 7 files ≈ 1.55 MB, logos/ufo 6 files 786 KB, logos/combined 7 files 3.66 MB including `TheGraysXBnr.png` 2,326,952 B and `04_Two_logos_pattern_bg.jpg` 746,379 B. Each 2000×2000 PNG decodes to 16 MB; the Combined window opens 7 such decodes at once. No `loading="lazy"`, no thumbs folder (`ls logos/*/thumbs` → none).
- evidence: `ls -l logos/ptgc logos/ufo logos/combined`; `grep -c 'loading="lazy"' index.html` → 1 (the hub icons only).
- why it matters: a phone user who taps "Logos" to grab a sticker downloads 1.5–3.7 MB and the tab can get killed by memory pressure on a 3 GB iPhone while decoding seven 16 MB bitmaps; the grid also pops in one tile at a time.
- fix: generate `logos/<token>/thumbs/<file>.webp` at 320 px (one `cwebp -resize 320 0 -q 80` loop; add the folder to nothing — `logos/` is already in the `_site` allow-list), use them for the `<img>`, keep `href`+`download` on the originals, add `loading="lazy" decoding="async"`.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### P-5 · Chart.js is render-blocking on index.html and calculators.html although nothing draws a chart at mount; html2canvas is eager on charts.html
- status: OPTIONAL IMPROVEMENT
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:69 (`chart.umd.js`, 205,125 B / 69,246 gz), calculators.html:16; charts.html:14 (`html2canvas.min.js`, 198,689 B / 45,612 gz)
- problem: on index.html `new window.Chart(` appears only in the DAO Buys window (7340, 7367, 7438) and the Affiliates referrer window (15818 `new Chart(ctx,…)` — the one site without a `window.Chart` guard); on calculators the six charts live in tab panels. Every visit pays 69 KB gz on the critical path for a window most visitors never open. charts.html loads html2canvas (45 KB gz) at parse time for the "Share Chart" download only (2379), while index.html already has a lazy, SRI-pinned loader for the same file (`loadHtml2Canvas`, 6515–6529).
- evidence: harness boots: `canvases: 0` on `#/ptgc`, `#/ufo`, Home and calculators; the lines quoted.
- why it matters: 69 KB gz ≈ 0.4 s at 1.6 Mbps on every dashboard visit; with P-1's `defer` it stops blocking paint but still competes with data fetches at boot.
- fix: cheapest: `defer` (P-1) already removes it from the paint path. Better: drop the tag and reuse the `loadHtml2Canvas` pattern for Chart.js (`loadChartJs()` awaited in the two windows' effects; guard 15818 with it); on charts.html lazy-load html2canvas inside the share handler with the same SRI constant.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### P-6 · The four sibling pages have no `preconnect` hints and an empty `#root` — a blocked-CDN or slow visitor gets a black page with nothing to read
- status: OPTIONAL IMPROVEMENT
- priority: P3
- area: portfolio.html:1071, calculators.html:83, ledger.html:25 (`<div id="root"></div>`); heads of portfolio/calculators/charts/ledger (`grep -c preconnect` → 0, index.html has 5 at :48–52); no `<noscript>` on any page
- problem: index.html got the c14 shell + preconnects; the siblings did not. They link unpkg, jsdelivr, fonts.googleapis and fonts.gstatic with no `preconnect`, so fonts.gstatic's TLS handshake starts only after the fonts CSS arrives, and until Babel finishes (1.6–1.8 s on this CPU, more on a phone) the body is empty.
- evidence: the grep counts; harness `dom` at mount for portfolio = 172 nodes, all React-rendered.
- why it matters: same class of problem c14 fixed on index — a crawler, a corporate network that blocks unpkg, or a slow link sees an empty page; a shared Portfolio/Calculators link previews as nothing (c38).
- fix: copy index.html:48–51 (four `preconnect` lines) into the four heads; put a one-paragraph static shell inside each `#root` (title + one line + links back to the dashboard) like index.html:1553–1560 — React's `render` replaces it.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### P-7 · `apple-touch-icon` as a `data:` URI is ignored by iOS (home-screen icon becomes a screenshot); sitemap lacks ledger.html; ledger ships a different favicon
- status: LIKELY ISSUE
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:34 (`<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="data:image/png;base64,…">`, 4,352 base64 chars), portfolio.html:9, calculators.html:9, charts.html:8, ledger.html:8 (a different 56×64 icon, 6,872 chars); sitemap.xml (4 `<url>`s, no `<lastmod>`); no `/apple-touch-icon.png` in the repo root (`cp *.png` in deploy.yml:55 would publish one if it existed)
- problem: iOS Safari resolves `apple-touch-icon` only from an http(s) URL; `data:` hrefs are not supported (long-standing WebKit behaviour — Apple's "Configuring Web Applications" guide specifies a file). When the link is unusable iOS requests `/apple-touch-icon-precomposed.png` and `/apple-touch-icon.png` from the site root; both 404 on GitHub Pages (the default 404 HTML — c38's missing `404.html` again), so "Add to Home Screen" gets a page thumbnail instead of the alien. The favicon `data:` URI works in Chrome/Firefox; desktop Safari's support for data-URI favicons is version-dependent — a real file removes both doubts. sitemap.xml lists 4 pages and omits `ledger.html` (linked from the DAO panel, index.html:14776); `changefreq` without `lastmod` is ignored by Google.
- evidence: lines quoted; `ls 404.html apple-touch-icon.png` → none; cannot be exercised from this sandbox — confirm on an iPhone (Share → Add to Home Screen).
- why it matters: the home-screen icon is the one "app-like" touch the dashboard offers phone users; the omitted ledger page is uncrawled.
- fix: write the same 64×64 PNG (and a 180×180) to `/apple-touch-icon.png` + `/favicon.png`, point all five pages at them (`<link rel="icon" href="favicon.png">`, `<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="apple-touch-icon.png">`), drop the 8.7 KB of inline base64 per page; add `ledger.html` to the sitemap. (c38's `404.html`, descriptions, canonicals and `document.title` are still open — one line below.)
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### P-8 · Container queries carry the phone layout of the KPI card and the Top 100 window with no media-query fallback — iOS 15 / Safari ≤ 15.6 renders the desktop grid inside a 390 px window
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:631 (`.kp{container-type:inline-size;…}`) + 718–760 (`@container (max-width:540px){ .kp .kp-pr{grid-template-columns:1fr auto} … }`); index.html:947 + 1078–1100 (`.t1m{container-type:inline-size}` / `@container (max-width:560px){ .t1th,.t1r{grid-template-columns:34px minmax(0,1fr) 30px 108px} … }`) — source tools/top100/t1.css:4,135
- problem: `@container` needs Safari 16.0 (Sept 2022). On older WebKit the whole block is dropped: the Top 100 list keeps its desktop `.t1th,.t1r` column template and 2-row pods inside a `max-width:980px; overflow:hidden` modal that is 390 px wide — the balance column is clipped, the header stays 300 px tall; the KPI card keeps its two-column price grid at 36 px+ sizes. Both windows are `overflow:hidden`, so the cut-off content is simply gone, not scrollable. There is no `@supports` or `@media` twin.
- evidence: the rules quoted; `grep -c "@supports (container" index.html` → 0; iOS 15 is the floor the brief asks about (iPhone 6s/7 are stuck there). Everything else in the page is ES2020 + CSS that Safari 14–15 understands (see Checked OK), so these two windows would be the only broken surfaces on such a phone.
- why it matters: an iOS 15 holder opening the Top 100 window to check their rank sees the list with the balance column cut off.
- fix: duplicate the two `@container (max-width:…)` blocks under `@media (max-width:600px)` (the containers are full-width on phones, so the rules coincide), or wrap the copies in `@supports not (container-type:inline-size)`. ~2 KB of CSS, no logic.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### P-9 · Small WebKit degradations (cosmetic, no fallback needed except where noted): unprefixed `backdrop-filter`, `h-[100dvh]` without a `vh` fallback, `color-mix` selected state, `@property`, `:has`
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:107 (`.modal-overlay{backdrop-filter:blur(8px)}`), :111 (`.buy-btn{…backdrop-filter:blur(12px)}`), calculators.html:31, charts.html:27, :67, :154 — six rules with no `-webkit-backdrop-filter` (index has the prefix in 3 other rules, Tailwind's `backdrop-blur-*` emits both); index.html:7451, 10963, 11041, 11155, 11551 (`h-[100dvh] sm:h-auto …` on the phone modal panels; tw.css has only `.h-\[100dvh\]{height:100dvh}`); charts.html:47 (`.token-btn.selected{border-color:color-mix(…);background:color-mix(…)}`); index.html:5772 (`@property --p`); index.html:1308–1326 and portfolio.html:575, 806 (`:has(`)
- problem / floors: unprefixed `backdrop-filter` needs Safari 18 (Sept 2024) — on iOS 15–17 the modal overlay is the plain `bg-black/80`, no blur; `100dvh` needs iOS 15.4 — on 15.0–15.3 the five phone panels get `height:auto` (they still have `overflow-y-auto` and the `Modal` overlay is fixed, so content past the fold is unreachable on those four releases); `color-mix` needs 16.2 — on older the selected token button on charts.html keeps only its white label and dot (still distinguishable); `@property` 16.4 — the Live Feed scanline fill does not animate; `:has` 15.4 — the `.pf2-fr .t2:not(:has(.nt)){display:none}` empty line shows and the blue panel tiles lose 96 px padding.
- evidence: grep counts in the sweep log (`backdrop-filter` 9 vs `-webkit-backdrop-filter` 3 in index.html; `dvh` 13 in index.html, 6 rules in tw.css); every `mask-image`/`mask:` in index.html, portfolio.html and h2.css has its `-webkit-` twin (6/6, 2/2, 8/8) — masks are fine.
- why it matters: none of it blanks a page; the `dvh` case hides content but only on iOS 15.0–15.3 (five months of releases in 2021–22).
- fix: add `-webkit-backdrop-filter` beside the six unprefixed rules (one line each); for the panels add an inline rule `.mdl-phone{height:100vh;height:100dvh}` and put the class on the five panels (`h-screen` has no rule in tw.css today — it is unused, so do not rely on it without `npm run css`). Leave `color-mix`, `@property`, `:has` as they are.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### P-10 · Two components are still declared inside render bodies (gotcha 14): `Side` in `HvbShareCard` and `Fold` in the Humans-vs-Bots window
- status: OPTIONAL IMPROVEMENT
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:10840 (`const Side=({cls,tag,pct,vol,n,gen,note,art})=>(` inside `HvbShareCard`, used at 10864/10866 with an `<img className="art" src={art}>`), index.html:11144 (`const Fold=({open,onToggle,title,count,color,children})=>(` inside the Humans vs Bots modal, used at 11244/11259/11274)
- problem: both are new function identities every render, so React unmounts and remounts their subtrees on every parent render. `Fold` renders `{open&&children}` — toggling one fold (`setShowPools`) re-creates the DOM of the other open folds (the "By pool" list, the bot-contract list) and re-requests their `<img>`s; `Side` remounts both share-card halves whenever the card's props change.
- evidence: `grep -n -E "^ {6,}const [A-Z]\w+\s*=\s*\(" index.html` → exactly these two (the rest of the page was hoisted in u4). The parent renders only on user toggles and the file fetch, so the cost is a DOM rebuild per click, not a loop.
- why it matters: a stutter when toggling folds on a phone and lost scroll position inside a fold; cheap to remove and it keeps the u4 rule intact.
- fix: move both to module scope next to their parents; `Fold` needs no closure (all inputs are props), `Side` reads nothing from the parent either.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### P-11 · Box-shadow keyframe animations on the Home BUY/SELL buttons repaint every frame while the page is idle
- status: OPTIONAL IMPROVEMENT
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:244 (`.hc-buy{…animation:hcGlow 3s ease-in-out infinite}`) + 251 (`@keyframes hcGlow{…box-shadow:…}`); index.html:111–112 (`.buy-btn{…animation:buyGlow 3s … infinite}` + `@keyframes buyGlow{…box-shadow:…}`, used by the token-card BUY buttons at 6037/14111 and on calculators/charts)
- problem: `box-shadow` is not compositor-animatable, so each of the two Home buttons (and the `::after` sweep on the same element) forces a paint of its layer ~60×/s for as long as the page is open. The header `.h2-buy` shimmer (h2.css:98) animates `transform` on a pseudo-element — that one is composited and cheap.
- evidence: harness Home run: `document.getAnimations()` → 4 infinite: `hcGlow@button.hc-buy` ×2 and `hcSweep@button.hc-buy::after` ×2, all on-screen. Dashboard runs: 4–5 infinite animations, only the `.h2-buy::after` shimmer and sparkline `pulse` on-screen; `animate-fire` (transform) is off-screen and `prefers-reduced-motion` guards exist for all of them (index.html:125–129, 268; h2.css:172; portfolio 27/353/659; calculators 37; charts 30; t1 1101).
- why it matters: a few percent of a phone core while Home is open; battery, not correctness.
- fix: animate `opacity` of a pseudo-element that carries the glow (`.hc-buy::before{box-shadow:…;opacity:0}` + keyframes on `opacity`), or `filter:drop-shadow` — or simply drop `hcGlow` and keep the sweep.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### Still open from Audit III (one line each, verified still true)
- still open from Audit III: c16 — `lv-snapshots.json` is now 402,422 B / 59,709 gz (921 rows, +60 in 15 days) and `new-holders.json` 171,432 / 67,974 gz, both re-read on the dashboard interval; `transaction-history.json` 338,861 B (853 rows, 12.6 rows/day, 397 B/row) is capped at 100 days (c4) so it tops out ≈ 950 KB raw / ~160 KB gz at a true hourly beat — all for 30-day sparklines.
- still open from Audit III: c38 — portfolio/calculators/charts/ledger have no description/canonical/og/twitter/theme-color, `document.title` is static on every route (`grep document.title *.html` → 0), no `404.html`, robots allows `/data/`.
- still open from Audit III: c36 — charts.html loads 4 families / 21 weights (`Orbitron:400;500;600;700;800;900`, `JetBrains Mono` ×5, `Outfit` ×5, `Rajdhani` ×5) vs 8 weights on the other pages.

### Checked and found OK
- Dependencies: all five pages pin the same versions (react 18.3.1, react-dom 18.3.1, @babel/standalone 7.26.4, chart.js 4.4.1; charts adds chartjs-adapter-date-fns 3.0.0 and html2canvas 1.4.1 from cdnjs; index lazy-loads the same html2canvas with the same hash). Every `integrity` hash was recomputed from the npm tarball files in `tools/harness/node_modules` and matches (react/react-dom/babel on 4 pages, chart on 3, adapter 1, html2canvas 2). No duplicate chart or font libraries beyond c36. No advisory applies: CVE-2025-27789 (@babel/helpers regexp-named-groups transform) needs the `env` preset, which `data-presets="react"` does not load; the 2025 React advisories are react-server-dom-* (not the 18.3.1 UMD client build); chart.js 4.4.1 and html2canvas 1.4.1 have no published advisory. No upgrade is needed.
- Babel: every page's `text/babel` tag carries `data-presets="react"` (Audit III P-1 done); the shipped JS is ES2020 on all six sources (acorn: `?.`/`??` and BigInt literals are the newest syntax) → Safari 14 / iOS 14 floor, consistent across pages. No lookbehind regex, `.at()`, `structuredClone`, `Object.hasOwn`, `replaceAll`, `findLast`, `toSorted`, `requestIdleCallback`, `URL.canParse`, `AbortSignal.timeout`, `Intl.NumberFormat` compact/signDisplay anywhere. The plain head script (index.html:36–46) is ES5. `crypto.subtle` is guarded with a JS SHA-256 fallback (10183–10186); `navigator.share` is feature-checked (6563); `navigator.clipboard` writes are inside try/catch or guarded; `ResizeObserver`/`IntersectionObserver` are feature-checked where they matter (6770, 10921, portfolio 2792) and every one is disconnected in the effect cleanup; all `Date.parse` inputs are ISO strings (pipeline `generatedAt`/`lastUpdated`, Blockscout `timestamp`) — no non-ISO date strings reach Safari.
- localStorage: all 10 index.html writes, 13 portfolio, 6 calculators, 12 charts writes are inside try/catch (or `lsSet`/`pfLsSet`), so Safari private mode cannot throw out of a handler; `lsSweep` and the charts cache fall back to sessionStorage.
- Timers/listeners: index.html 9 `setInterval` / 10 `clearInterval` (the extra clear is the deck spark trail's self-clear), every refresh interval guarded by `document.hidden` (6164, 6378, 9882, 10669, 13821); add/removeEventListener balanced per event type (resize 7/7, scroll 4/4, keydown 3/3, visibilitychange 3/3, hashchange 1/1, pointerdown 1/1); h2-header.jsx 3/3. Chart.js: every `new Chart`/`new window.Chart` (index 4, calculators 6, charts 2) destroys the previous instance first and in the effect cleanup; `canvases: 0` at mount on every route.
- Lists: Top 100 (101 rows) is `useMemo`'d (t1.jsx:112, 124, 195) with row components at module scope; the ledger renders one month (≤ 95 rows in treasury-recent.json); the affiliates registry is small. No virtualisation needed. Only two nested components remain (P-10). Dashboard DOM at mount 1,581 nodes (phone and desktop), Home 148, portfolio 172, calculators 351, charts 511, ledger 91.
- Caching: c12 done — index.html uses `DATA_FETCH={cache:'no-cache'}` (2015) for the history files, hourly keys for dao-buys/token-allocation, the 5-min bucket for value-generated; ledger `DATA_BUST=''` + `{cache:'no-cache'}`; portfolio/calculators `{cache:'no-cache'}`; charts hourly keys. The three header-art `<link rel="preload" as="image">`s (c14) ARE matched by the later `<img>`/CSS consumers: a harness run without route interception shows one server hit per file (the double hit seen with interception on is Playwright disabling the cache for routed requests — a harness artefact worth knowing when reading byte counts).
- Images: every `TOKENS.*.logo` and sibling config reads `logos/*/logo-512.webp`; nothing outside the Logos windows draws the 2000×2000 originals (harness `bigDecode` empty on every route once the DexScreener stub's 2000 px logo is excluded). Backdrops are WebP (c15); the remaining JPGs in use are dash-bg 198 KB (2168×350, drawn 1440×233 desktop / 2911×470 phone), tabs-bg 131 KB, bg-burn 113 KB, rh-heart-stage 58 KB, cosmic-ground 46 KB. alien-head.webp (1024×910) is drawn 340×302 desktop / 250×222 phone — right for 2–3× screens. `hub-sky.webp` is one URL in two `<img>`s. Fonts: `display=swap` on all five, 8 weights on four pages, preconnect to googleapis+gstatic on index.
- Animations: 31 `animation:` in index.html (14 infinite) with 7 `prefers-reduced-motion` blocks; portfolio 10/4, calculators 5/1, charts 9/1, h2.css 5/1, pf2.css 8/3, t1.css 2/1 — c29 done. Live Feed motes/dashes/sweep run only while the deck window is mounted.
- Compatibility (CSS): masks all `-webkit-` prefixed; `background-clip:text` always with `-webkit-background-clip:text` + `-webkit-text-fill-color`; `subgrid` wrapped in `@supports` (portfolio 231); `inset`, flex `gap`, `aspect-ratio`, `inert` (7557, with `aria-hidden` fallback), `overscroll-behavior`, `scrollbar-gutter`, `:focus-visible` all degrade silently. `scrollbar-width`/`::-webkit-scrollbar` pairs present. No `100vh` modal sizing (all phone panels use `dvh` — P-9 for the 15.0–15.3 edge). `touch-action` only where needed (portfolio).
- SEO on index.html: title, description, canonical, og:title/description/type/site_name/url/image (+width/height/alt), twitter:card/title/description/image, theme-color, `lang="en"` on all five pages, valid `WebSite` JSON-LD (parsed), `share-preview.png` is 1200×630 as declared, robots.txt + sitemap.xml are well-formed XML, the c14 static shell gives crawlers real text and links.

### Ideas (grounded in what exists)
- `defer` (P-1) plus a static shell in the four sibling `#root`s turns the c14 idea into a site-wide "something on screen in < 1 s" — the shell text can be the page's own `<title>` line and the links already in index.html:1558.
- The harness already rewrites the CDN tags (run.js:14–20); teach the rewrite to keep `defer`, add a `TW=committed` switch that serves `../../tw.css` instead of `tw.out.css`, and a `THROTTLE=<kbps>` CDP option (the copy in `/home/claude/audit/work/P/run-p4.js` shows the 10 lines) — then P-1/P-2 class regressions show up in the normal walk.
- `probes/` could gain the two probes from this sweep (`perf-probe.js`: DOM count, `<img>` count, infinite animations and whether on-screen, big decodes, data files; `lt-probe.js`: long tasks via `PerformanceObserver buffered`) as a "perf budget" line: index compile long task ≤ 3 s, `canvases:0`, infinite animations on-screen ≤ 2.
- `chart.umd.js` behind a `loadChartJs()` (same shape as `loadHtml2Canvas`) would let the DAO Buys and Affiliates windows own their 69 KB; calculators' six charts likewise.
- A `favicon.png` + `apple-touch-icon.png` pair in the root (deploy.yml already copies `*.png`) replaces 5 × 8.7 KB of inline base64 and fixes P-7 in one commit.

`probes/a11y-scan.js`, `probes/modal-geometry.js`, `probes/overflow.js` on: Home, #/ptgc, #/ufo, Socials, KPI, Affiliates
(AFFIL_DATA=1 and the empty program), Live Feed (DECK_LOGS=8 and empty), the Leagues / TOP 100 / Holders details / Volume /
RH / DAO Buys / Disclaimer (NO_ACCEPT=1) windows, portfolio.html (2 seeded wallets, 1 wallet, empty; rank window, leagues,
Get Code, Load Code, Edit wallet), calculators.html, charts.html, ledger.html. Logs and screenshots: `/home/claude/audit/work/U/*.log|png`.

#### U-1 · GET CODE silently produces an EMPTY backup code when any wallet label has a character outside Latin-1 (emoji, “–”, CJK)
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: portfolio.html:1538–1543 (`encodePortfolio`), 3811–3815 (`handleGetCode`), 3896–3912 (the code window)
- problem: `return'GRAY-'+btoa(JSON.stringify(data));` inside `try{…}catch{return null}`. `btoa()` throws `InvalidCharacterError`
  for any code point > 255, so the whole encode returns `null`; `handleGetCode` does `setGeneratedCode(code||'')` and opens the
  window anyway. The user sees an empty box and a working "Copy to Clipboard" button that copies "".
- evidence: harness — seed `[{address:0x1111…,name:"Cold 🚀"}]`, click GET CODE →
  `EVAL {"code":"","btn":"Copy to Clipboard"}` (`pf-emoji.log`, screenshot `pf-emoji-code.png`: an empty gold-bordered box).
  `node -e "btoa(JSON.stringify([{n:'Cold 🚀'}]))"` → `InvalidCharacterError: Invalid character`. The owner has already hit this
  exact `btoa` Latin-1 trap twice on the Worker (HANDOVER.md:886–888, 1902) — this is the third copy of it, in the page.
  A label with "é" survives (U+00E9 ≤ 255); "Ledger – cold" (en dash U+2013), any emoji, any CJK, "₿" do not.
- why it matters: GET CODE is the only backup / cross-device path the portfolio has. A user who names a wallet with an emoji
  (on a site whose own buttons are "📋 GET CODE") believes they saved a code, then LOAD CODE on the new phone says
  "Invalid portfolio code" — and LOAD CODE replaces the current list, so a second device's wallets can be lost too.
- fix: UTF-8-safe base64 both ways, backward compatible:
  `btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(data))))` on encode;
  on decode try `JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(Uint8Array.from(atob(b64),c=>c.charCodeAt(0))))` first and fall back to
  `JSON.parse(atob(b64))` for codes made before the change. And: when `encodePortfolio` returns null, show
  "Couldn't build the code — a wallet label has an unsupported character" instead of an empty box; disable the copy button.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### U-2 · Portfolio's Get Code and Load Code windows are not dialogs: no Escape, no focus trap, no scroll lock, page behind stays tabbable
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: portfolio.html:3896–3930 (`showCodeModal`, `showLoadModal`); the dead copies at 3932–3952 (`editingWallet`, never set by

- problem: both windows are plain `<div className="fixed inset-0 …" onClick=…>` — not `PfModal` (1873), which is the page's
  dialog shell (role=dialog, Escape, Tab cycle, iOS scroll lock, focus return). The Load Code `input` has `focus-visible` styling

- evidence: `pf-loadcode.log`: `modal-geometry → "nodialog"`, `document.activeElement → "BUTTON.h2-pfb"` (focus still on the
  opener), after `key=Escape` the input `placeholder^=GRAY` is still present (`true`). `pf-getcode.log`: same, `code` still present
  after Escape. a11y-scan scope is "page", i.e. every control behind the overlay is reachable with Tab.
- why it matters: keyboard / screen-reader users cannot leave the Load Code window except by clicking the backdrop; on iOS the
  page behind scrolls under the overlay (the reason PfModal uses `position:fixed`). It is also the window that REPLACES the
  wallet list, so it is the one that most needs a deliberate Escape.
- fix: wrap both in `<PfModal onClose={…} label="Portfolio code">` / `label="Load a portfolio code"` exactly like
  `PfEditWallet` (3125); delete the two dead components (3932–3952, 3971–4010) so nobody copies them again.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### U-3 · Holder rank ranks a wallet that SOLD since the snapshot against its own stale entry — rank one too low, "to pass #N" points at its own ghost
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: portfolio.html:1966–1968 (`pfRank`, source tools/pf-v2/PfLeagues.jsx:160–162); index.html:12559–12561 (Top 100 checker:
  `const own=view.top&&view.top.find(x=>x.a===al); const list=own?t1Without(view.all,own.v):view.all;`)
- problem: `holder-ranks.json` carries addresses only for the first 110 (`top`); `b` is 17,247 bare balances. `pfRank` has no
  address at all, so the wallet's own listed amount stays in the list. While the live balance is ≥ the listed one the own entry
  sits at or below the wallet and is harmless (ties share a rank). As soon as the user has sold / moved tokens since the
  2-hourly snapshot, their old balance is ABOVE them and counts as "someone to pass". The Top 100 checker fixed this (A2) only for
  wallets in `top`; the same gap remains for #111 and below.
- evidence: unit test on the real function (`/home/claude/audit/work/U/unit/pf.js`, extracted from portfolio.html):
  `b=[100,50,50,10]`, wallet listed at 50, now holds 40 → `pfRank → {r:4, pass:11}`; without its own entry the truth is
  `r=3`, and "▲ 11 more to pass #3" is asking the user to pass THEMSELVES. Holding 60 (bought) → `{r:2,pass:41}` correct.
  Ties are common: 3,844 of 17,247 adjacent `b` values are equal (whole-token rounding), so a wallet's ghost is often tied with
  real neighbours and the badge, the rank window hero ("TODAY #4") and the breakdown column all inherit the error for up to 2 h,
  or 48 h under `PF_RANKS_STALE`.
- why it matters: it is a number shown as "your rank" and a buy target ("+ X more to pass") to people deciding whether to buy;
  it is wrong precisely for the users who just sold, i.e. the ones most likely to look.
- fix (touches a displayed number; shown old vs new above): the rank-index shard already knows the wallet (PfRank.jsx:2–7,
  `rank-index/<TOKEN>/<shard>.json`, its last day = the rank at the snapshot). When the shard has loaded, drop that one entry:
  `const list=r0?t1Without-equivalent(b, b[r0-1]):b` before the binary search (same rule as index.html:12561). For the badge
  before the shard lands, keep today's value but label it "as of the list" — or, cheaper for the builder: write each wallet's
  listed amount into its shard record so `pfRank` can subtract it without the day history.
- effort: Medium
- risk: Medium
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### U-4 · The disclaimer's "I Understand & Accept" button is below the fold on common screens, with no scroll cue; `90vh` on iOS
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:17521–17570 (`DisclaimerModal`: `style={{maxHeight:'90vh',overflowY:'auto'}}`, button at 17566)
- problem: the panel is 916 px of content at 1380×900 and 1,116 px at 390×844; the panel caps at 90vh and scrolls INSIDE,
  but nothing says so (no fade, no sticky button, the backdrop is `bg-black/95` so the page gives no hint either).
- evidence: `disc.log` geometry `panelH 810, sh 916` → 106 px hidden, screenshot `disc-modal.png` shows a 4 px sliver of the
  gold button at the bottom edge; `disc-m.log` `panelH 760, sh 1116` → 356 px hidden on a phone. `90vh` is the LARGE viewport on
  iOS Safari (toolbars showing), so on an iPhone the panel can be taller than what is visible and the button needs a scroll of
  the panel while the body is scroll-locked.
- why it matters: this is the first thing every new visitor sees (c33 is about its look; this is about reaching the only
  button). A user who does not realise the card scrolls sees a disclaimer with no way to accept and leaves.
- fix: `maxHeight:'90dvh'` (every other window already uses `100dvh`), make the button `position:sticky; bottom:0` inside the
  scroll box with a dark gradient above it, or shorten the copy (no `DISCLAIMER_VERSION` bump needed if the wording stays).
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### U-5 · The "unknown, not zero" explanations are hover-only `title=` text — invisible on every phone
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:14376 ("The chain read for this window failed. This is not zero — it is unknown."), 14723 ("Not read yet (or
  the read failed). Unknown, not zero."), 14921, 14926, 14350 ("more than a week old, so these windows are shown as unknown"),
  14345, 14367, 14371, 10165/14280 ("24h transaction counts unavailable — DexScreener did not answer"), 11951, 12469, 3472
  (FreshnessBadge "Last refresh failed — data may be stale. Click to retry."), 5229
- problem: the honesty pass (gotcha 20) prints "—" everywhere a read failed, and puts WHY in `title=`. Gotcha 10 itself says
  `title=` is hover-only; 12 of the 139 `title=` on index.html carry exactly the text a phone user needs to tell "unknown" from
  "nothing".
- evidence: grep `title=[^>]*(unknown|unavailable|failed|not zero|too old)` → 12 hits (lines above). The burn window at
  14365–14378 renders `<div … title="The chain read … unknown">—</div>` with no visible sibling text; the FreshnessBadge
  (3472) has only a coloured dot + "4m ago" and its failure state is `text-red-300/80` + the title. Counts per page of
  `title=`: index 139 (5 empty), portfolio 20, calculators 9, charts 1, ledger 1.
- why it matters: on a phone a failed read and "really zero" look identical ("—" vs "0" is a convention the page never
  explains visibly). The dashboard went to a lot of trouble to never claim $0; the explanation of the dash is not delivered to
  ~70 % of visitors.
- fix: one visible legend per panel foot ("— = not read / unavailable", already the house style on the Holders window's foot),
  and for the four states that matter (chain read failed, snapshot too old, DexScreener silent, refresh failed) an `InfoTip`
  with `EXPLAINERS.unknown` next to the dash — the component exists and works on touch (6639–6697, click toggles, Escape
  closes). The FreshnessBadge failure can be a visible word ("refresh failed · tap to retry").
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### U-6 · Affiliates v2: the referrer card is reachable only by mouse — rows, name spans, "▶ 2" expanders and sort headers are click-only
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:17351 (`<tr … cursor-pointer onClick={()=>setSelectedReferrer(s.name)}>`), 17316 (phone card `div onClick`),
  17394 and 17453 (`<span className="… cursor-pointer" onClick=…>{e.username}</span>`), 17455 (`<td … cursor-pointer onClick=…>`
  expander), 17339–17346 and 17436–17442 (sortable `<th onClick>`), 17188 (receipt username span), 16927/16932/16972/16977
  ("show more" divs), 17173 (receipt day header div)
- problem: none of these carries `role="button"`, `tabIndex`, or a key handler. The new AfPodium (16151–16169) does it right
  (`role="button" tabIndex={0} onKeyDown`), so the pattern was known when the page was redone on 2026-10-22.
- evidence: `affil.log` divClick = 6 (`span.block.truncate…"alice"`, `td.py-3…"▶2"`, ×3); `affil-m.log` divClick = 3; the
  referrer window with its threshold verdict / progress card opens only from those (`af-ref2.log` — reached with a mouse click on

  SHOW → the Setup link; no referrer is ever focused.
- why it matters: the threshold verdict ("$60.00 to go", BELOW MIN) is the number a referrer comes for; a keyboard or
  switch-access user, and a screen reader, cannot open it. Sorting is also unreachable.

- effort: Small

#### U-7 · `npm run compile` compiles 0 bytes of portfolio.html and calculators.html — the "fails loudly on a syntax error" check only covers index.html and ledger.html
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: tools/harness/compile.js:3 (`html.match(/<script type="text\/babel"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/)`), README "Compile … fails
  loudly on a syntax error"; portfolio.html:1073 and calculators.html (the first babel tag is `src="h2-header.jsx?v=3"` with an
  empty body)
- problem: the regex takes the FIRST babel tag; on the two pages that load `h2-header.jsx` first that tag has no body, so the
  4,000- and 5,000-line inline blocks are never compiled. `h2-header.jsx` itself is never compiled either.
- evidence: `node compile.js ../../portfolio.html → compiled ok 0 bytes 27 ms`; same for calculators; ledger → 70,098 bytes;
  index → 1,222,216 bytes in 4.2 s. (`run.js` mounts the pages for real, so a broken page is caught only when someone runs a
  mount recipe for that page.)
- why it matters: a stray character in portfolio.html (edited through `tools/pf-v2.py` splices) ships as a blank page, and the
  one check meant to catch it says "ok". This is the cheapest possible gate and it is green for the wrong reason.
- fix: `const blocks=[...html.matchAll(/<script type="text\/babel"(?![^>]*\bsrc=)[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/g)]`, compile every
  block (and `h2-header.jsx` as a file), exit 1 on the first error; loop the five pages by default. The extractor at
  `/home/claude/audit/work/U/unit/extract.js` already uses that regex.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### U-8 · A compile error leaves "LOADING…" on screen forever on index.html and a black page on the four sibling pages
- status: LIKELY ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:1553–1560 (the c14 boot shell, last line "LOADING…"); portfolio.html:1071, calculators.html:83, ledger.html:25
  (`<div id="root"></div>`, nothing inside); no `window.addEventListener('error'…)` on any page (grep: 0 hits)
- problem: Babel standalone reports a syntax error to the console and renders nothing. Index keeps the static shell — whose
  last line is "LOADING…" — so the visitor waits; the sibling pages have no shell at all, so they are black for the ~1–3 s
  compile on a phone and black forever on an error. The React `ErrorBoundary` (a26) only exists after a successful compile.
- evidence: structure above; `compile.js` is the only guard and (U-7) misses two pages. With `SLOW=9000` the sibling pages show
  nothing until mount (portfolio has its own `initialLoad` loader only after React is up).
- why it matters: the deploy is a copy of the working tree; one bad splice = a blank portfolio for everyone until noticed.
- fix: a 6-line inline script in `<head>` of all five pages: `window.addEventListener('error',e=>{if(/Babel|Unexpected
  token/.test(e.message||'')||!document.querySelector('#root>*')){…swap the shell text to "Something broke while loading —
  reload, and if it keeps happening tell us in the Telegram"…}})` plus a 12 s timer that does the same if `#root` is still the
  shell; give the sibling pages the same 10-line shell as index (title + "LOADING…").
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### U-9 · Buys / sells are told apart by colour only (TXNS 24H tile, LP rows, pool cards)
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:10165 (`<small><span className="b">{buys}</span><span className="sl"> / </span><span className="s">{sells}</span></small>`,

  this is the one place the rule was missed.
- evidence: `ptgc-d.log` text: `TXNS 24H | 200 | 128 / 90`; a11y-scan `contrast` is fine (it is not a contrast issue).
- why it matters: ~8 % of men are red-green colour-blind; for them and for every screen-reader user the split is two numbers
  with no meaning. The tile is in the first row of the dashboard.
- fix: `<span className="b"><span className="sr-only">buys </span>128</span>` … `<span className="s"><span className="sr-only">sells

- effort: Small

- priority: P3

  filter tiles are `<div onClick>`), ledger month pills
- problem / evidence: a11y-scan smallTap — `pf.log`: `26x24 button.pf2-tg`, `24x24 button.pf2-ed`, `24x28 "Remove A"`,
  `5x8 button.day`; `pf-edit.log`: `12x24 button.x "Close"`; `pf-rank1.log`: `22x28 button.x`, `15x15 span.ii "About the cost"`,

  bars share ~280 px, i.e. 9 px apart with 19 px hit boxes — the overlapping hit areas make the neighbour win half the time.
- why it matters: these are the newest, most-used controls (every wallet card has three of them); 24 px is under both Apple's
  44 pt and the site's own 32 px `.tap` rule (h2.css:158).
- fix: add `.hit` (h2.css:160 — invisible 32×32 box, no layout change) to `.pf2-ed`, `.pf2-tg`, `.t1cp`, the two `.x` closes and

  "Day by day" list button next to the strip label).
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### U-11 · `fmtAbbr`'s a67 boundary fix never reached calculators.html, ledger.html or portfolio's classic copy; negatives and Infinity print raw
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P3
- area: calculators.html:290, ledger.html:30–40, portfolio.html:1129 (old bands `>=1e12 / 1e9 / 1e6 / 1e3`); index.html:3406 and
  portfolio.html:1868 `pfAbbr` (fixed bands `999.995e9 …`)
- problem: the three old copies pick the unit BEFORE rounding. Also none of the five abbreviates a negative (falls through to
  `Math.floor(num).toLocaleString`) and `Infinity` → `"InfinityT"`.
- evidence (node, both implementations side by side): 999,999 → index `1.00M` / calc+ledger `1000.00K`; 999,999,999 → `1.00B` /
  `1000.00M`; 999,999,999,999 → `1.00T` / `1000.00B`; −5,000,000 → `-5,000,000` on both (7 characters wider than "5.00M");
  −0.5 → `-1`; Infinity → `InfinityT`. ledger.html calls its `fmtAbbr` 58 times (DAO transfers in the 10⁸–10⁹ range are exactly
  where `1000.00M` appears); calculators 8 times; portfolio's old `fmtAbbr` is used twice (1129 is also the one `fmtDollar`
  shares its line with).
- why it matters: "1000.00M" next to "1.00B" on the next page is the kind of thing that makes a reader doubt the numbers.
- fix: paste the index.html:3406 body into the three copies (one line each), guard `!isFinite(num)` → "—", and route negatives
  through `(num<0?'-':'')+fmtAbbr(Math.abs(num))` where a signed amount is possible (portfolio's day window already does
  `pfAbbr(Math.abs(r.amt))`). This is a formatting change, not a formula change.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### U-12 · When holder-ranks.json is missing, stale (> 48 h) or malformed the whole HOLDER RANK feature vanishes without a word
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P3
- area: portfolio.html:1952–1965 (`PF_RANKS_STALE=48*3600e3`, `usePfRanks` → `null` on any failure, `stale:true` after 48 h);
  tools/pf-v2/PortfolioV2.jsx:369/432 (`rankRow={rkOne&&<PfRankRow …/>}` → `PfRankRow` returns `null` when `x` is null), :492
  (`hasR=!!ranks&&!ranks.stale` hides the column), :481 (the breakdown subtitle drops "holder rank, ")
- problem: the row, the badge column and the "holder rank" word all disappear — the user who saw "#2,114" yesterday sees a
  League bar with no rank line and nothing says "rank list not available / being rebuilt". The Top 100 window has the honest
  states for the SAME file (index.html:12627 "Couldn't load the list … Retry"; 12629 `t1old` "read N h ago — too old to rank
  against, so ranks are hidden until the next …"), so two surfaces fed by one file disagree on how to fail.
- evidence: `pf-rank.log` / `pf-rank-m.log`: CLICKFAIL on `button[aria-label^='PTGC holder rank']` with two wallets (expected —
  `rkOne`), but the same silent absence is what a stale file produces; the code paths above return `null` with no state carried
  to the UI (`usePfRanks` has no `error` field).
- why it matters: the pipeline runs "~5 times a day" and the file is 2-hourly — a weekend outage crosses 48 h and every
  portfolio user loses the feature silently; some will think they sold out of the list.
- fix: `usePfRanks` returns `{state:'loading'|'ok'|'stale'|'failed', …}`; `PfRankRow` prints the Top 100 wording for stale /

  Retry"), and the breakdown keeps the column with "—".
- effort: Small

    ×12) · "League" (portfolio bar) · "TOP 100" (window) · "HOLDER RANK" (portfolio badge/window ×13) · "Tier" (charts ×79, a
    different meaning — chart density tiers).
  · affiliate money: "Commissions" (×16) · "Comm. Paid" / "Comm. paid" (×6) · "Commission paid" (×2) · "Paid out" (×4) ·
    "Pending" / "Unpaid" / "BELOW MIN" / "Threshold Met" (the same status in four spellings).
  · freshness: "as of" (×26 on index), "Updated 1.5h ago", "Last update unknown", "Data as of … — 2.5h old", "read just now",
    "chart as of 2.6h ago — the hourly update …" (c37 L-16 counted five phrasings; it is six now with "read just now").
- evidence: the grep table above; screenshots `pf-crop1.png` (LIFETIME EARNED / EARNED BY SOURCE / REFLECTIONS / "rewards day by
  day" on one card) and `feed-empty-deck.png` ("FEES GENERATED 30D" next to "GENERATED 30D").
- why it matters: a holder reading "Value Generated $79,654" on the dashboard, "Lifetime Earned 19.73B" on the portfolio and
  "Rewards" on the calculator cannot tell whether these are the same thing (they are three different things, which makes the
  naming matter more, not less).
- fix: a 10-line glossary decision in HANDOVER (one noun per concept + where each is allowed), then rename labels only — no
  maths. Suggested: "Value Generated" (protocol-level, dashboard) / "Earned" (your wallet, portfolio) / "Projected rewards"
  (calculators); "Leagues" everywhere a tier is meant (retire "Holder tiers" from the dialog label); "Commission" + one status
  vocabulary (Paid / Pending / Below minimum).
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### U-15 · Five golds across the site, three on the portfolio alone (extends c36 L-8, which counted one)
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P3
- area: index.html `#D4AF37` ×132, `#E8C044` ×45, `#F2C94C` ×22; calculators.html `#D4AF37` ×100; charts.html `#D4AF37` ×17 +
  `#EAB308` ×6; ledger.html `#D9CA5D` ×21; portfolio.html `#F2D35B` ×63 + `#F6E98A` ×37 + `#D9CA5D` ×28 + `#F2C94C` ×7
  (tools/pf-v2/pf2.css: F2D35B ×47, F6E98A ×35; tools/top100/t1.css: F2C94C ×6; tools/affiliate/af.css: F2C94C ×2 + D4AF37 ×2);
  Tailwind `yellow-400/500` ×31 on index, ×28 on calculators; `amber-*` ×29 index
- evidence: `grep -o -i "#[0-9a-f]{6}" … | sort | uniq -c` (full table in this sweep's notes). On one portfolio screen the header
  uses `#F2C94C`/h2 gold, the cards `#F2D35B`, the Get Code window `#D9CA5D` (`pf-emoji-code.png`: the window's border is
  visibly yellower than the page).
- why it matters: brand gold is the one colour the site is known by; the newest surfaces (Top 100, Affiliates v2) chose
  `#F2C94C`, Portfolio v2 chose `#F2D35B`, and c36 is asking to move everything to `#D4AF37`. Decide once before more pages copy
  the wrong one.
- fix: one `--gold` / `--gold-hi` pair in h2.css (`#F2C94C` / `#F6E98A` if the new surfaces are the reference), the fragments'
  CSS read the variables, the Tailwind arbitrary values become `text-[var(--gold)]`.
- effort: Medium
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### U-16 · The browser Back button inside an open window leaves the dashboard instead of closing the window
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:17583–17600 (hash router: "every navigation assigns location.hash"); the MODAL SHELL 6431–6498 (modals are
  React state only); `popstate` — 0 hits in index/portfolio/calculators/charts
- problem: opening Leagues / TOP 100 / Holders / DAO Buys / the KPI share cards pushes nothing onto history. On Android the
  reflex for "close this sheet" is the hardware Back; here it navigates to the previous hash (Home, or the previous token), the
  Dashboard unmounts and the window is gone with it. A window opened from the Socials tab (which switches token first) can send
  the user two steps away.
- evidence: structure only (no harness Back action); every window checked is `showX && <Modal>` with no history call.
- why it matters: a user mid-read in the Top 100 table (the longest window) is dumped on Home and has to find the panel again;
  not data loss, but it is the most common gesture on the majority platform.
- fix: in `useDialog` (6457), `history.pushState({modal:id},'')` on open and a `popstate` listener that calls `closeRef.current()`
  when the top of `MODAL_STACK` is this dialog; on programmatic close, `history.back()` only if the state is ours. ~15 lines in one
  place; PfModal needs the same copy.
- effort: Small
- risk: Medium
- recommendation: LATER

#### U-17 · Saving the wallet list is a silent `catch{}` — a full / disabled localStorage loses wallets without a word
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P3
- area: portfolio.html:3637 (`try{localStorage.setItem(PORTFOLIO_STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(wallets));}catch{}`); 1864 `pfLsSet`
  (used for the history / earned caches, shrinks and retries up to 40 times but is NOT used for the wallet list); 3596 (boot read)
- problem: the two caches (`PFH_LS`, `PFE_LS`, 2008 / 2350) shrink themselves only when THEIR write throws, so they settle at
  the quota edge; the wallet list's own write then fails and nothing is shown. Private mode on older Safari and "block site data"
  throw on every write; the page behaves as if it saved.
- evidence: code above; the v2 add form validates and announces (`role="alert"`, PortfolioV2.jsx:282) but has no path for a
  save failure. Not reproduced in the harness (quota is not stubbable); reasoning only.
- why it matters: the user adds five wallets, closes the tab, reopens — empty, and Get Code was never offered as the fallback.
- fix: route the wallet list through `pfLsSet` (no shrink) and on `false` show the `pf2-addmsg` alert "Couldn't save your wallets
  on this device (storage full or blocked) — use GET CODE to keep them"; evict the two caches first when the list fails.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### U-18 · "need 1 ≈ $0": the league bar prints a $0 claim for a sub-dollar gap (and "100.0%" while still below the line)
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P3
- area: portfolio.html:3059–3066 (`pfLeague`: `pct:Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(balance-floor)/(ceil-floor)*100))`,
  `need:Math.max(0,ceil-balance)`), 3107 (`need <b>{pfAbbr(L.need)}</b>{cost!=null&&<> ≈ {fmtDollar(cost,0)}</>}`)
- problem: `pfAbbr` floors, `fmtDollar(cost,0)` rounds; a gap of 1.3 tokens at $0.000142 prints "need 1 ≈ $0" under "100.0% to
  Poseidon" — three statements that contradict each other (100 % but not there; need 1; costs nothing).
- evidence: `pf-crop1.png` (harness stub: 2 × 16,666,666,666 = 33,333,333,332 vs the 10 % line 33,333,333,333.3) shows exactly
  "100.0% to Poseidon · need 1 ≈ $0"; unit run: `pfLeague(33333333332, 333333333333) → {pct:99.99999999566666, need:1.3000030517578125}`.
  Real wallets sit this close to a line whenever they bought "exactly" a tier amount and a reflection or a rounding moved them.
- why it matters: gotcha 20 — "$0 is a claim". Small, but it is on the one bar every holder looks at.
- fix: `pct` → `Math.min(99.9, …)` while `need>0`; `need` → `pfPassAmt(need)` (already handles < 1: portfolio.html:1969);
  cost under $0.50 → "< $1". Formatting only.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### U-19 · Nothing runs in CI; the smallest job that catches a blank page
- status: OPTIONAL IMPROVEMENT
- priority: P2
- area: .github/workflows (only data-pipeline.yml and deploy.yml); tools/harness (README: "Not wired to CI (roadmap b7/b8)")
- problem: a blank page can be deployed by any commit; nothing between the editor and GitHub Pages executes the code.
- evidence: `ls .github/workflows` → `data-pipeline.yml deploy.yml`; U-7 shows the manual compile check is also partial.
- why it matters: five pages × one-file edits × a Python splicer = the highest-probability failure on this project is a syntax
  slip, and it is 100 % catchable.
- fix — the job, ~3 minutes, free on a public repo: `on: push: paths: ['index.html','portfolio.html','calculators.html',
  'charts.html','ledger.html','h2-header.jsx','h2.css','tw.css','tools/harness/**']`; steps: checkout → `node 22` → `cd
  tools/harness && npm ci` (playwright-core only, ~30 s; no browser download: use `container: mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.47.0-jammy`
  which ships the matching Chromium at `CHROME=/ms-playwright/chromium-*/chrome-linux/chrome`) → `node compile.js` for the five
  pages (after the U-7 fix; ~12 s) → five mounts: `node run.js "#/ptgc" 1280 900 out/p "wait=4000;eval=document.querySelectorAll('.h2-tile').length"`,
  `#/`, `HTML=portfolio.html`, `HTML=calculators.html`, `HTML=charts.html` (~15 s each) → a 10-line `assert.js` that fails when
  `errors` contains anything but the `[BABEL] deoptimised` note and the known GeckoTerminal 404s (allow-list the two strings), or
  when the eval printed 0 tiles / `#root` still holds the boot shell (`probes/c-p5.js` already reads that). Cost: ~3 min/run; on a
  private repo ~60 runs = 180 of the 2,000 free minutes a month. Add `npm run css` drift later (`git diff --exit-code tw.css`).
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

### Testing coverage — inventory and the ten most valuable missing checks

**What exists (101 probes, all browser-side `evalfile` snippets, none asserting on their own).** Grouped by what they read:
geometry / overflow (`overflow`, `modal-geometry`, `deck-header`, `burn-toggle`, `lp-fit`, `pf2-squeeze`, `pf2-align`, `vg-fit`,
`vg-names`, `card-slack`, `cvg-fit`, `t1-hero`, `hd-pos`, `h2-imgs`, `h2-stats`); a11y (`a11y-scan`, `pf2-a11y2`, `c-p3`, `price-copy`);
honest-failure states (`fetch-fail`, `fetch-fail-all`, `ledger-summary`, `calc-sanity` NaN/Infinity/$0, `portfolio-state`,
`skeleton-watch`, `ladder-watch`, `eb-state`, `first-paint`, `h2-spark-state`, `lp-curves`, `lp-src`, `nh-state`, `kpi-combined`);
numbers-agree-with-each-other (`stale-tab` volume vs header, `c-holders` one holders number, `affil-a54` / `affil-card` /
`affil-registry` commission maths from a fixture, `c-p2`, `rc-state`, `vw-state`, `rh-modal`, `ledger-digest` per-TZ identity,
`ledger-rows`, `t1-audit` / `t1-check` Top 100 checker against the real #118, `pf2-moon`, `pf2-pls4x`, `pf2-v1`, `pf2-ed*`);
markup identity between builds (`dom-hash`, `lp-dom`, `vg-dom*`, `panels-classic`, `pf2-classic-hash` retired); share cards
(`share-png-real`, `nhs-social`, `rh-card`, `sh-rows`, `sh-rh`); driving scripts (`calc-type`, `pf2-add`, `pf2-edit`, `pf2-ui`,
`clock-shift`, `t1-btn`). The README holds ~45 recipes. Everything needs Chromium and a human to read the EVAL line.

**Not covered — ten checks, each tied to a money/data surface, and how each is a `node` unit test without a browser.** The
helpers below are module-scope `const`s inside the babel block; `/home/claude/audit/work/U/unit/extract.js` (70 lines, uses the
pinned `@babel/standalone`'s parser) pulls named top-level declarations with their top-level dependencies into a CommonJS file
(`idx.js`: 11 declarations for `fmtAbbr,fmtPrice,…,getBurnC,tierCountLabel,valueGenBucketUsd`; `pf.js`: `pfRank,pfLeague,
encodePortfolio,…`). That is enough for 1–8; 9–10 want a 30-line `lib/` move.

1. **Holder rank at ties and with the wallet's own stale entry** (U-3) — `pfRank` (portfolio.html:1966) and `t1Rank` /
   `t1Ranks` / `t1Without` (index.html:12434, 12497, 12500): `b=[100,50,50,10]` → ranks 1,2,2,4; balance 50 → #2 pass 51; 40
   with own listed 50 → must be #3 after the fix; dust 0.5 → pass 9.500001. Pure today.
2. **Top 100 "to break in" / cushion** (index.html:12562–12579): `T2=list[99]`, `need=T2-k+1`, `cush=k-list[100]`; fixtures with
   exactly 100 entries (`list[100]` undefined → cushion = whole balance — document that), ties at #100/#101, staked vs locked
   (`buy=need-free`). The maths sits inside the component's IIFE; move the 8 lines into `t1Verdict(list,k,b,l)` → pure.
3. **Affiliate threshold verdict and `lifetimeOf`** (the referrer card, a54): already has a browser probe; the function
   `lifetimeOf` / the month `meetsThreshold` reducer can be extracted as-is (no DOM) and run against `AFFIL_DATA`'s fixture:
   alice 40.00K not 41.60K, bob BELOW MIN, carol's own rate, `thresholdType:'USD'` in the wrong case.
4. **`computeValueGen` / `valueGenBucketUsd` null-through** (index.html module scope, gotcha 16): `basis` delivered /
   accrued / volume, `pending` → "—", `total:null` → "—", UFO never volume×fee unless `estimate:true`, every bucket with a
   null price → null (never 0). Pure today (extracted `valueGenBucketUsd` already runs).
5. **UFO reflections scan window exactness** (`getLogsChunked(...,exactTo)`, gotcha 4): the chunker is pure given a stubbed
   `getLogs(from,to)` recorder — assert the last chunk's `toBlock` equals `exactTo` (never `'latest'`), chunk boundaries are
   contiguous and non-overlapping, and the same for `build-value-generated.mjs`'s copy (a plain Node module — `require` it).
6. **Burn windows vs balance deltas** (`fetchBurnPeriodsOnChain` → windows h24/d7/d30/d90): feed a synthetic transfer log list
   with known burn amounts at known block times; windows must equal the sums, a window older than launch must carry
   `sinceLaunch:true` and equal the lifetime, and `carriedForward` must never be painted over a chain read. Needs the fetcher
   split into `windowsFromLogs(logs,nowBlock)` (pure) + the RPC shell.
7. **`fmt*` boundary values** (U-11): the five `fmtAbbr`s, `fmtPrice` (0.0001 edge, 4.542e-5 → `{pre:'$0.0',z:4,d:'4542'}`),
   `fmtSnapshotAge` (89.9 min → "90m ago"? 90 → "1.5h ago", 2880 → "2d ago"), `fmtDollar(0.4,0)` → "$0" (U-18), null/NaN/
   Infinity/negative on each. Pure today — a 40-line table test; this is where `1000.00K` would have been caught.
8. **"A null price never prints $0"** across N surfaces: `t1Usd(null)`, `valueGenBucketUsd(vg,{price:null})`, portfolio
   `usd(need,px)` with `px=0`, calculators' MCap with `price=null` (`calc-sanity` covers the page; the helpers can be table-tested).
9. **`pfLeague` / `getBurnC` tier boundaries** (U-18): balance exactly on a line (33,333,333,333.3 of 333.33B) → next tier; one
   wei below → `pct<100`, `need>0`; `LEAGUE_TIERS` and `BURN_C` must agree on the same fractions (two tables, two files).
10. **`encodePortfolio` / `decodePortfolio` round-trip** (U-1): ASCII, Latin-1, emoji, CJK names; a code made by the old
    encoder still decodes; `sanitizeWallets` drops bad addresses and dupes and keeps order.

**Pure enough today** (no DOM / fetch / React in their bodies): `fmt, fmtUSD, fmtComma, fmtDollar, fmtAbbr, fmtSnapshotAge,
fmtPrice, t1Key, t1Rank, t1Ranks, t1Without, t1Px, t1Short, t1Usd, t1League, getBurnC, creatureCount, tierCountLabel,
valueGenBucketUsd, computeValueGen, computeValueGenBuckets, computeDaoBuysView, computeDaoSocialView, pfRankKey, pfRank,
pfPassAmt, pfLeague, pfAbbr, encodePortfolio, decodePortfolio, sanitizeWallets, isValidAddr, routeFromHash, hashForRoute`.
**Need a seam first**: the Top 100 verdict (inside a component), `fetchBurnPeriodsOnChain` (windows + RPC in one function),
`lifetimeOf`'s callers, `getLogsChunked` (takes the RPC function — already injectable if `getLogs` is passed in).
**Two ways to run them**: (a) keep the pages as they are and generate the module in the test step with `extract.js` (zero
change to the site; brittle only if a helper starts touching `window`); (b) move the pure list into `lib/fmt.js` +
`lib/rank.js` loaded by a plain `<script src>` before the babel block (and `require`d by the tests) — one-time, ~200 lines
moved, and `tools/*.py` splicers untouched. `tools/harness/compiled.js` can also be `require`d after prepending a shim
(`global.React=require('react'); global.ReactDOM=…; global.window=global; global.document={querySelector:()=>null,…};
global.localStorage={getItem:()=>null,setItem(){},removeItem(){}}`) — it evaluates to the top-level consts, but the block ends in
`ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root'))`, so the shim must stub `getElementById` and `createRoot`; workable, uglier
than (a).

### Checked and found OK

- **Modal shell** (index.html:6431–6498): every index.html window reached in this sweep is a `<Modal>` with `role=dialog`,
  `aria-modal`, a label, focus moved in, Tab cycled, scroll locked (`bodyOverflow: hidden` in every geometry read), a named
  36×36 Close (Top 100, Holders, Volume, RH, DAO Buys, Leagues "Close" text button). `InfoTip` (6639–6697) is click-toggled,
  works on touch, `aria-expanded` / `aria-controls`, Escape closes the popover before the window (`claimKeys`). Portfolio's
  `PfModal` (1873–1885) is an equivalent (Escape, Tab trap, iOS `position:fixed` lock, focus return) and is used by Leagues,
  rank, rewards, Edit wallet, transactions, "How it works". The Top 100 `.t1m` is a `<Modal>` child (12615).
- **Names and alts**: a11y-scan `noName = 0` and `noAlt = 0` on every one of the 45 runs (every icon button carries an aria-label;
  decorative art is `alt=""`). `noFocusRing = 0` everywhere (u9 ring in h2.css:166–170 reaches index, portfolio, calculators,
  charts).
- **No horizontal page scroll at 390** on #/ptgc, Home, Affiliates, KPI, Socials, portfolio, calculators, charts, ledger
  (`overflow.js → scrollW=390 vw=390` ×9) — c2 is fixed; the tab band has the ‹ › cues (c3).
- **Tab band**: a `<nav aria-label="Dashboard sections">` of real buttons/links with `aria-current="page"` (index.html:10115–10126,
  h2-header.jsx:94) — keyboard reachable, screen-reader labelled. Supply-basis toggles are proper `role=tablist/tab`
  (12166–12172) and `aria-pressed` groups (PfSupplyToggle, Moon Math `role=group`).
- **Honest states on the new surfaces**: Top 100 — `undefined` loading / `null` → "Couldn't load the list … Retry" (12627),
  stale list → `role=status` "too old to rank against" (12629), invalid address → "That isn't a wallet address — paste one that
  starts with 0x (42 characters), or a PulseScan link" (`top100-check.log`); Live Feed with no events → "No swaps in the last 900
  blocks. Waiting for the next contact." (`feed-empty-deck.png`); Affiliates empty program → zero tiles + "Last update unknown",
  no crash (`af-empty.log`); portfolio with no wallets → the add capsule + "Track PTGC and UFO across all your wallets"
  (`pf-empty-top.png`); add-wallet validation is visible (`role="alert"`, PortfolioV2.jsx:210–214, 282) for bad address /
  duplicate / unnamed group; Remove asks first (portfolio.html:3120 `rm` state, round 23 A8).
- **Token-switch races**: `KPIContent` is unmounted by `setLoading(true)` on a token change (13389–13394, `activeTab==='kpi'&&!loading`),
  so its un-guarded `loadOtherToken` cannot paint UFO data under a PTGC heading; charts.html supersedes a running load
  (`fetchGeneration` + `AbortController`, 1458–1472); calculators keeps a7/a9; portfolio v2 has no token switch (both tokens on one
  page) and de-dupes transactions by `pftKey` (PfTx.jsx:54/67).
- **Routing**: `?token=` handoff is converted with `replaceState` so Back returns to the sibling page (17659–17672); `?open=`
  is stripped; `lastToken` remembers the Affiliates Back target (c9 done); `lsGet/lsSet/lsTake` are try/catch'd (1633–1635) so a
  blocked localStorage degrades to "prompt every visit", not a crash.
- **Colour + sign** on every price-change element checked (index 6090, 11792; portfolio 1202, 2328, 3084; charts 2294, 2455) —
  ▲/▼ or +/− always accompanies the colour; the exception is U-9.
- **SRI on charts' html2canvas** — the harness error "Failed to find a valid digest" is the harness's own stub replacing the file;
  the live hash matches the pinned 1.4.1 (`openssl dgst -sha384` = `ZZ1pncU3…`), not a site bug.
- **`lang="en"`** on all five pages; `fmtPrice` sub-micro notation carries the `sr-only` full decimal (a48).

### Ideas

- The disclaimer is only on index.html; portfolio / calculators / charts / ledger never show it (portfolio's copy at 3971 is
  dead code), so a shared `calculators.html` link skips it entirely. If that is deliberate, a one-line "not financial advice"
  in the sibling pages' foot (calculators has one, the others do not) is the cheap version.
- `probes/a11y-scan.js` + `modal-geometry.js` are already the right shape for the CI job in U-19: run them per window and fail
  on `noName`, `noAlt`, `smallTap` growing above today's numbers (a ratchet, not a cliff).
- Discoverability: "RH" on the Liquidity tile and "ⓘ" on Volume/Holders are the only entrances to three windows; the Combined
  KPI / Leagues / Burn / Value Generated cards, Targets and Logos exist only on the Socials tab, whose label does not say
  "share cards". A "Windows" row under the KPI tiles (Volume · Holders · RH · Top 100 · Leagues · DAO Buys) and renaming the
  tab "Share" would surface what already exists.
- The rank-index shard per wallet already holds the day-by-day rank; the breakdown table could show a 30-day ▲/▼ next to
  each badge from it (no new data).
- `extract.js` + the ten tests above are a one-afternoon `tools/harness/test/` folder (`node --test`), runnable in the U-19 job
  before the browser step; it would have caught U-1, U-11 and U-18 on the day they were written.

### Sweep V — Responsive / mobile scan (dynamic, harness) — 2026-10-22

Method: `tools/harness/run.js` (headless Chromium, stubbed RPC/DexScreener/PulseScan, real fonts) at 1920×1080, 1380×900,
1024×768, 768×1024, 390×844 (isMobile+touch) and 320×568, every route + every window at 390 and 1380. Every run got
`/home/claude/audit/work/V/rwd.js` (scrollWidth vs viewport, elements past the right edge — free and inside clipped boxes —,
ellipsis-clipped text, nowrap-hidden text, tap targets under 32 px, sticky/fixed bars, inputs inside fixed containers,
text under 11 px) and the windows got `/home/claude/audit/work/V/modal.js` (panel box vs 100dvh, scroll axes, close
button box, elements past the edge). Logs + full-page PNGs: `/home/claude/audit/work/V/out/<run>.log|.png`; runner
`/home/claude/audit/work/V/run.sh`. The 33-trillion DAO value and the "$33,333,404,…" ellipses in the logs are the stub's
fake PLS reserves (README: "the stub numbers are nonsense"), not a layout bug — real DAO values fit.

Console errors: none on index.html / portfolio / calculators / ledger; the "404" lines on Socials/KPI/Feed/Affiliates are
GeckoTerminal without `GT_CANDLES`; charts.html's 8 are the same 404s + the harness's html2canvas stub failing charts.html's
SRI hash (charts.html:12 has `integrity=`, the stub body differs) — harness-only.
