#### V-1 · The disclaimer's accept button is below the fold on every phone — a first-time phone visitor sees a wall of text and no button
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P1
- area: index.html:17523–17566 (`DisclaimerModal`)
- problem: `<div className="bg-[#111] … max-w-lg w-full p-6 shadow-2xl" style={{maxHeight:'90vh',overflowY:'auto'}}>` — the whole
  modal (heading, six paragraphs, the "I Understand & Accept" button at 17566) is ONE scroll box. At 390×844 the box is 758 px
  tall with 1,116 px of content: the button's top is at y=1039, i.e. 358 px below the panel's bottom edge (802). At 320×568
  (vh 647 after the browser chrome) it is 667 px down. Nothing in the panel says "scroll" — the last visible line is the
  middle of the "No Liability" paragraph (`out/disc-390-modal.png`).
- evidence: `NO_ACCEPT=1 node run.js "#/ptgc" 390 844 … evalfile=disc.js` → `{"vh":844,"buttons":[{"t":"IUnderstand&Accept","top":1039,"bottom":1091}],"panelBottom":802,"sh":1116,"ch":758,"hiddenPx":358}`;
  320×568 → `hiddenPx:667`; 768×1024 → `hiddenPx:0` (fits). Screenshot `/home/claude/audit/work/V/out/disc-390-modal.png`.
  Escape / backdrop cannot dismiss it (Audit II line 934: no `onClose`), so the only way in is to find the hidden button.
- why it matters: this is the first screen every new visitor on a phone gets (localStorage empty), and phones are the majority
  of a Telegram-driven audience. A visitor who does not think to scroll inside a modal sees a site that looks stuck; the fast
  ones scroll, the rest bounce. It also hides the one control the owner legally wants tapped.
- fix: keep the copy, split the box: panel = `flex flex-col max-h-[90dvh]`, the text in a `flex-1 overflow-y-auto min-h-0`
  div, the button in a non-scrolling footer (`pt-4 border-t border-white/10`) — the button is always on screen, the text
  scrolls above it. Optional: a 24 px bottom fade on the text box so the cut-off paragraph reads as "more below". Copy
  unchanged → `DISCLAIMER_VERSION` need not bump (gotcha 18). Pairs with c33's reskin if that is done at the same time.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### V-2 · 320-px phones (iPhone SE 1st gen / 5s, cheap Androids): the dashboard, charts and calculators scroll sideways, and six blocks collide or clip
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:15033–15047 (LP sort row), h2.css:215/240 + index.html:9773 (`h2Scale` floor), index.html:223 (`.hc-dash`), 179 (`.hc-t2`), 1513–1525 (`.sh-hd`), charts.html (`.range-bar`), calculators.html:4692–4702
- problem: nothing in the CSS is written for under ~360 px; the phone layouts are tuned to 390 and stop shrinking:
  1. **The page scrolls sideways on #/ptgc, #/ufo, Live Feed (dashboard beneath) — scrollW 364 / 336 vs vw 320.** Cause: the LP
     Pairs phone toolbar `<div className="flex gap-2 items-center">` (15033) holds the ALL/RH switch and the `Sort · Ratio Volume
     Liquidity 24h %` group, every button `whitespace-nowrap`, no `flex-wrap`, no `overflow-x-auto` → the group ends at x=336.
     `probes/overflow.js`: `scrollW=364 vw=320 div.bg-white/[0.03]… right=336 "SORT Ratio Volume↓ Liqui" | button.tap-h.px-1 right=329 "24h %"`.
     A 44-px dark gutter is visible right of every panel in `/home/claude/audit/work/V/out/ptgc-320.png`.
  2. **charts.html scrolls sideways (386/320)**: the range bar `24H 7D 14D 1M 3M 6M 1Y` (r=386) and the preset row (r=345) are
     nowrap flex rows — `out/charts-320.log` wideFree.
  3. **calculators.html scrolls sideways (355/320)**: the New Capital Simulator's two token cards (`flex-1` side by side, r=355) and
     the page title `span.cp-t.w` r=326 — `out/calc-320.log`.
  4. **KPI tiles**: `h2Scale` = `hsp:Math.max(.9,Math.min(1.3,w/390))` (9773) floors the phone scale at .9 while 320/390 = .82, so
     the two-column tiles are 10 % too big for the column: the Volume tile's ⓘ + bot badge sit on the "H" of VOLUME 24H
     (`out/ptgc-320-tiles.png`), and the 📷 share buttons of the Burn / Value Generated panels are pushed outside the panel
     and clipped (`button.tap.-my-2 r=342 in section…overflow-hidden "📷"`) — the share entry disappears on these phones.
  5. **Home**: `.hc-dash` (BUY / SELL pill, `position:absolute;top:12px;right:12px`, 223) lands on the token name — "PT|GC" and
     "UF|O" are half covered (`out/home-320.png`); `.hc-t2 DASHBOARD` (min 2.2rem Orbitron 900, 179) is 348 px wide and loses
     "D" and the last "D" to `overflow:hidden` on `.hc-page`.
  6. **Socials Hub**: each `.sh-col` is 386 px wide in a 320 viewport (`div.sh-col r=386 in section.sh`) — the right border and
     the › chevron are cut off down the whole 4,000-px stack (`out/soc-320-a.png`); cause: `.sh-hd` = 96-px logo (`flex:none`)
     + 27-px Orbitron name + 36-px `.go`, no `min-width:0` on the name block.
  Also: the UFO Value Generated USD/TOK toggle is clipped at the panel's left edge (`out/ufo-320-mid.png`); the portfolio
  stat tiles `.pf2-st.v3` are 326 px wide (6 px over) and the Earned/Lock boxes spill 7–26 px (`pf2-squeeze.js` at 320:
  `spill` ×7, `clip` ×2 — the probe is clean at 390).
- evidence: the six logs `out/{ptgc,ufo,feed,charts,calc,home,soc}-320.log` (scrollW line + wideFree/wideClipped), the
  screenshots named above. Nothing overflows at 390 on any route (every `scrollW=390`).
- why it matters: 320–360 px is still 5–10 % of Android traffic (Galaxy A-series in 320 CSS px mode, older iPhones). On those,
  the main page wobbles sideways on every scroll, the share buttons are gone, and the Home card's buy pill hides the name.
- fix (all small, no layout redesign): (1) `flex gap-2 items-center flex-wrap` on 15033 (the sort group drops under the
  switch) — this alone removes the page-level scroll on index.html; (2) charts.html `.range-bar{flex-wrap:wrap}` or
  `overflow-x:auto`; (3) calculators 4549: `flex-col sm:flex-row` for the two cards (see V-3 — the cards are already too narrow
  at 390); (4) `hsp:Math.max(.78,…)` so the floor equals 305/390 — every tile dimension is in `--u`, so this is one number;
  (5) `.hc-dash` under 360 px: `position:static` after the name, or `top:auto;bottom:12px`; `.hc-t2` min `1.9rem`;
  (6) `.sh-hd .nm{min-width:0}` + `.sh-hd>div{min-width:0}` and `.lg{width:72px;height:72px}` under 360.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER (but (1) is a one-word change and worth doing now)

#### V-3 · Calculators (390 — the default tab): the New Capital Simulator's Fee badge is printed over the token name and "Split" is clipped
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: calculators.html:4548–4566 (token card header), 4692–4702 (amount + mode row)
- problem: the two token cards are `flex-1` side by side at every width (4548: `<div className="flex-1 …">`), so at 390 each is
  173 px wide. Inside, 4551 `px-5 pt-5 pb-2 flex items-center justify-between` holds a 48-px logo + `font-orbitron text-2xl`
  name on the left and `<div className="text-right"><div>Fee {(tCfg.feeRate*100)}%</div>…` on the right — "PTGC" spans x 94–174
  and "Fee 5%" x 134–163: a 29-px overprint; "UFO" 287–367 vs "Fee 6%" 327–356, a 29-px overprint
  (`/home/claude/audit/work/V/out/calc-390-fee.png`). Above it, 4692 `flex items-center gap-4 … justify-center` with a `w-72`
  input and three `whitespace-nowrap` mode buttons: "Split" ends at x=380 inside a card clipped at 378 — its right border is
  cut (`out/calc-390-mode.png`), and the `$ Amount` box is squeezed to ~105 px, too narrow to show "Amount" + "USD" and a value.
- evidence: `HTML=calculators.html node run.js "" 390 844 … evalfile=calcprobe.js` → `{"split":[317,380],"clipRight":378,"fee":[[134,163],[327,356]],"names":[["PTGC",94,174],["UFO",287,367]],"cardW":[173,173]}`.
  Not present at 768 and up (`out/calc-768.png`). Calculators is the 4th tab and the New Capital Simulator opens by default.
- why it matters: the first thing a phone user sees on the default calculator is a garbled card header (fee text through the
  token name) and a mode row with a cut button — the page reads as broken before a number is typed.
- fix: 4548 wrapper → `flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3` (cards stack under 640, each full width — the Portfolio page already
  stacks its PTGC/UFO panels the same way); 4692 → `flex flex-col sm:flex-row` so the input is full width and the three modes a
  row under it. No formula touched.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### V-4 · Calculators (390): Moon Math's "📂 Load from Portfolio →" is cut off at the card edge
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: calculators.html:3582–3591
- problem: `<div className="flex items-center gap-3">` with the "My Bag:" label, a `w-44` (176 px) input and the
  `whitespace-nowrap` "📂 Load from Portfolio →" button, no wrap: the button's right edge is at x=415 in a 390 viewport, inside
  `rounded-2xl overflow-hidden`, so the user sees "📂 Load from Port" and the → is gone (`/home/claude/audit/work/V/out/calc390-moon-a.png`).
  The same row pattern is at 3161 (X Multiplier) and 4146 (Rewards) — those happen to fit at 390 because their input is narrower;
  check them at 360.
- evidence: `out/calc390-moon.log` wideClipped: `div.flex.items-center.gap-3 r=415 in div.rounded-2xl.overflow-hidden "MY BAG: 📂 Load from Por" | button… r=415 "📂 Load from Portfolio →"`.
- why it matters: the Portfolio → calculator hand-off is the owner's own feature (it reads `ptgc_ufo_portfolio`); on a phone the
  button looks truncated and its purpose ("…from Portfolio") is cut mid-word.
- fix: `flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3` (the button drops to its own line) or `w-32 sm:w-44` on the input.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### V-5 · Leagues window (390): "STARTING SUPPLY" and "CIRCULATING SUPPLY" run into each other on the toggle
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:1120 (`.lg-hd .tg{position:absolute;top:14px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%)}`), 12161–12176 (`SupplyBasisToggle`: `inline-grid grid-cols-2`, labels `whitespace-nowrap`)
- problem: the toggle is absolutely positioned with `left:50%` and no width, so its shrink-to-fit width is capped at the
  containing block's remaining half (366 px panel → 183 px). `grid-cols-2` then gives each label ~86 px while "CIRCULATING SUPPLY"
  at 11 px / tracking-wider needs ~130 px: the inactive label overflows leftwards under the gold pill and the two labels read as
  one word (`/home/claude/audit/work/V/out/lg-390-toggle.png`). At 560 px (desktop panel) the half is 280 px → 134-px columns → fine.
  Same component on portfolio.html:1914 (`lg-m` copy) — same geometry.
- evidence: `node run.js "#/ptgc" 390 844 "…click=button:has-text('Leagues'):visible…evalfile=modal.js"` → `smallTapEg: 86x29 "STARTING SUPPLY" | 86x29 "CIRCULATING SUPPLY"`; at 1380 → `134x28`. Screenshot `out/lg-390-modal.png`.
- why it matters: the toggle is the window's main control (the tier table flips with it); on a phone it is illegible and the
  tap area for "Circulating" is partly under the gold pill of "Starting".
- fix: `.lg-hd .tg{left:0;right:0;display:flex;justify-content:center;transform:none}` (the inline-grid then sizes to its
  content) — one CSS line in both files; or `width:max-content` on the toggle.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### V-6 · Socials Hub at 901–1150 px (small laptops, iPad landscape): the "Combined" column's header runs past the column — the title is cut to "Combine"
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P2
- area: index.html:1511–1525 (`.sh-cols` three columns down to 901 px; `.sh-hd .lg2{width:120px;…transform:scale(1.55)}`, `.sh-hd .nm{font-size:27px}` Orbitron 900, `.sh-hd .go{margin-left:auto;width:36px}`)
- problem: the Combined column's header is the 120-px double logo (`flex:none`) + "Combined" at 27 px Orbitron (~190 px) + the
  36-px chevron + gaps ≈ 400 px, but a column at 901–1150 px is 270–330 px wide and nothing in `.sh-hd` may shrink
  (`.nm` has no `min-width:0`, `white-space` effectively nowrap). The header overflows the column by 81 px at 901, 40 at 1024,
  15 at 1100; at 1024 `section.sh{overflow:hidden}` clips the chevron off the page (`span.go r=1038 in section.sh`), and the
  title is cut at the column border (`/home/claude/audit/work/V/out/sh-901-hd.png`, `out/soc-1024-hd.png`). Under 900 the
  grid is single-column and fine; from 1200 up it fits.
- evidence: `sh.js` across widths — 901: `Combined colRight=875 goRight=956 over=81`; 1024: `over=40`; 1100: `over=15`; 1200: `over=-18`.
- why it matters: 1024–1150 is the iPad-landscape / small-laptop band; the Combined column is the one that carries the
  two-token cards the owner promotes, and its name is the thing that is cut.
- fix: `.sh-hd .nm{font-size:clamp(18px,2.4vw,27px)}` and `.sh-hd>div{min-width:0}`; or drop to the single-column stack at
  `max-width:1150px` instead of 900 (the phone switcher idea in c64 would then also serve this band).
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: FIX NOW

#### V-7 · Affiliates at 640–880 px (portrait tablet): the Commission Log's Status (paid / unpaid) column is behind a sideways scroll with no cue
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:17432–17441 (`<div className="hidden sm:block overflow-x-auto"><table className="af-tbl w-full text-sm table-fixed min-w-[820px]">`), 17335 (Registry table, same pattern), design note 16023–16026
- problem: the note says the tables keep a min-width "so a tablet scrolls them sideways instead of overlapping" — deliberate.
  The consequence it does not mention: at 768 the content column is 718 px, so the last 102 px (exactly the 12 % `Status` column,
  plus half of `Commission`) are off-screen with no scrollbar on a touch tablet and no fade — unlike the Live Feed, which got a
  56-px right fade for the same situation at 641–1060 (index.html:5785, c30/M-8). `/home/claude/audit/work/V/out/aff-768-b.png`:
  "28.38M" is cut at the edge and nothing hints there is a Status column.
- evidence: `out/aff-768.log`: `hscroll = div.hidden.sm:block.overflow-x-auto sw=836 cw=718`; at 1024 cw ≥ 836 → no scroll.
- why it matters: Paid/Unpaid is the one column a referrer opens the log for; on an iPad they may conclude it is not shown.
- fix: copy the deck's cue: `@media(min-width:640px) and (max-width:900px){.af-tblwrap::after{…gradient fade…}}` on both
  wrappers, or make Status the 2nd column so it is never the one that falls off.
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### V-8 · Tap targets under 32 px that c28 did not cover, plus the ones it missed
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:10159 (tile badge buttons `tap-h` without `hit`), h2-header.jsx `h2-back` (22×22 on portfolio/calculators/charts), tools/pf-v2/PfRank.jsx:129 (`button.x` 22×28), 142 (period pills 36×25), the A/B wallet pills (30×26), portfolio.html `pf2-tg` 26×24 / `pf2-ed` 24×24; index.html:17046/17151 Affiliates Leaderboard + Receipts "×" 12×32; the window token/period pills 40–60×22 (Holders details, Volume, Humans vs Bots)
- problem: c28 (marked done) added `.hit` (h2.css:160, a 32×32 `::before`) to the header ⓘ and the Copy buttons — those now pass.
  Still measured under 32 px at 390: `14x32 "Volume by period"`, `27x32 "Humans vs Bots"`, `22x32 "Liquidity with RH Core"`,
  `14x32 "Holders by period"`, `27x32 "Holders details"` (tile badges, `tap-h` = height only, no `hit`); `22x22 button.h2-back
  "Back"` on the sibling pages; every window pill `…x22`; the new rank window's close `22x28`, wallet pills `30x26`, period pills
  `36x25`; the Affiliates windows' `×` `12x32`.
- evidence: `rwd.js`/`modal.js` smallTapEg in `out/ptgc-390.log`, `out/pf-390.log`, `out/pfrank-390.log`, `out/afflb-390.log`, `out/nh-390.log`, `out/vol-390.log`, `out/hvb-390.log`.
- why it matters: these are the controls that open the windows and close them; 22-px pills next to each other mis-tap.
- fix: add `hit` beside `tap-h` on the five tile badges (10159 and the Holders/Liquidity siblings); `.h2-back{min-width:32px;min-height:32px;display:inline-flex}`; `.pf2-rkw .x{width:32px;height:32px}`; the window pill rows `height:30px` → `32px`; the Affiliates `×` → the shared window close (L-14).
- effort: Small
- risk: Low
- recommendation: LATER

#### V-9 · Dashboard footer text touches both screen edges on phones
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:15715 (`<footer className="text-center py-4 border-t border-white/10 mt-8"><p className="text-white/30 text-xs">`)
- problem: no horizontal padding on the footer or the paragraph, and the footer sits outside the padded content column: the
  text spans x 1.9–388.1 at 390 (`foot.js` → `left:0,right:390,pad:"0px",parentPad:"16px 0px",textMaxRight:388.1,textMinLeft:1.9`).
  Every panel above has a 12-px gutter; the last line of the page is the only edge-to-edge text (`out/ptgc-390-part3.png` bottom).
- fix: `px-4` on the footer.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

#### V-10 · Windows sized in `vh` (not `dvh`) can lose their bottom edge under iOS Safari's toolbars
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:12367 + portfolio.html:1914 (Leagues `max-h-[92vh]`), 17524 (disclaimer `maxHeight:'90vh'`), 15848 / 16640 / 17151 (Affiliates windows `max-h-[90vh]`), 15435 / 15472 / 15508 (Logos `max-h-[80vh]`) — the newer windows use `h-[100dvh]` (11 places) and are fine
- problem: `vh` on iOS Safari is the LARGE viewport (toolbar collapsed). At first open on an iPhone 14 (small viewport ≈ 664 px,
  large ≈ 746) a 92vh panel is 686 px tall — 22 px of it, i.e. the Leagues "Close" button's lower half, sits under the bottom
  toolbar until the user scrolls the page (which a modal with `body{overflow:hidden}` will not let them do). The disclaimer's
  90vh = 671 px → 7 px under. Cannot be reproduced in the harness (fixed viewport); flagged from the units.
- fix: `max-h-[92dvh]` / `90dvh` / `80dvh` (same fallback behaviour on browsers without dvh if written as two declarations).
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

#### V-11 · Hover-only explainers: the "About the cost" ⓘ in Top 100 and the rank window, and 61 `title=` tooltips
- status: POTENTIAL RISK
- priority: P3
- area: index.html:12452 (`T1Cost`: `<span className="t1ii" tabIndex={0}>` with a `:hover/:focus` tip), portfolio.html:1010 (`.pf2-rkw .ii:hover .tip,.ii:focus .tip`), plus 61 `title="…"` attributes in index.html (e.g. the sea-creature counts, the Affiliates creature icon at 16028)
- problem: a `span[tabindex=0]` opens its tip on hover/focus; on iOS the first tap gives it `:hover` (works) but there is no
  close other than tapping elsewhere, and on Android Chrome a tap on a non-focusable-by-default element does not always
  focus it. `title=` never shows on touch. The dashboard's `InfoTip` (6264) is click-toggled and fine — the two newer windows
  (Top 100 2026-10-22, rank 2026-10-21) did not reuse it.
- fix: make `.t1ii` / `.ii` a `<button>` that toggles an `open` class (same as `InfoTip`); the `title=`s stay as a desktop bonus.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

#### V-12 · KPI tile at 1024–1050 px: the Volume tile's ⓘ sits on the "H" of VOLUME 24H
- status: CONFIRMED ISSUE
- priority: P3
- area: h2.css:215 (`.h2-tile .h2-tl{…right:calc(56*var(--u))…}`) and index.html:9773 (`hsd:Math.max(.47,…)` — the desktop floor)
- problem: at 1024 the desktop header sits at its .47 floor, the label reserves 56u = 26 px for the badge, but the Volume tile
  has two badges (ⓘ + bot, 27 px) — `tile.js`: `VOLUME 24H textRight=256 badgeLeft=255 overlap=0` (zero clearance; the glyph
  touches the letter, `out/ptgc-1024-vol.png`). At 1100 the gap is 4 px, at 1280 11 px. The phone side of the same rule is V-2 (4).
- fix: `.h2-tile .h2-tl{right:calc(96*var(--u))}` for tiles with `.h2-tb` holding two badges (add a `two` class), or `right:calc(56*var(--u)) + 8px`.
- effort: Small · risk: Low · recommendation: LATER

Windows (modal.js, 390×844 / 1380×900): every window's panel fits inside the viewport (`panelBottom ≤ vh`), scrolls on its
own y axis with `body{overflow:hidden}`, and nothing but the decorative glow divs (`pointer-events-none absolute -top-32`,
r=515) is past the right edge. Close buttons: 36×36 on the dashboard windows, 32×32 on the share card and Top 100 (390),
22×28 rank window, 12×32 Affiliates (V-8), none on Leagues (a full-width "Close" bar at the bottom, 44 px). Inputs inside
fixed windows: Top 100 (1), rank window (1), Leaderboard search (1) — on a phone the keyboard halves 100dvh; all three
panels scroll internally so the field can be brought up; not testable here.

### Where a dedicated phone layout would be a big win (vs the shrunk desktop)

- **Dashboard (#/ptgc, #/ufo)** — already has one (phone header, 2-col tiles, stacked panels) and it is good at 390. The one
  block that is still the 2025 desktop idea squeezed down is **LP Pairs** (c35): 26 stacked cards, 4 figures each, no actions,
  2,700 px. A phone list of one line per pair (logo · name · vol · 24 h badge, tap to expand to the glass squares + ⓘ/chart/trade)
  would cut it to ~1,000 px and give back the links. Biggest win on the site.
- **Socials Hub** — the 4,000-px stack is the desktop three-column grid turned vertical (c64). A token switcher (PTGC · UFO ·
  Combined chips, sticky under the tab band) + one column is the phone design; it also fixes V-6's band if the switcher kicks in
  at ≤1150.
- **Calculators** — the inputs are desktop rows forced into 390 (V-3, V-4; M-16). A phone version is "one thing per row":
  full-width amount box, mode pills under it, the two token result cards stacked. The 10 Yr Projection's year table (887 px wide
  in a 364-px box) wants a per-year card or a sticky first column — the only table on the site that a phone user must pan.
- **charts.html** — the 1M comparison chart is 270 px wide at 390 with a 10-label date axis that nearly touches
  (`Sep 22Sep 27Oct 2…`, `out/charts-390-b.png`) and the five summary cards stack to 750 px. Phone version: full-bleed chart
  (drop the card padding), `maxTicksLimit` 5 under 480 px, summary cards 2-up.
- **Live Feed** — has a real phone layout (two-line contacts, pods strip); the 641–1060 band is a cued sideways scroll (M-8).
  Fine as is.
- **Affiliates** — real phone layout (cards) since 09-29; only the tablet band (V-7) is the shrunk desktop.
- **Portfolio** — the best phone layout on the site (round 39 squeeze probe is clean at 390, the 320 spill is 6–26 px). The only
  phone-specific thing missing is a "jump to" for the 8,700-px page (Holdings · PTGC · UFO · History · Transactions · Wallets)
  — the sticky band already exists to hang it on.
- **Windows** (Top 100, Holders, Volume, RH, DAO Buys, HvB, rank, Leagues) — all already 100dvh phone sheets with their own
  scroll; good. The share cards are the known 358-px screenshots (M-15).
- **Home** — fine at 390; 320 needs the two tweaks in V-2(5).
- **Disclaimer** — not a layout question, a CTA question (V-1).

### At 1920 (looked at every route)
- Nothing over-stretched: the dashboard panels cap at ~1,240 px, LP table at 1,240, sibling pages at ~1,000–1,100, Home cards
  at 2 × 640, Socials at 3 × 380, Affiliates at 1,200, Portfolio at 1,240, Ledger at 900. The KPI tile row is the one element
  that spans the full 1,920 (seven tiles, 2166-px reference design) while everything under it is 1,240 wide — by design, reads fine.
- The 1920 full-page shots show a black band where the sticky tab band lives (`div.h2.h2-tabs sticky`) — that is the
  full-page capture of a sticky element, not a gap on the real page (verified with viewport shots at scroll 0).
- `TEDDY BEAR ㉾/pTGC` is ellipsised in the LP table at every width ≥ 768 (`div.p.text-[#F0DC78] by=3–12`) — the pair-name
  column is 2 of 12 grid units; a `title=` with the full name would do (index.html:10514 `<div className={`p ${theme.text}`}>{name}</div>` has no `title`).

### Checked and found OK
- **No horizontal page scroll at 390 on any route or tab** (Home, #/ptgc, #/ufo, Socials, KPI, Live Feed, Affiliates, charts,
  calculators × 5 tabs, ledger, portfolio with both folds and both ledgers open) — every `scrollW=390`. M-1 (the glow
  pseudo-element at 401 px) is fixed.
- **The sticky tab band** is 75 px at 390 (107 with the portfolio mini header), top=0, never covers a panel title after a
  tab change; the active tab is in view (`out/pf-390top-s1500.png`).
- **Portfolio v2 at 390**: `pf2-squeeze.js` → `spill:[] clip:[] hit:[]` with 2 wallets, PF_STAKE/HIST/TX/V1/ED, both folds open;
  wallet addresses are `0x1111…1111` + copy, never wrap; big figures ($9,449,626.67, 33.33B, 18.40B) fit their tiles; the
  rank window opens from the per-wallet badge (`button.pf2-rkb.btn`) and fits 100dvh.
- **Sub-micro prices** (`DS_PRICE=4.542e-5`): `$0.0₄4542` fits the phone header, the Home cards, the price strip and the
  portfolio's `$0.0₃1420` at 390 and 320 (`out/sub-320-top.png`, `out/subhome-320-top.png`).
- **Top 100 window at 390**: the checker takes a typed address (`T1_BAL=5000000` → "HOLDS 5.00M PTGC · RANK #2,587 Squid ·
  TO BREAK IN +407.49M · COST TODAY $57,862 · 1.2 % of the way"), the podium is 3-up, the table under it is 2 columns + balance
  — nothing past the edge (`out/t1chk-390-chk.png`).
- **Live Feed at 390**: dedicated layout (pods 5-across strip, two-line contacts with the 5-way split under each) —
  `out/feed-390-vp.png`; at 768/1024 the tape scrolls sideways under the fade cue (M-8).
- **Affiliates at 390**: cards for Recent Activity / Registry / Commission Log, podium 3-up, stats 2-col; nothing clipped
  (`out/aff-390-a.png`, `-b.png`).
- **Every window at 390** is a 100dvh sheet with internal scroll and a 36×36 close (Holders, Volume, RH, DAO Buys, HvB,
  Top 100, swap); the DAO Buys chart redraws at 358 px with its dots, the monthly bar strip and the four stat tiles.
- **switch.win SwapModal** at 390: panel 390 wide, 41–844 (fits), iframe full width, "Trouble connecting? Open in Switch" link visible.
- **768 portrait**: the phone header scales up (`hsp` 1.3 cap), 2-col tiles, stacked panels; Burn / Value Generated / Token
  Allocation / DAO Treasury / LP Pairs all fit (`out/ptgc-768-a.png`, `-b.png`).
- **1024**: full desktop layout at the `.47` floor; everything fits except the 1-px ⓘ touch (V-12).
- **Disclaimer / modal text clipping**: none — the only `text-overflow:ellipsis` hits with real data are the Affiliates
  display names (`@ptgcofficialonboard`, by design) and the pair name above.
- **Console**: zero page errors on every run of index.html, portfolio, calculators, ledger at all six widths.

### Ideas (grounded in what exists)
- The `hit` helper (h2.css:160) is the right tool for every remaining small target in V-8 — one class, no layout change.
- `h2Scale` (index.html:9773) already has both floors in one place; lowering `hsp`'s floor to .78 and adding the 56u→96u badge
  reserve (V-12) fixes both ends of the tile problem with two numbers.
- The deck's right-edge fade (5785) is a ready-made cue for the two Affiliates tables (V-7) and the 10 Yr Projection table.
- The disclaimer's sticky-footer split (V-1) is the same shape as the Leagues window (scrolling body, fixed "Close" bar at
  12396) — reuse that structure.
- The Portfolio's sticky band (`.h2-tabs` with the mini PTGC/UFO header) could carry a "Holdings · PTGC · UFO · History ·
  Transactions · Wallets" jump row under 640 px — the section anchors (`.pf2-vh`, `.pf2-rt`, `.pf2-fold`) already exist.
